LUK 3:17 11 And he answered and said unto them, He that hath two coats, let him impart to him that hath none; and he that hath food, let him do likewise.
LUK 3:18 12 And there came also publicans to be baptized, and they said unto him, Teacher, what must we do?
LUK 3:19 13 And he said unto them, Extort no more than that which is appointed you.
LUK 3:20 14 And soldiers also asked him, saying, And we, what must we do? And he said unto them, Extort from no man by violence, neither accuse any one wrongfully; and be content with your wages.
LUK 3:21 15 And as the people were in expectation, and all men reasoned in their hearts concerning John, whether haply he were the Christ;
LUK 3:22 16 John answered, saying unto them all, I indeed baptize you with water; but there cometh he that is mightier than I, the latchet of whose shoes I am not worthy to unloose: he shall baptize you in the Holy Spirit and in fire:
LUK 3:23 17 whose fan is in his hand, thoroughly to cleanse his threshing-floor, and to gather the wheat into his garner; but the chaff he will burn up with unquenchable fire.
LUK 3:24 18 With many other exhortations therefore preached he good tidings unto the people;
LUK 3:25 19 but Herod the tetrarch, being reproved by him for Herodias his brother’s wife, and for all the evil things which Herod had done,
LUK 3:26 20 added this also to them all, that he shut up John in prison.
LUK 3:27 21 Now it came to pass, when all the people were baptized, that, Jesus also having been baptized, and praying, the heaven was opened,
LUK 3:28 22 and the Holy Spirit descended in a bodily form, as a dove, upon him, and a voice came out of heaven, Thou art my beloved Son; in thee I am well pleased.
LUK 3:29 23 And Jesus himself, when he began to teach , was about thirty years of age, being the son (as was supposed) of Joseph, the son of Heli,
LUK 3:30 24 the son of Matthat, the son of Levi, the son of Melchi, the son of Jannai, the son of Joseph,
LUK 3:31 25 the son of Mattathias, the son of Amos, the son of Nahum, the son of Esli, the son of Naggai,
LUK 3:32 26 the son of Maath, the son of Mattathias, the son of Semein, the son of Josech, the son of Joda,
LUK 3:33 27 the son of Joanan, the son of Rhesa, the son of Zerubbabel, the son of Shealtiel, the son of Neri,
LUK 3:34 28 the son of Melchi, the son of Addi, the son of Cosam, the son of Elmadam, the son of Er,
LUK 3:35 29 the son of Jesus, the son of Eliezer, the son of Jorim, the son of Matthat, the son of Levi,
LUK 3:36 30 the son of Symeon, the son of Judas, the son of Joseph, the son of Jonam, the son of Eliakim,
LUK 3:37 31 the son of Melea, the son of Menna, the son of Mattatha, the son of Nathan, the son of David,
LUK 3:38 32 the son of Jesse, the son of Obed, the son of Boaz, the son of Salmon, the son of Nahshon,
LUK 3:39 33 the son of Amminadab, the son of Arni, the son of Hezron, the son of Perez, the son of Judah,
LUK 3:40 34 the son of Jacob, the son of Isaac, the son of Abraham, the son of Terah, the son of Nahor,
LUK 3:41 35 the son of Serug, the son of Reu, the son of Peleg, the son of Eber, the son of Shelah,
LUK 3:42 36 the son of Cainan, the son of Arphaxad, the son of Shem, the son of Noah, the son of Lamech,
LUK 3:43 37 the son of Methuselah, the son of Enoch, the son of Jared, the son of Mahalaleel, the son of Cainan,
LUK 3:44 38 the son of Enos, the son of Seth, the son of Adam, the son of God.
LUK 4:1 1 And Jesus, full of the Holy Spirit, returned from the Jordan, and was led in the Spirit in the wilderness
LUK 4:2 2 during forty days, being tempted of the devil. And he did eat nothing in those days: and when they were completed, he hungered.
LUK 4:3 3 And the devil said unto him, If thou art the Son of God, command this stone that it become bread.
LUK 4:4 4 And Jesus answered unto him, It is written, Man shall not live by bread alone.
LUK 4:5 5 And he led him up, and showed him all the kingdoms of the world in a moment of time.
LUK 4:6 6 And the devil said unto him, To thee will I give all this authority, and the glory of them: for it hath been delivered unto me; and to whomsoever I will I give it.
LUK 4:7 7 If thou therefore wilt worship before me, it shall all be thine.
LUK 4:8 8 And Jesus answered and said unto him, It is written, Thou shalt worship the Lord thy God, and him only shalt thou serve.
LUK 4:9 9 And he led him to Jerusalem, and set him on the pinnacle of the temple, and said unto him, If thou art the Son of God, cast thyself down from hence:
LUK 4:10 10 for it is written,
LUK 4:11 He shall give his angels charge concerning thee, to guard thee:
LUK 4:12 11 and,
LUK 4:13 On their hands they shall bear thee up,
LUK 4:14 Lest haply thou dash thy foot against a stone.
LUK 4:15 12 And Jesus answering said unto him, It is said, Thou shalt not make trial of the Lord thy God.
LUK 4:16 13 And when the devil had completed every temptation, he departed from him for a season.
LUK 4:17 14 And Jesus returned in the power of the Spirit into Galilee: and a fame went out concerning him through all the region round about.
LUK 4:18 15 And he taught in their synagogues, being glorified of all.
LUK 4:19 16 And he came to Nazareth, where he had been brought up: and he entered, as his custom was, into the synagogue on the sabbath day, and stood up to read.
LUK 4:20 17 And there was delivered unto him the book of the prophet Isaiah. And he opened the book, and found the place where it was written,
LUK 4:21 18 The Spirit of the Lord is upon me,
LUK 4:22 Because he anointed me to preach good tidings to the poor:
LUK 4:23 He hath sent me to proclaim release to the captives,
LUK 4:24 And recovering of sight to the blind,
LUK 4:25 To set at liberty them that are bruised,
LUK 4:26 19 To proclaim the acceptable year of the Lord.
LUK 4:27 20 And he closed the book, and gave it back to the attendant, and sat down: and the eyes of all in the synagogue were fastened on him.
LUK 4:28 21 And he began to say unto them, To-day hath this scripture been fulfilled in your ears.
LUK 4:29 22 And all bare him witness, and wondered at the words of grace which proceeded out of his mouth: and they said, Is not this Joseph’s son?
LUK 4:30 23 And he said unto them, Doubtless ye will say unto me this parable, Physician, heal thyself: whatsoever we have heard done at Capernaum, do also here in thine own country.
LUK 4:31 24 And he said, Verily I say unto you, No prophet is acceptable in his own country.
LUK 4:32 25 But of a truth I say unto you, There were many widows in Israel in the days of Elijah, when the heaven was shut up three years and six months, when there came a great famine over all the land;
LUK 4:33 26 and unto none of them was Elijah sent, but only to Zarephath, in the land of Sidon, unto a woman that was a widow.
LUK 4:34 27 And there were many lepers in Israel in the time of Elisha the prophet; and none of them was cleansed, but only Naaman the Syrian.
LUK 4:35 28 And they were all filled with wrath in the synagogue, as they heard these things;
LUK 4:36 29 and they rose up, and cast him forth out of the city, and led him unto the brow of the hill whereon their city was built, that they might throw him down headlong.
LUK 4:37 30 But he passing through the midst of them went his way.
LUK 4:38 31 And he came down to Capernaum, a city of Galilee. And he was teaching them on the sabbath day:
LUK 4:39 32 and they were astonished at his teaching; for his word was with authority.
LUK 4:40 33 And in the synagogue there was a man, that had a spirit of an unclean demon; and he cried out with a loud voice,
LUK 4:41 34 Ah! what have we to do with thee, Jesus thou Nazarene? art thou come to destroy us? I know thee who thou art, the Holy One of God.
LUK 4:42 35 And Jesus rebuked him, saying, Hold thy peace, and come out of him. And when the demon had thrown him down in the midst, he came out of him, having done him no hurt.
LUK 4:43 36 And amazement came upon all, and they spake together, one with another, saying, What is this word? for with authority and power he commandeth the unclean spirits, and they come out.
LUK 4:44 37 And there went forth a rumor concerning him into every place of the region round about.
LUK 4:45 38 And he rose up from the synagogue, and entered into the house of Simon. And Simon’s wife’s mother was holden with a great fever; and they besought him for her.
LUK 4:46 39 And he stood over her, and rebuked the fever; and it left her: and immediately she rose up and ministered unto them.
LUK 4:47 40 And when the sun was setting, all they that had any sick with divers diseases brought them unto him; and he laid his hands on every one of them, and healed them.
LUK 4:48 41 And demons also came out from many, crying out, and saying, Thou art the Son of God. And rebuking them, he suffered them not to speak, because they knew that he was the Christ.
LUK 4:49 42 And when it was day, he came out and went into a desert place: and the multitudes sought after him, and came unto him, and would have stayed him, that he should not go from them.
LUK 4:50 43 But he said unto them, I must preach the good tidings of the kingdom of God to the other cities also: for therefore was I sent.
LUK 4:51 44 And he was preaching in the synagogues of Galilee.
LUK 5:1 1 Now it came to pass, while the multitude pressed upon him and heard the word of God, that he was standing by the lake of Gennesaret;
LUK 5:2 2 and he saw two boats standing by the lake: but the fishermen had gone out of them, and were washing their nets.
LUK 5:3 3 And he entered into one of the boats, which was Simon’s, and asked him to put out a little from the land. And he sat down and taught the multitudes out of the boat.
LUK 5:4 4 And when he had left speaking, he said unto Simon, Put out into the deep, and let down your nets for a draught.
LUK 5:5 5 And Simon answered and said, Master, we toiled all night, and took nothing: but at thy word I will let down the nets.
LUK 5:6 6 And when they had done this, they inclosed a great multitude of fishes; and their nets were breaking;
LUK 5:7 7 and they beckoned unto their partners in the other boat, that they should come and help them. And they came, and filled both the boats, so that they began to sink.
LUK 5:8 8 But Simon Peter, when he saw it, fell down at Jesus’ knees, saying, Depart from me; for I am a sinful man, O Lord.
LUK 5:9 9 For he was amazed, and all that were with him, at the draught of the fishes which they had taken;
LUK 5:10 10 and so were also James and John, sons of Zebedee, who were partners with Simon. And Jesus said unto Simon, Fear not; from henceforth thou shalt catch men.
LUK 5:11 11 And when they had brought their boats to land, they left all, and followed him.
LUK 5:12 12 And it came to pass, while he was in one of the cities, behold, a man full of leprosy: and when he saw Jesus, he fell on his face, and besought him, saying, Lord, if thou wilt, thou canst make me clean.
LUK 5:13 13 And he stretched forth his hand, and touched him, saying, I will; be thou made clean. And straightway the leprosy departed from him.
LUK 5:14 14 And he charged him to tell no man: but go thy way, and show thyself to the priest, and offer for thy cleansing, according as Moses commanded, for a testimony unto them.
LUK 5:15 15 But so much the more went abroad the report concerning him: and great multitudes came together to hear, and to be healed of their infirmities.
LUK 5:16 16 But he withdrew himself in the deserts, and prayed.
LUK 5:17 17 And it came to pass on one of those days, that he was teaching; and there were Pharisees and doctors of the law sitting by, who were come out of every village of Galilee and Judæa and Jerusalem: and the power of the Lord was with him to heal.
LUK 5:18 18 And behold, men bring on a bed a man that was palsied: and they sought to bring him in, and to lay him before him.
LUK 5:19 19 And not finding by what way they might bring him in because of the multitude, they went up to the housetop, and let him down through the tiles with his couch into the midst before Jesus.
LUK 5:20 20 And seeing their faith, he said, Man, thy sins are forgiven thee.
LUK 5:21 21 And the scribes and the Pharisees began to reason, saying, Who is this that speaketh blasphemies? Who can forgive sins, but God alone?
LUK 5:22 22 But Jesus perceiving their reasonings, answered and said unto them, Why reason ye in your hearts?
LUK 5:23 23 Which is easier, to say, Thy sins are forgiven thee; or to say, Arise and walk?
LUK 5:24 24 But that ye may know that the Son of man hath authority on earth to forgive sins (he said unto him that was palsied), I say unto thee, Arise, and take up thy couch, and go unto thy house.
LUK 5:25 25 And immediately he rose up before them, and took up that whereon he lay, and departed to his house, glorifying God.
LUK 5:26 26 And amazement took hold on all, and they glorified God; and they were filled with fear, saying, We have seen strange things to-day.
LUK 5:27 27 And after these things he went forth, and beheld a publican, named Levi, sitting at the place of toll, and said unto him, Follow me.
LUK 5:28 28 And he forsook all, and rose up and followed him.
LUK 5:29 29 And Levi made him a great feast in his house: and there was a great multitude of publicans and of others that were sitting at meat with them.
LUK 5:30 30 And the Pharisees and their scribes murmured against his disciples, saying, Why do ye eat and drink with the publicans and sinners?
LUK 5:31 31 And Jesus answering said unto them, They that are in health have no need of a physician; but they that are sick.
LUK 5:32 32 I am not come to call the righteous but sinners to repentance.
LUK 5:33 33 And they said unto him, The disciples of John fast often, and make supplications; likewise also the disciples of the Pharisees; but thine eat and drink.
LUK 5:34 34 And Jesus said unto them, Can ye make the sons of the bride-chamber fast, while the bridegroom is with them?
LUK 5:35 35 But the days will come; and when the bridegroom shall be taken away from them, then will they fast in those days.
LUK 5:36 36 And he spake also a parable unto them: No man rendeth a piece from a new garment and putteth it upon an old garment; else he will rend the new, and also the piece from the new will not agree with the old.
LUK 5:37 37 And no man putteth new wine into old wine-skins; else the new wine will burst the skins, and itself will be spilled, and the skins will perish.
LUK 5:38 38 But new wine must be put into fresh wine-skins.
LUK 5:39 39 And no man having drunk old wine desireth new; for he saith, The old is good.
LUK 6:1 1 Now it came to pass on a sabbath, that he was going through the grainfields; and his disciples plucked the ears, and did eat, rubbing them in their hands.
LUK 6:2 2 But certain of the Pharisees said, Why do ye that which it is not lawful to do on the sabbath day?
LUK 6:3 3 And Jesus answering them said, Have ye not read even this, what David did, when he was hungry, he, and they that were with him;
LUK 6:4 4 how he entered into the house of God, and took and ate the showbread, and gave also to them that were with him; which it is not lawful to eat save for the priests alone?
LUK 6:5 5 And he said unto them, The Son of man is lord of the sabbath.
LUK 6:6 6 And it came to pass on another sabbath, that he entered into the synagogue and taught: and there was a man there, and his right hand was withered.
LUK 6:7 7 And the scribes and the Pharisees watched him, whether he would heal on the sabbath; that they might find how to accuse him.
LUK 6:8 8 But he knew their thoughts; and he said to the man that had his hand withered, Rise up, and stand forth in the midst. And he arose and stood forth.
LUK 6:9 9 And Jesus said unto them, I ask you, Is it lawful on the sabbath to do good, or to do harm? to save a life, or to destroy it?
LUK 6:10 10 And he looked round about on them all, and said unto him, Stretch forth thy hand. And he did so : and his hand was restored.
LUK 6:11 11 But they were filled with madness; and communed one with another what they might do to Jesus.
LUK 6:12 12 And it came to pass in these days, that he went out into the mountain to pray; and he continued all night in prayer to God.
LUK 6:13 13 And when it was day, he called his disciples; and he chose from them twelve, whom also he named apostles:
LUK 6:14 14 Simon, whom he also named Peter, and Andrew his brother, and James and John, and Philip and Bartholomew,
LUK 6:15 15 and Matthew and Thomas, and James the son of Alphæus, and Simon who was called the Zealot,
LUK 6:16 16 and Judas the son of James, and Judas Iscariot, who became a traitor;
LUK 6:17 17 and he came down with them, and stood on a level place, and a great multitude of his disciples, and a great number of the people from all Judæa and Jerusalem, and the sea coast of Tyre and Sidon, who came to hear him, and to be healed of their diseases;
LUK 6:18 18 and they that were troubled with unclean spirits were healed.
LUK 6:19 19 And all the multitude sought to touch him; for power came forth from him, and healed them all.
LUK 6:20 20 And he lifted up his eyes on his disciples, and said, Blessed are ye poor: for yours is the kingdom of God.
LUK 6:21 21 Blessed are ye that hunger now: for ye shall be filled. Blessed are ye that weep now: for ye shall laugh.
LUK 6:22 22 Blessed are ye, when men shall hate you, and when they shall separate you from their company , and reproach you, and cast out your name as evil, for the Son of man’s sake.
LUK 6:23 23 Rejoice in that day, and leap for joy : for behold, your reward is great in heaven; for in the same manner did their fathers unto the prophets.
LUK 6:24 24 But woe unto you that are rich! for ye have received your consolation.
LUK 6:25 25 Woe unto you, ye that are full now! for ye shall hunger. Woe unto you , ye that laugh now! for ye shall mourn and weep.
LUK 6:26 26 Woe unto you , when all men shall speak well of you! for in the same manner did their fathers to the false prophets.
LUK 6:27 27 But I say unto you that hear, Love your enemies, do good to them that hate you,
LUK 6:28 28 bless them that curse you, pray for them that despitefully use you.
LUK 6:29 29 To him that smiteth thee on the one cheek offer also the other; and from him that taketh away thy cloak withhold not thy coat also.
LUK 6:30 30 Give to every one that asketh thee; and of him that taketh away thy goods ask them not again.
LUK 6:31 31 And as ye would that men should do to you, do ye also to them likewise.
LUK 6:32 32 And if ye love them that love you, what thank have ye? for even sinners love those that love them.
LUK 6:33 33 And if ye do good to them that do good to you, what thank have ye? for even sinners do the same.
LUK 6:34 34 And if ye lend to them of whom ye hope to receive, what thank have ye? even sinners lend to sinners, to receive again as much.
LUK 6:35 35 But love your enemies, and do them good, and lend, never despairing; and your reward shall be great, and ye shall be sons of the Most High: for he is kind toward the unthankful and evil.
LUK 6:36 36 Be ye merciful, even as your Father is merciful.
LUK 6:37 37 And judge not, and ye shall not be judged: and condemn not, and ye shall not be condemned: release, and ye shall be released:
LUK 6:38 38 give, and it shall be given unto you; good measure, pressed down, shaken together, running over, shall they give into your bosom. For with what measure ye mete it shall be measured to you again.
LUK 6:39 39 And he spake also a parable unto them, Can the blind guide the blind? shall they not both fall into a pit?
LUK 6:40 40 The disciple is not above his teacher: but every one when he is perfected shall be as his teacher.
LUK 6:41 41 And why beholdest thou the mote that is in thy brother’s eye, but considerest not the beam that is in thine own eye?
LUK 6:42 42 Or how canst thou say to thy brother, Brother, let me cast out the mote that is in thine eye, when thou thyself beholdest not the beam that is in thine own eye? Thou hypocrite, cast out first the beam out of thine own eye, and then shalt thou see clearly to cast out the mote that is in thy brother’s eye.
LUK 6:43 43 For there is no good tree that bringeth forth corrupt fruit; nor again a corrupt tree that bringeth forth good fruit.
LUK 6:44 44 For each tree is known by its own fruit. For of thorns men do not gather figs, nor of a bramble bush gather they grapes.
LUK 6:45 45 The good man out of the good treasure of his heart bringeth forth that which is good; and the evil man out of the evil treasure bringeth forth that which is evil: for out of the abundance of the heart his mouth speaketh.
LUK 6:46 46 And why call ye me, Lord, Lord, and do not the things which I say?
LUK 6:47 47 Every one that cometh unto me, and heareth my words, and doeth them, I will show you to whom he is like:
LUK 6:48 48 he is like a man building a house, who digged and went deep, and laid a foundation upon the rock: and when a flood arose, the stream brake against that house, and could not shake it: because it had been well builded.
LUK 6:49 49 But he that heareth, and doeth not, is like a man that built a house upon the earth without a foundation; against which the stream brake, and straightway it fell in; and the ruin of that house was great.
LUK 7:1 1 After he had ended all his sayings in the ears of the people, he entered into Capernaum.
LUK 7:2 2 And a certain centurion’s servant, who was dear unto him, was sick and at the point of death.
LUK 7:3 3 And when he heard concerning Jesus, he sent unto him elders of the Jews, asking him that he would come and save his servant.
LUK 7:4 4 And they, when they came to Jesus, besought him earnestly, saying, He is worthy that thou shouldest do this for him;
LUK 7:5 5 for he loveth our nation, and himself built us our synagogue.
LUK 7:6 6 And Jesus went with them. And when he was now not far from the house, the centurion sent friends to him, saying unto him, Lord, trouble not thyself; for I am not worthy that thou shouldest come under my roof:
LUK 7:7 7 wherefore neither thought I myself worthy to come unto thee: but say the word, and my servant shall be healed.
LUK 7:8 8 For I also am a man set under authority, having under myself soldiers: and I say to this one, Go, and he goeth; and to another, Come, and he cometh; and to my servant, Do this, and he doeth it.
LUK 7:9 9 And when Jesus heard these things, he marvelled at him, and turned and said unto the multitude that followed him, I say unto you, I have not found so great faith, no, not in Israel.
LUK 7:10 10 And they that were sent, returning to the house, found the servant whole.
LUK 7:11 11 And it came to pass soon afterwards, that he went to a city called Nain; and his disciples went with him, and a great multitude.
LUK 7:12 12 Now when he drew near to the gate of the city, behold, there was carried out one that was dead, the only son of his mother, and she was a widow: and much people of the city was with her.
LUK 7:13 13 And when the Lord saw her, he had compassion on her, and said unto her, Weep not.
LUK 7:14 14 And he came nigh and touched the bier: and the bearers stood still. And he said, Young man, I say unto thee, Arise.
LUK 7:15 15 And he that was dead sat up, and began to speak. And he gave him to his mother.
LUK 7:16 16 And fear took hold on all: and they glorified God, saying, A great prophet is arisen among us: and, God hath visited his people.
LUK 7:17 17 And this report went forth concerning him in the whole of Judæa, and all the region round about.
LUK 7:18 18 And the disciples of John told him of all these things.
LUK 7:19 19 And John calling unto him two of his disciples sent them to the Lord, saying, Art thou he that cometh, or look we for another?
LUK 7:20 20 And when the men were come unto him, they said, John the Baptist hath sent us unto thee, saying, Art thou he that cometh, or look we for another?
LUK 7:21 21 In that hour he cured many of diseases and plagues and evil spirits; and on many that were blind he bestowed sight.
LUK 7:22 22 And he answered and said unto them, Go and tell John the things which ye have seen and heard; the blind receive their sight, the lame walk, the lepers are cleansed, and the deaf hear, the dead are raised up, the poor have good tidings preached to them.
LUK 7:23 23 And blessed is he, whosoever shall find no occasion of stumbling in me.
LUK 7:24 24 And when the messengers of John were departed, he began to say unto the multitudes concerning John, What went ye out into the wilderness to behold? a reed shaken with the wind?
LUK 7:25 25 But what went ye out to see? a man clothed in soft raiment? Behold, they that are gorgeously apparelled, and live delicately, are in kings’ courts.
LUK 7:26 26 But what went ye out to see? a prophet? Yea, I say unto you, and much more than a prophet.
LUK 7:27 27 This is he of whom it is written,
LUK 7:28 Behold, I send my messenger before thy face,
LUK 7:29 Who shall prepare thy way before thee.
LUK 7:30 28 I say unto you, Among them that are born of women there is none greater than John: yet he that is but little in the kingdom of God is greater than he.
LUK 7:31 29 And all the people when they heard, and the publicans, justified God, being baptized with the baptism of John.
LUK 7:32 30 But the Pharisees and the lawyers rejected for themselves the counsel of God, being not baptized of him.
LUK 7:33 31 Whereunto then shall I liken the men of this generation, and to what are they like?
LUK 7:34 32 They are like unto children that sit in the marketplace, and call one to another; who say, We piped unto you, and ye did not dance; we wailed, and ye did not weep.
LUK 7:35 33 For John the Baptist is come eating no bread nor drinking wine; and ye say, He hath a demon.
LUK 7:36 34 The Son of man is come eating and drinking; and ye say, Behold, a gluttonous man, and a winebibber, a friend of publicans and sinners!
LUK 7:37 35 And wisdom is justified of all her children.
LUK 7:38 36 And one of the Pharisees desired him that he would eat with him. And he entered into the Pharisee’s house, and sat down to meat.
LUK 7:39 37 And behold, a woman who was in the city, a sinner; and when she knew that he was sitting at meat in the Pharisee’s house, she brought an alabaster cruse of ointment,
LUK 7:40 38 and standing behind at his feet, weeping, she began to wet his feet with her tears, and wiped them with the hair of her head, and kissed his feet, and anointed them with the ointment.
LUK 7:41 39 Now when the Pharisee that had bidden him saw it, he spake within himself, saying, This man, if he were a prophet, would have perceived who and what manner of woman this is that toucheth him, that she is a sinner.
LUK 7:42 40 And Jesus answering said unto him, Simon, I have somewhat to say unto thee. And he saith, Teacher, say on.
LUK 7:43 41 A certain lender had two debtors: the one owed five hundred shillings, and the other fifty.
LUK 7:44 42 When they had not wherewith to pay, he forgave them both. Which of them therefore will love him most?
LUK 7:45 43 Simon answered and said, He, I suppose, to whom he forgave the most. And he said unto him, Thou hast rightly judged.
LUK 7:46 44 And turning to the woman, he said unto Simon, Seest thou this woman? I entered into thy house, thou gavest me no water for my feet: but she hath wetted my feet with her tears, and wiped them with her hair.
LUK 7:47 45 Thou gavest me no kiss: but she, since the time I came in, hath not ceased to kiss my feet.
LUK 7:48 46 My head with oil thou didst not anoint: but she hath anointed my feet with ointment.
LUK 7:49 47 Wherefore I say unto thee, Her sins, which are many, are forgiven; for she loved much: but to whom little is forgiven, the same loveth little.
LUK 7:50 48 And he said unto her, Thy sins are forgiven.
LUK 7:51 49 And they that sat at meat with him began to say within themselves, Who is this that even forgiveth sins?
LUK 7:52 50 And he said unto the woman, Thy faith hath saved thee; go in peace.
LUK 8:1 1 And it came to pass soon afterwards, that he went about through cities and villages, preaching and bringing the good tidings of the kingdom of God, and with him the twelve,
LUK 8:2 2 and certain women who had been healed of evil spirits and infirmities: Mary that was called Magdalene, from whom seven demons had gone out,
LUK 8:3 3 and Joanna the wife of Chuzas Herod’s steward, and Susanna, and many others, who ministered unto them of their substance.
LUK 8:4 4 And when a great multitude came together, and they of every city resorted unto him, he spake by a parable:
LUK 8:5 5 The sower went forth to sow his seed: and as he sowed, some fell by the way side; and it was trodden under foot, and the birds of the heaven devoured it.
LUK 8:6 6 And other fell on the rock; and as soon as it grew, it withered away, because it had no moisture.
LUK 8:7 7 And other fell amidst the thorns; and the thorns grew with it, and choked it.
LUK 8:8 8 And other fell into the good ground, and grew, and brought forth fruit a hundredfold. As he said these things, he cried, He that hath ears to hear, let him hear.
LUK 8:9 9 And his disciples asked him what this parable might be.
LUK 8:10 10 And he said, Unto you it is given to know the mysteries of the kingdom of God: but to the rest in parables; that seeing they may not see, and hearing they may not understand.
LUK 8:11 11 Now the parable is this: The seed is the word of God.
LUK 8:12 12 And those by the way side are they that have heard; then cometh the devil, and taketh away the word from their heart, that they may not believe and be saved.
LUK 8:13 13 And those on the rock are they who, when they have heard, receive the word with joy; and these have no root, who for a while believe, and in time of temptation fall away.
LUK 8:14 14 And that which fell among the thorns, these are they that have heard, and as they go on their way they are choked with cares and riches and pleasures of this life, and bring no fruit to perfection.
LUK 8:15 15 And that in the good ground, these are such as in an honest and good heart, having heard the word, hold it fast, and bring forth fruit with patience.
LUK 8:16 16 And no man, when he hath lighted a lamp, covereth it with a vessel, or putteth it under a bed; but putteth it on a stand, that they that enter in may see the light.
LUK 8:17 17 For nothing is hid, that shall not be made manifest; nor anything secret, that shall not be known and come to light.
LUK 8:18 18 Take heed therefore how ye hear: for whosoever hath, to him shall be given; and whosoever hath not, from him shall be taken away even that which he thinketh he hath.
LUK 8:19 19 And there came to him his mother and brethren, and they could not come at him for the crowd.
LUK 8:20 20 And it was told him, Thy mother and thy brethren stand without, desiring to see thee.
LUK 8:21 21 But he answered and said unto them, My mother and my brethren are these that hear the word of God, and do it.
LUK 8:22 22 Now it came to pass on one of those days, that he entered into a boat, himself and his disciples; and he said unto them, Let us go over unto the other side of the lake: and they launched forth.
LUK 8:23 23 But as they sailed he fell asleep: and there came down a storm of wind on the lake; and they were filling with water , and were in jeopardy.
LUK 8:24 24 And they came to him, and awoke him, saying, Master, master, we perish. And he awoke, and rebuked the wind and the raging of the water: and they ceased, and there was a calm.
LUK 8:25 25 And he said unto them, Where is your faith? And being afraid they marvelled, saying one to another, Who then is this, that he commandeth even the winds and the water, and they obey him?
LUK 8:26 26 And they arrived at the country of the Gerasenes, which is over against Galilee.
LUK 8:27 27 And when he was come forth upon the land, there met him a certain man out of the city, who had demons; and for a long time he had worn no clothes, and abode not in any house, but in the tombs.
LUK 8:28 28 And when he saw Jesus, he cried out, and fell down before him, and with a loud voice said, What have I to do with thee, Jesus, thou Son of the Most High God? I beseech thee, torment me not.
LUK 8:29 29 For he was commanding the unclean spirit to come out from the man. For oftentimes it had seized him: and he was kept under guard, and bound with chains and fetters; and breaking the bands asunder, he was driven of the demon into the deserts.
LUK 8:30 30 And Jesus asked him, What is thy name? And he said, Legion; for many demons were entered into him.
LUK 8:31 31 And they entreated him that he would not command them to depart into the abyss.
LUK 8:32 32 Now there was there a herd of many swine feeding on the mountain: and they entreated him that he would give them leave to enter into them. And he gave them leave.
LUK 8:33 33 And the demons came out from the man, and entered into the swine: and the herd rushed down the steep into the lake, and were drowned.
LUK 8:34 34 And when they that fed them saw what had come to pass, they fled, and told it in the city and in the country.
LUK 8:35 35 And they went out to see what had come to pass; and they came to Jesus, and found the man, from whom the demons were gone out, sitting, clothed and in his right mind, at the feet of Jesus: and they were afraid.
LUK 8:36 36 And they that saw it told them how he that was possessed with demons was made whole.
LUK 8:37 37 And all the people of the country of the Gerasenes round about asked him to depart from them; for they were holden with great fear: and he entered into a boat, and returned.
LUK 8:38 38 But the man from whom the demons were gone out prayed him that he might be with him: but he sent him away, saying,
LUK 8:39 39 Return to thy house, and declare how great things God hath done for thee. And he went his way, publishing throughout the whole city how great things Jesus had done for him.
LUK 8:40 40 And as Jesus returned, the multitude welcomed him; for they were all waiting for him.
LUK 8:41 41 And behold, there came a man named Jairus, and he was a ruler of the synagogue: and he fell down at Jesus’ feet, and besought him to come into his house;
LUK 8:42 42 for he had an only daughter, about twelve years of age, and she was dying. But as he went the multitudes thronged him.
LUK 8:43 43 And a woman having an issue of blood twelve years, who had spent all her living upon physicians, and could not be healed of any,
LUK 8:44 44 came behind him, and touched the border of his garment: and immediately the issue of her blood stanched.
LUK 8:45 45 And Jesus said, Who is it that touched me? And when all denied, Peter said, and they that were with him, Master, the multitudes press thee and crush thee .
LUK 8:46 46 But Jesus said, Some one did touch me; for I perceived that power had gone forth from me.
LUK 8:47 47 And when the woman saw that she was not hid, she came trembling, and falling down before him declared in the presence of all the people for what cause she touched him, and how she was healed immediately.
LUK 8:48 48 And he said unto her, Daughter, thy faith hath made thee whole; go in peace.
LUK 8:49 49 While he yet spake, there cometh one from the ruler of the synagogue’s house , saying, Thy daughter is dead; trouble not the Teacher.
LUK 8:50 50 But Jesus hearing it, answered him, Fear not: only believe, and she shall be made whole.
LUK 8:51 51 And when he came to the house, he suffered not any man to enter in with him, save Peter, and John, and James, and the father of the maiden and her mother.
LUK 8:52 52 And all were weeping, and bewailing her: but he said, Weep not; for she is not dead, but sleepeth.
LUK 8:53 53 And they laughed him to scorn, knowing that she was dead.
LUK 8:54 54 But he, taking her by the hand, called, saying, Maiden, arise.
LUK 8:55 55 And her spirit returned, and she rose up immediately: and he commanded that something be given her to eat.
LUK 8:56 56 And her parents were amazed: but he charged them to tell no man what had been done.
LUK 9:1 1 And he called the twelve together, and gave them power and authority over all demons, and to cure diseases.
LUK 9:2 2 And he sent them forth to preach the kingdom of God, and to heal the sick.
LUK 9:3 3 And he said unto them, Take nothing for your journey, neither staff, nor wallet, nor bread, nor money; neither have two coats.
LUK 9:4 4 And into whatsoever house ye enter, there abide, and thence depart.
LUK 9:5 5 And as many as receive you not, when ye depart from that city, shake off the dust from your feet for a testimony against them.
LUK 9:6 6 And they departed, and went throughout the villages, preaching the gospel, and healing everywhere.
LUK 9:7 7 Now Herod the tetrarch heard of all that was done: and he was much perplexed, because that it was said by some, that John was risen from the dead;
LUK 9:8 8 and by some, that Elijah had appeared; and by others, that one of the old prophets was risen again.
LUK 9:9 9 And Herod said, John I beheaded: but who is this, about whom I hear such things? And he sought to see him.
LUK 9:10 10 And the apostles, when they were returned, declared unto him what things they had done. And he took them, and withdrew apart to a city called Bethsaida.
LUK 9:11 11 But the multitudes perceiving it followed him: and he welcomed them, and spake to them of the kingdom of God, and them that had need of healing he cured.
LUK 9:12 12 And the day began to wear away; and the twelve came, and said unto him, Send the multitude away, that they may go into the villages and country round about, and lodge, and get provisions: for we are here in a desert place.
LUK 9:13 13 But he said unto them, Give ye them to eat. And they said, We have no more than five loaves and two fishes; except we should go and buy food for all this people.
LUK 9:14 14 For they were about five thousand men. And he said unto his disciples, Make them sit down in companies, about fifty each.
LUK 9:15 15 And they did so, and made them all sit down.
LUK 9:16 16 And he took the five loaves and the two fishes, and looking up to heaven, he blessed them, and brake; and gave to the disciples to set before the multitude.
LUK 9:17 17 And they ate, and were all filled: and there was taken up that which remained over to them of broken pieces, twelve baskets.
LUK 9:18 18 And it came to pass, as he was praying apart, the disciples were with him: and he asked them, saying, Who do the multitudes say that I am?
LUK 9:19 19 And they answering said, John the Baptist; but others say , Elijah; and others, that one of the old prophets is risen again.
LUK 9:20 20 And he said unto them, But who say ye that I am? And Peter answering said, The Christ of God.
LUK 9:21 21 But he charged them, and commanded them to tell this to no man;
LUK 9:22 22 saying, The Son of man must suffer many things, and be rejected of the elders and chief priests and scribes, and be killed, and the third day be raised up.
LUK 9:23 23 And he said unto all, If any man would come after me, let him deny himself, and take up his cross daily, and follow me.
LUK 9:24 24 For whosoever would save his life shall lose it; but whosoever shall lose his life for my sake, the same shall save it.
LUK 9:25 25 For what is a man profited, if he gain the whole world, and lose or forfeit his own self?
LUK 9:26 26 For whosoever shall be ashamed of me and of my words, of him shall the Son of man be ashamed, when he cometh in his own glory, and the glory of the Father, and of the holy angels.
LUK 9:27 27 But I tell you of a truth, There are some of them that stand here, who shall in no wise taste of death, till they see the kingdom of God.
LUK 9:28 28 And it came to pass about eight days after these sayings, that he took with him Peter and John and James, and went up into the mountain to pray.
LUK 9:29 29 And as he was praying, the fashion of his countenance was altered, and his raiment became white and dazzling.
LUK 9:30 30 And behold, there talked with him two men, who were Moses and Elijah;
LUK 9:31 31 who appeared in glory, and spake of his decease which he was about to accomplish at Jerusalem.
LUK 9:32 32 Now Peter and they that were with him were heavy with sleep: but when they were fully awake, they saw his glory, and the two men that stood with him.
LUK 9:33 33 And it came to pass, as they were parting from him, Peter said unto Jesus, Master, it is good for us to be here: and let us make three tabernacles; one for thee, and one for Moses, and one for Elijah: not knowing what he said.
LUK 9:34 34 And while he said these things, there came a cloud, and overshadowed them: and they feared as they entered into the cloud.
LUK 9:35 35 And a voice came out of the cloud, saying, This is my Son, my chosen: hear ye him.
LUK 9:36 36 And when the voice came, Jesus was found alone. And they held their peace, and told no man in those days any of the things which they had seen.
LUK 9:37 37 And it came to pass, on the next day, when they were come down from the mountain, a great multitude met him.
LUK 9:38 38 And behold, a man from the multitude cried, saying, Teacher, I beseech thee to look upon my son; for he is mine only child:
LUK 9:39 39 and behold, a spirit taketh him, and he suddenly crieth out; and it teareth him that he foameth, and it hardly departeth from him, bruising him sorely.
LUK 9:40 40 And I besought thy disciples to cast it out; and they could not.
LUK 9:41 41 And Jesus answered and said, O faithless and perverse generation, how long shall I be with you, and bear with you? bring hither thy son.
LUK 9:42 42 And as he was yet a coming, the demon dashed him down, and tare him grievously. But Jesus rebuked the unclean spirit, and healed the boy, and gave him back to his father.
LUK 9:43 43 And they were all astonished at the majesty of God.
LUK 9:44 But while all were marvelling at all the things which he did, he said unto his disciples,
LUK 9:45 44 Let these words sink into your ears: for the Son of man shall be delivered up into the hands of men.
LUK 9:46 45 But they understood not this saying, and it was concealed from them, that they should not perceive it; and they were afraid to ask him about this saying.
LUK 9:47 46 And there arose a reasoning among them, which of them was the greatest.
LUK 9:48 47 But when Jesus saw the reasoning of their heart, he took a little child, and set him by his side,
LUK 9:49 48 and said unto them, Whosoever shall receive this little child in my name receiveth me: and whosoever shall receive me receiveth him that sent me: for he that is least among you all, the same is great.
LUK 9:50 49 And John answered and said, Master, we saw one casting out demons in thy name; and we forbade him, because he followeth not with us.
LUK 9:51 50 But Jesus said unto him, Forbid him not: for he that is not against you is for you.
LUK 9:52 51 And it came to pass, when the days were well-nigh come that he should be received up, he stedfastly set his face to go to Jerusalem,
LUK 9:53 52 and sent messengers before his face: and they went, and entered into a village of the Samaritans, to make ready for him.
LUK 9:54 53 And they did not receive him, because his face was as though he were going to Jerusalem.
LUK 9:55 54 And when his disciples James and John saw this , they said, Lord, wilt thou that we bid fire to come down from heaven, and consume them?
LUK 9:56 55 But he turned, and rebuked them.
LUK 9:57 56 And they went to another village.
LUK 9:58 57 And as they went on the way, a certain man said unto him, I will follow thee whithersoever thou goest.
LUK 9:59 58 And Jesus said unto him, The foxes have holes, and the birds of the heaven have nests; but the Son of man hath not where to lay his head.
LUK 9:60 59 And he said unto another, Follow me. But he said, Lord, suffer me first to go and bury my father.
LUK 9:61 60 But he said unto him, Leave the dead to bury their own dead; but go thou and publish abroad the kingdom of God.
LUK 9:62 61 And another also said, I will follow thee, Lord; but first suffer me to bid farewell to them that are at my house.
LUK 9:63 62 But Jesus said unto him, No man, having put his hand to the plow, and looking back, is fit for the kingdom of God.
LUK 10:1 1 Now after these things the Lord appointed seventy others, and sent them two and two before his face into every city and place, whither he himself was about to come.
LUK 10:2 2 And he said unto them, The harvest indeed is plenteous, but the laborers are few: pray ye therefore the Lord of the harvest, that he send forth laborers into his harvest.
LUK 10:3 3 Go your ways; behold, I send you forth as lambs in the midst of wolves.
LUK 10:4 4 Carry no purse, no wallet, no shoes; and salute no man on the way.
LUK 10:5 5 And into whatsoever house ye shall enter, first say, Peace be to this house.
LUK 10:6 6 And if a son of peace be there, your peace shall rest upon him: but if not, it shall turn to you again.
LUK 10:7 7 And in that same house remain, eating and drinking such things as they give: for the laborer is worthy of his hire. Go not from house to house.
LUK 10:8 8 And into whatsoever city ye enter, and they receive you, eat such things as are set before you:
LUK 10:9 9 and heal the sick that are therein, and say unto them, The kingdom of God is come nigh unto you.
LUK 10:10 10 But into whatsoever city ye shall enter, and they receive you not, go out into the streets thereof and say,
LUK 10:11 11 Even the dust from your city, that cleaveth to our feet, we wipe off against you: nevertheless know this, that the kingdom of God is come nigh.
LUK 10:12 12 I say unto you, It shall be more tolerable in that day for Sodom, than for that city.
LUK 10:13 13 Woe unto thee, Chorazin! woe unto thee, Bethsaida! for if the mighty works had been done in Tyre and Sidon, which were done in you, they would have repented long ago, sitting in sackcloth and ashes.
LUK 10:14 14 But it shall be more tolerable for Tyre and Sidon in the judgment, than for you.
LUK 10:15 15 And thou, Capernaum, shalt thou be exalted unto heaven? thou shalt be brought down unto Hades.
LUK 10:16 16 He that heareth you heareth me; and he that rejecteth you rejecteth me; and he that rejecteth me rejecteth him that sent me.
LUK 10:17 17 And the seventy returned with joy, saying, Lord, even the demons are subject unto us in thy name.
LUK 10:18 18 And he said unto them, I beheld Satan fallen as lightning from heaven.
LUK 10:19 19 Behold, I have given you authority to tread upon serpents and scorpions, and over all the power of the enemy: and nothing shall in any wise hurt you.
LUK 10:20 20 Nevertheless in this rejoice not, that the spirits are subject unto you; but rejoice that your names are written in heaven.
LUK 10:21 21 In that same hour he rejoiced in the Holy Spirit, and said, I thank thee, O Father, Lord of heaven and earth, that thou didst hide these things from the wise and understanding, and didst reveal them unto babes: yea, Father; for so it was well-pleasing in thy sight.
LUK 10:22 22 All things have been delivered unto me of my Father: and no one knoweth who the Son is, save the Father; and who the Father is, save the Son, and he to whomsoever the Son willeth to reveal him .
LUK 10:23 23 And turning to the disciples, he said privately, Blessed are the eyes which see the things that ye see:
LUK 10:24 24 for I say unto you, that many prophets and kings desired to see the things which ye see, and saw them not; and to hear the things which ye hear, and heard them not.
LUK 10:25 25 And behold, a certain lawyer stood up and made trial of him, saying, Teacher, what shall I do to inherit eternal life?
LUK 10:26 26 And he said unto him, What is written in the law? how readest thou?
LUK 10:27 27 And he answering said, Thou shalt love the Lord thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy strength, and with all thy mind; and thy neighbor as thyself.
LUK 10:28 28 And he said unto him, Thou hast answered right: this do, and thou shalt live.
LUK 10:29 29 But he, desiring to justify himself, said unto Jesus, And who is my neighbor?
LUK 10:30 30 Jesus made answer and said, A certain man was going down from Jerusalem to Jericho; and he fell among robbers, who both stripped him and beat him, and departed, leaving him half dead.
LUK 10:31 31 And by chance a certain priest was going down that way: and when he saw him, he passed by on the other side.
LUK 10:32 32 And in like manner a Levite also, when he came to the place, and saw him, passed by on the other side.
LUK 10:33 33 But a certain Samaritan, as he journeyed, came where he was: and when he saw him, he was moved with compassion,
LUK 10:34 34 and came to him, and bound up his wounds, pouring on them oil and wine; and he set him on his own beast, and brought him to an inn, and took care of him.
LUK 10:35 35 And on the morrow he took out two shillings, and gave them to the host, and said, Take care of him; and whatsoever thou spendest more, I, when I come back again, will repay thee.
LUK 10:36 36 Which of these three, thinkest thou, proved neighbor unto him that fell among the robbers?
LUK 10:37 37 And he said, He that showed mercy on him. And Jesus said unto him, Go, and do thou likewise.
LUK 10:38 38 Now as they went on their way, he entered into a certain village: and a certain woman named Martha received him into her house.
LUK 10:39 39 And she had a sister called Mary, who also sat at the Lord’s feet, and heard his word.
LUK 10:40 40 But Martha was cumbered about much serving; and she came up to him, and said, Lord, dost thou not care that my sister did leave me to serve alone? bid her therefore that she help me.
LUK 10:41 41 But the Lord answered and said unto her, Martha, Martha, thou art anxious and troubled about many things:
LUK 10:42 42 but one thing is needful: for Mary hath chosen the good part, which shall not be taken away from her.
LUK 11:1 1 And it came to pass, as he was praying in a certain place, that when he ceased, one of his disciples said unto him, Lord, teach us to pray, even as John also taught his disciples.
LUK 11:2 2 And he said unto them, When ye pray, say, Father, Hallowed be thy name. Thy kingdom come.
LUK 11:3 3 Give us day by day our daily bread.
LUK 11:4 4 And forgive us our sins; for we ourselves also forgive every one that is indebted to us. And bring us not into temptation.
LUK 11:5 5 And he said unto them, Which of you shall have a friend, and shall go unto him at midnight, and say to him, Friend, lend me three loaves;
LUK 11:6 6 for a friend of mine is come to me from a journey, and I have nothing to set before him;
LUK 11:7 7 and he from within shall answer and say, Trouble me not: the door is now shut, and my children are with me in bed; I cannot rise and give thee?
LUK 11:8 8 I say unto you, Though he will not rise and give him because he is his friend, yet because of his importunity he will arise and give him as many as he needeth.
LUK 11:9 9 And I say unto you, Ask, and it shall be given you; seek, and ye shall find; knock, and it shall be opened unto you.
LUK 11:10 10 For every one that asketh receiveth; and he that seeketh findeth; and to him that knocketh it shall be opened.
LUK 11:11 11 And of which of you that is a father shall his son ask a loaf, and he give him a stone? or a fish, and he for a fish give him a serpent?
LUK 11:12 12 Or if he shall ask an egg, will he give him a scorpion?
LUK 11:13 13 If ye then, being evil, know how to give good gifts unto your children, how much more shall your heavenly Father give the Holy Spirit to them that ask him?
LUK 11:14 14 And he was casting out a demon that was dumb. And it came to pass, when the demon was gone out, the dumb man spake; and the multitudes marvelled.
LUK 11:15 15 But some of them said, By Beelzebub the prince of the demons casteth he out demons.
LUK 11:16 16 And others, trying him , sought of him a sign from heaven.
LUK 11:17 17 But he, knowing their thoughts, said unto them, Every kingdom divided against itself is brought to desolation; and a house divided against a house falleth.
LUK 11:18 18 And if Satan also is divided against himself, how shall his kingdom stand? because ye say that I cast out demons by Beelzebub.
LUK 11:19 19 And if I by Beelzebub cast out demons, by whom do your sons cast them out? therefore shall they be your judges.
LUK 11:20 20 But if I by the finger of God cast out demons, then is the kingdom of God come upon you.
LUK 11:21 21 When the strong man fully armed guardeth his own court, his goods are in peace:
LUK 11:22 22 but when a stronger than he shall come upon him, and overcome him, he taketh from him his whole armor wherein he trusted, and divideth his spoils.
LUK 11:23 23 He that is not with me is against me; and he that gathereth not with me scattereth.
LUK 11:24 24 The unclean spirit when he is gone out of the man, passeth through waterless places, seeking rest, and finding none, he saith, I will turn back unto my house whence I came out.
LUK 11:25 25 And when he is come, he findeth it swept and garnished.
LUK 11:26 26 Then goeth he, and taketh to him seven other spirits more evil than himself; and they enter in and dwell there: and the last state of that man becometh worse than the first.
LUK 11:27 27 And it came to pass, as he said these things, a certain woman out of the multitude lifted up her voice, and said unto him, Blessed is the womb that bare thee, and the breasts which thou didst suck.
LUK 11:28 28 But he said, Yea rather, blessed are they that hear the word of God, and keep it.
LUK 11:29 29 And when the multitudes were gathering together unto him, he began to say, This generation is an evil generation: it seeketh after a sign; and there shall no sign be given to it but the sign of Jonah.
LUK 11:30 30 For even as Jonah became a sign unto the Ninevites, so shall also the Son of man be to this generation.
LUK 11:31 31 The queen of the south shall rise up in the judgment with the men of this generation, and shall condemn them: for she came from the ends of the earth to hear the wisdom of Solomon; and behold, a greater than Solomon is here.
LUK 11:32 32 The men of Nineveh shall stand up in the judgment with this generation, and shall condemn it: for they repented at the preaching of Jonah; and behold, a greater than Jonah is here.
LUK 11:33 33 No man, when he hath lighted a lamp, putteth it in a cellar, neither under the bushel, but on the stand, that they which enter in may see the light.
LUK 11:34 34 The lamp of thy body is thine eye: when thine eye is single, thy whole body also is full of light; but when it is evil, thy body also is full of darkness.
LUK 11:35 35 Look therefore whether the light that is in thee be not darkness.
LUK 11:36 36 If therefore thy whole body be full of light, having no part dark, it shall be wholly full of light, as when the lamp with its bright shining doth give thee light.
LUK 11:37 37 Now as he spake, a Pharisee asketh him to dine with him: and he went in, and sat down to meat.
LUK 11:38 38 And when the Pharisee saw it, he marvelled that he had not first bathed himself before dinner.
LUK 11:39 39 And the Lord said unto him, Now ye the Pharisees cleanse the outside of the cup and of the platter; but your inward part is full of extortion and wickedness.
LUK 11:40 40 Ye foolish ones, did not he that made the outside make the inside also?
LUK 11:41 41 But give for alms those things which are within; and behold, all things are clean unto you.
LUK 11:42 42 But woe unto you Pharisees! for ye tithe mint and rue and every herb, and pass over justice and the love of God: but these ought ye to have done, and not to leave the other undone.
LUK 11:43 43 Woe unto you Pharisees! for ye love the chief seats in the synagogues, and the salutations in the marketplaces.
LUK 11:44 44 Woe unto you! for ye are as the tombs which appear not, and the men that walk over them know it not.
LUK 11:45 45 And one of the lawyers answering saith unto him, Teacher, in saying this thou reproachest us also.
LUK 11:46 46 And he said, Woe unto you lawyers also! for ye load men with burdens grievous to be borne, and ye yourselves touch not the burdens with one of your fingers.
LUK 11:47 47 Woe unto you! for ye build the tombs of the prophets, and your fathers killed them.
LUK 11:48 48 So ye are witnesses and consent unto the works of your fathers: for they killed them, and ye build their tombs .
LUK 11:49 49 Therefore also said the wisdom of God, I will send unto them prophets and apostles; and some of them they shall kill and persecute;
LUK 11:50 50 that the blood of all the prophets, which was shed from the foundation of the world, may be required of this generation;
LUK 11:51 51 from the blood of Abel unto the blood of Zachariah, who perished between the altar and the sanctuary: yea, I say unto you, it shall be required of this generation.
LUK 11:52 52 Woe unto you lawyers! for ye took away the key of knowledge: ye entered not in yourselves, and them that were entering in ye hindered.
LUK 11:53 53 And when he was come out from thence, the scribes and the Pharisees began to press upon him vehemently, and to provoke him to speak of many things;
LUK 11:54 54 laying wait for him, to catch something out of his mouth.
LUK 12:1 1 In the mean time, when the many thousands of the multitude were gathered together, insomuch that they trod one upon another, he began to say unto his disciples first of all, Beware ye of the leaven of the Pharisees, which is hypocrisy.
LUK 12:2 2 But there is nothing covered up, that shall not be revealed; and hid, that shall not be known.
LUK 12:3 3 Wherefore whatsoever ye have said in the darkness shall be heard in the light; and what ye have spoken in the ear in the inner chambers shall be proclaimed upon the housetops.
LUK 12:4 4 And I say unto you my friends, Be not afraid of them that kill the body, and after that have no more that they can do.
LUK 12:5 5 But I will warn you whom ye shall fear: Fear him, who after he hath killed hath power to cast into hell; yea, I say unto you, Fear him.
LUK 12:6 6 Are not five sparrows sold for two pence? and not one of them is forgotten in the sight of God.
LUK 12:7 7 But the very hairs of your head are all numbered. Fear not: ye are of more value than many sparrows.
LUK 12:8 8 And I say unto you, Every one who shall confess me before men, him shall the Son of man also confess before the angels of God:
LUK 12:9 9 but he that denieth me in the presence of men shall be denied in the presence of the angels of God.
LUK 12:10 10 And every one who shall speak a word against the Son of man, it shall be forgiven him: but unto him that blasphemeth against the Holy Spirit it shall not be forgiven.
LUK 12:11 11 And when they bring you before the synagogues, and the rulers, and the authorities, be not anxious how or what ye shall answer, or what ye shall say:
LUK 12:12 12 for the Holy Spirit shall teach you in that very hour what ye ought to say.
LUK 12:13 13 And one out of the multitude said unto him, Teacher, bid my brother divide the inheritance with me.
LUK 12:14 14 But he said unto him, Man, who made me a judge or a divider over you?
LUK 12:15 15 And he said unto them, Take heed, and keep yourselves from all covetousness: for a man’s life consisteth not in the abundance of the things which he possesseth.
LUK 12:16 16 And he spake a parable unto them, saying, The ground of a certain rich man brought forth plentifully:
LUK 12:17 17 and he reasoned within himself, saying, What shall I do, because I have not where to bestow my fruits?
LUK 12:18 18 And he said, This will I do: I will pull down my barns, and build greater; and there will I bestow all my grain and my goods.
LUK 12:19 19 And I will say to my soul, Soul, thou hast much goods laid up for many years; take thine ease, eat, drink, be merry.
LUK 12:20 20 But God said unto him, Thou foolish one, this night is thy soul required of thee; and the things which thou hast prepared, whose shall they be?
LUK 12:21 21 So is he that layeth up treasure for himself, and is not rich toward God.
LUK 12:22 22 And he said unto his disciples, Therefore I say unto you, Be not anxious for your life, what ye shall eat; nor yet for your body, what ye shall put on.
LUK 12:23 23 For the life is more than the food, and the body than the raiment.
LUK 12:24 24 Consider the ravens, that they sow not, neither reap; which have no store-chamber nor barn; and God feedeth them: of how much more value are ye than the birds!
LUK 12:25 25 And which of you by being anxious can add a cubit unto the measure of his life?
LUK 12:26 26 If then ye are not able to do even that which is least, why are ye anxious concerning the rest?
LUK 12:27 27 Consider the lilies, how they grow: they toil not, neither do they spin; yet I say unto you, Even Solomon in all his glory was not arrayed like one of these.
LUK 12:28 28 But if God doth so clothe the grass in the field, which to-day is, and to-morrow is cast into the oven; how much more shall he clothe you, O ye of little faith?
LUK 12:29 29 And seek not ye what ye shall eat, and what ye shall drink, neither be ye of doubtful mind.
LUK 12:30 30 For all these things do the nations of the world seek after: but your Father knoweth that ye have need of these things.
LUK 12:31 31 Yet seek ye his kingdom, and these things shall be added unto you.
LUK 12:32 32 Fear not, little flock; for it is your Father’s good pleasure to give you the kingdom.
LUK 12:33 33 Sell that which ye have, and give alms; make for yourselves purses which wax not old, a treasure in the heavens that faileth not, where no thief draweth near, neither moth destroyeth.
LUK 12:34 34 For where your treasure is, there will your heart be also.
LUK 12:35 35 Let your loins be girded about, and your lamps burning;
LUK 12:36 36 and be ye yourselves like unto men looking for their lord, when he shall return from the marriage feast; that, when he cometh and knocketh, they may straightway open unto him.
LUK 12:37 37 Blessed are those servants, whom the lord when he cometh shall find watching: verily I say unto you, that he shall gird himself, and make them sit down to meat, and shall come and serve them.
LUK 12:38 38 And if he shall come in the second watch, and if in the third, and find them so, blessed are those servants .
LUK 12:39 39 But know this, that if the master of the house had known in what hour the thief was coming, he would have watched, and not have left his house to be broken through.
LUK 12:40 40 Be ye also ready: for in an hour that ye think not the Son of man cometh.
LUK 12:41 41 And Peter said, Lord, speakest thou this parable unto us, or even unto all?
LUK 12:42 42 And the Lord said, Who then is the faithful and wise steward, whom his lord shall set over his household, to give them their portion of food in due season?
LUK 12:43 43 Blessed is that servant, whom his lord when he cometh shall find so doing.
LUK 12:44 44 Of a truth I say unto you, that he will set him over all that he hath.
LUK 12:45 45 But if that servant shall say in his heart, My lord delayeth his coming; and shall begin to beat the menservants and the maidservants, and to eat and drink, and to be drunken;
LUK 12:46 46 the lord of that servant shall come in a day when he expecteth not, and in an hour when he knoweth not, and shall cut him asunder, and appoint his portion with the unfaithful.
LUK 12:47 47 And that servant, who knew his lord’s will, and made not ready, nor did according to his will, shall be beaten with many stripes ;
LUK 12:48 48 but he that knew not, and did things worthy of stripes, shall be beaten with few stripes . And to whomsoever much is given, of him shall much be required: and to whom they commit much, of him will they ask the more.
LUK 12:49 49 I came to cast fire upon the earth; and what do I desire, if it is already kindled?
LUK 12:50 50 But I have a baptism to be baptized with; and how am I straitened till it be accomplished!
LUK 12:51 51 Think ye that I am come to give peace in the earth? I tell you, Nay; but rather division:
LUK 12:52 52 for there shall be from henceforth five in one house divided, three against two, and two against three.
LUK 12:53 53 They shall be divided, father against son, and son against father; mother against daughter, and daughter against her mother; mother in law against her daughter in law, and daughter in law against her mother in law.
LUK 12:54 54 And he said to the multitudes also, When ye see a cloud rising in the west, straightway ye say, There cometh a shower; and so it cometh to pass.
LUK 12:55 55 And when ye see a south wind blowing, ye say, There will be a scorching heat; and it cometh to pass.
LUK 12:56 56 Ye hypocrites, ye know how to interpret the face of the earth and the heaven; but how is it that ye know not how to interpret this time?
LUK 12:57 57 And why even of yourselves judge ye not what is right?
LUK 12:58 58 For as thou art going with thine adversary before the magistrate, on the way give diligence to be quit of him; lest haply he drag thee unto the judge, and the judge shall deliver thee to the officer, and the officer shall cast thee into prison.
LUK 12:59 59 I say unto thee, Thou shalt by no means come out thence, till thou have paid the very last mite.
LUK 13:1 1 Now there were some present at that very season who told him of the Galilæans, whose blood Pilate had mingled with their sacrifices.
LUK 13:2 2 And he answered and said unto them, Think ye that these Galilæans were sinners above all the Galilæans, because they have suffered these things?
LUK 13:3 3 I tell you, Nay: but, except ye repent, ye shall all in like manner perish.
LUK 13:4 4 Or those eighteen, upon whom the tower in Siloam fell, and killed them, think ye that they were offenders above all the men that dwell in Jerusalem?
LUK 13:5 5 I tell you, Nay: but, except ye repent, ye shall all likewise perish.
LUK 13:6 6 And he spake this parable; A certain man had a fig tree planted in his vineyard; and he came seeking fruit thereon, and found none.
LUK 13:7 7 And he said unto the vinedresser, Behold, these three years I come seeking fruit on this fig tree, and find none: cut it down; why doth it also cumber the ground?
LUK 13:8 8 And he answering saith unto him, Lord, let it alone this year also, till I shall dig about it, and dung it:
LUK 13:9 9 and if it bear fruit thenceforth, well ; but if not, thou shalt cut it down.
LUK 13:10 10 And he was teaching in one of the synagogues on the sabbath day.
LUK 13:11 11 And behold, a woman that had a spirit of infirmity eighteen years; and she was bowed together, and could in no wise lift herself up.
LUK 13:12 12 And when Jesus saw her, he called her, and said to her, Woman, thou art loosed from thine infirmity.
LUK 13:13 13 And he laid his hands upon her: and immediately she was made straight, and glorified God.
LUK 13:14 14 And the ruler of the synagogue, being moved with indignation because Jesus had healed on the sabbath, answered and said to the multitude, There are six days in which men ought to work: in them therefore come and be healed, and not on the day of the sabbath.
LUK 13:15 15 But the Lord answered him, and said, Ye hypocrites, doth not each one of you on the sabbath loose his ox or his ass from the stall, and lead him away to watering?
LUK 13:16 16 And ought not this woman, being a daughter of Abraham, whom Satan had bound, lo, these eighteen years, to have been loosed from this bond on the day of the sabbath?
LUK 13:17 17 And as he said these things, all his adversaries were put to shame: and all the multitude rejoiced for all the glorious things that were done by him.
LUK 13:18 18 He said therefore, Unto what is the kingdom of God like? and whereunto shall I liken it?
LUK 13:19 19 It is like unto a grain of mustard seed, which a man took, and cast into his own garden; and it grew, and became a tree; and the birds of the heaven lodged in the branches thereof.
LUK 13:20 20 And again he said, Whereunto shall I liken the kingdom of God?
LUK 13:21 21 It is like unto leaven, which a woman took and hid in three measures of meal, till it was all leavened.
LUK 13:22 22 And he went on his way through cities and villages, teaching, and journeying on unto Jerusalem.
LUK 13:23 23 And one said unto him, Lord, are they few that are saved? And he said unto them,
LUK 13:24 24 Strive to enter in by the narrow door: for many, I say unto you, shall seek to enter in, and shall not be able.
LUK 13:25 25 When once the master of the house is risen up, and hath shut to the door, and ye begin to stand without, and to knock at the door, saying, Lord, open to us; and he shall answer and say to you, I know you not whence ye are;
LUK 13:26 26 then shall ye begin to say, We did eat and drink in thy presence, and thou didst teach in our streets;
LUK 13:27 27 and he shall say, I tell you, I know not whence ye are; depart from me, all ye workers of iniquity.
LUK 13:28 28 There shall be the weeping and the gnashing of teeth, when ye shall see Abraham, and Isaac, and Jacob, and all the prophets, in the kingdom of God, and yourselves cast forth without.
LUK 13:29 29 And they shall come from the east and west, and from the north and south, and shall sit down in the kingdom of God.
LUK 13:30 30 And behold, there are last who shall be first, and there are first who shall be last.
LUK 13:31 31 In that very hour there came certain Pharisees, saying to him, Get thee out, and go hence: for Herod would fain kill thee.
LUK 13:32 32 And he said unto them, Go and say to that fox, Behold, I cast out demons and perform cures to-day and to-morrow, and the third day I am perfected.
LUK 13:33 33 Nevertheless I must go on my way to-day and to-morrow and the day following: for it cannot be that a prophet perish out of Jerusalem.
LUK 13:34 34 O Jerusalem, Jerusalem, that killeth the prophets, and stoneth them that are sent unto her! how often would I have gathered thy children together, even as a hen gathereth her own brood under her wings, and ye would not!
LUK 13:35 35 Behold, your house is left unto you desolate : and I say unto you, Ye shall not see me, until ye shall say, Blessed is he that cometh in the name of the Lord.
LUK 14:1 1 And it came to pass, when he went into the house of one of the rulers of the Pharisees on a sabbath to eat bread, that they were watching him.
LUK 14:2 2 And behold, there was before him a certain man that had the dropsy.
LUK 14:3 3 And Jesus answering spake unto the lawyers and Pharisees, saying, Is it lawful to heal on the sabbath, or not?
LUK 14:4 4 But they held their peace. And he took him, and healed him, and let him go.
LUK 14:5 5 And he said unto them, Which of you shall have an ass or an ox fallen into a well, and will not straightway draw him up on a sabbath day?
LUK 14:6 6 And they could not answer again unto these things.
LUK 14:7 7 And he spake a parable unto those that were bidden, when he marked how they chose out the chief seats; saying unto them,
LUK 14:8 8 When thou art bidden of any man to a marriage feast, sit not down in the chief seat; lest haply a more honorable man than thou be bidden of him,
LUK 14:9 9 and he that bade thee and him shall come and say to thee, Give this man place; and then thou shalt begin with shame to take the lowest place.
LUK 14:10 10 But when thou art bidden, go and sit down in the lowest place; that when he that hath bidden thee cometh, he may say to thee, Friend, go up higher: then shalt thou have glory in the presence of all that sit at meat with thee.
LUK 14:11 11 For every one that exalteth himself shall be humbled; and he that humbleth himself shall be exalted.
LUK 14:12 12 And he said to him also that had bidden him, When thou makest a dinner or a supper, call not thy friends, nor thy brethren, nor thy kinsmen, nor rich neighbors; lest haply they also bid thee again, and a recompense be made thee.
LUK 14:13 13 But when thou makest a feast, bid the poor, the maimed, the lame, the blind:
LUK 14:14 14 and thou shalt be blessed; because they have not wherewith to recompense thee: for thou shalt be recompensed in the resurrection of the just.
LUK 14:15 15 And when one of them that sat at meat with him heard these things, he said unto him, Blessed is he that shall eat bread in the kingdom of God.
LUK 14:16 16 But he said unto him, A certain man made a great supper; and he bade many:
LUK 14:17 17 and he sent forth his servant at supper time to say to them that were bidden, Come; for all things are now ready.
LUK 14:18 18 And they all with one consent began to make excuse. The first said unto him, I have bought a field, and I must needs go out and see it; I pray thee have me excused.
LUK 14:19 19 And another said, I have bought five yoke of oxen, and I go to prove them; I pray thee have me excused.
LUK 14:20 20 And another said, I have married a wife, and therefore I cannot come.
LUK 14:21 21 And the servant came, and told his lord these things. Then the master of the house being angry said to his servant, Go out quickly into the streets and lanes of the city, and bring in hither the poor and maimed and blind and lame.
LUK 14:22 22 And the servant said, Lord, what thou didst command is done, and yet there is room.
LUK 14:23 23 And the lord said unto the servant, Go out into the highways and hedges, and constrain them to come in, that my house may be filled.
LUK 14:24 24 For I say unto you, that none of those men that were bidden shall taste of my supper.
LUK 14:25 25 Now there went with him great multitudes: and he turned, and said unto them,
LUK 14:26 26 If any man cometh unto me, and hateth not his own father, and mother, and wife, and children, and brethren, and sisters, yea, and his own life also, he cannot be my disciple.
LUK 14:27 27 Whosoever doth not bear his own cross, and come after me, cannot be my disciple.
LUK 14:28 28 For which of you, desiring to build a tower, doth not first sit down and count the cost, whether he have wherewith to complete it?
LUK 14:29 29 Lest haply, when he hath laid a foundation, and is not able to finish, all that behold begin to mock him,
LUK 14:30 30 saying, This man began to build, and was not able to finish.
LUK 14:31 31 Or what king, as he goeth to encounter another king in war, will not sit down first and take counsel whether he is able with ten thousand to meet him that cometh against him with twenty thousand?
LUK 14:32 32 Or else, while the other is yet a great way off, he sendeth an ambassage, and asketh conditions of peace.
LUK 14:33 33 So therefore whosoever he be of you that renounceth not all that he hath, he cannot be my disciple.
LUK 14:34 34 Salt therefore is good: but if even the salt have lost its savor, wherewith shall it be seasoned?
LUK 14:35 35 It is fit neither for the land nor for the dunghill: men cast it out. He that hath ears to hear, let him hear.
LUK 15:1 1 Now all the publicans and sinners were drawing near unto him to hear him.
LUK 15:2 2 And both the Pharisees and the scribes murmured, saying, This man receiveth sinners, and eateth with them.
LUK 15:3 3 And he spake unto them this parable, saying,
LUK 15:4 4 What man of you, having a hundred sheep, and having lost one of them, doth not leave the ninety and nine in the wilderness, and go after that which is lost, until he find it?
LUK 15:5 5 And when he hath found it, he layeth it on his shoulders, rejoicing.
LUK 15:6 6 And when he cometh home, he calleth together his friends and his neighbors, saying unto them, Rejoice with me, for I have found my sheep which was lost.
LUK 15:7 7 I say unto you, that even so there shall be joy in heaven over one sinner that repenteth, more than over ninety and nine righteous persons, who need no repentance.
LUK 15:8 8 Or what woman having ten pieces of silver, if she lose one piece, doth not light a lamp, and sweep the house, and seek diligently until she find it?
LUK 15:9 9 And when she hath found it, she calleth together her friends and neighbors, saying, Rejoice with me, for I have found the piece which I had lost.
LUK 15:10 10 Even so, I say unto you, there is joy in the presence of the angels of God over one sinner that repenteth.
LUK 15:11 11 And he said, A certain man had two sons:
LUK 15:12 12 and the younger of them said to his father, Father, give me the portion of thy substance that falleth to me. And he divided unto them his living.
LUK 15:13 13 And not many days after, the younger son gathered all together and took his journey into a far country; and there he wasted his substance with riotous living.
LUK 15:14 14 And when he had spent all, there arose a mighty famine in that country; and he began to be in want.
LUK 15:15 15 And he went and joined himself to one of the citizens of that country; and he sent him into his fields to feed swine.
LUK 15:16 16 And he would fain have filled his belly with the husks that the swine did eat: and no man gave unto him.
LUK 15:17 17 But when he came to himself he said, How many hired servants of my father’s have bread enough and to spare, and I perish here with hunger!
LUK 15:18 18 I will arise and go to my father, and will say unto him, Father, I have sinned against heaven, and in thy sight:
LUK 15:19 19 I am no more worthy to be called thy son: make me as one of thy hired servants.
LUK 15:20 20 And he arose, and came to his father. But while he was yet afar off, his father saw him, and was moved with compassion, and ran, and fell on his neck, and kissed him.
LUK 15:21 21 And the son said unto him, Father, I have sinned against heaven, and in thy sight: I am no more worthy to be called thy son.
LUK 15:22 22 But the father said to his servants, Bring forth quickly the best robe, and put it on him; and put a ring on his hand, and shoes on his feet:
LUK 15:23 23 and bring the fatted calf, and kill it, and let us eat, and make merry:
LUK 15:24 24 for this my son was dead, and is alive again; he was lost, and is found. And they began to be merry.
LUK 15:25 25 Now his elder son was in the field: and as he came and drew nigh to the house, he heard music and dancing.
LUK 15:26 26 And he called to him one of the servants, and inquired what these things might be.
LUK 15:27 27 And he said unto him, Thy brother is come; and thy father hath killed the fatted calf, because he hath received him safe and sound.
LUK 15:28 28 But he was angry, and would not go in: and his father came out, and entreated him.
LUK 15:29 29 But he answered and said to his father, Lo, these many years do I serve thee, and I never transgressed a commandment of thine; and yet thou never gavest me a kid, that I might make merry with my friends:
LUK 15:30 30 but when this thy son came, who hath devoured thy living with harlots, thou killedst for him the fatted calf.
LUK 15:31 31 And he said unto him, Son, thou art ever with me, and all that is mine is thine.
LUK 15:32 32 But it was meet to make merry and be glad: for this thy brother was dead, and is alive again ; and was lost, and is found.
LUK 16:1 1 And he said also unto the disciples, There was a certain rich man, who had a steward; and the same was accused unto him that he was wasting his goods.
LUK 16:2 2 And he called him, and said unto him, What is this that I hear of thee? render the account of thy stewardship; for thou canst be no longer steward.
LUK 16:3 3 And the steward said within himself, What shall I do, seeing that my lord taketh away the stewardship from me? I have not strength to dig; to beg I am ashamed.
LUK 16:4 4 I am resolved what to do, that, when I am put out of the stewardship, they may receive me into their houses.
LUK 16:5 5 And calling to him each one of his lord’s debtors, he said to the first, How much owest thou unto my lord?
LUK 16:6 6 And he said, A hundred measures of oil. And he said unto him, Take thy bond, and sit down quickly and write fifty.
