LUK 16:7 7 Then said he to another, And how much owest thou? And he said, A hundred measures of wheat. He saith unto him, Take thy bond, and write fourscore.
LUK 16:8 8 And his lord commended the unrighteous steward because he had done wisely: for the sons of this world are for their own generation wiser than the sons of the light.
LUK 16:9 9 And I say unto you, Make to yourselves friends by means of the mammon of unrighteousness; that, when it shall fail, they may receive you into the eternal tabernacles.
LUK 16:10 10 He that is faithful in a very little is faithful also in much: and he that is unrighteous in a very little is unrighteous also in much.
LUK 16:11 11 If therefore ye have not been faithful in the unrighteous mammon, who will commit to your trust the true riches ?
LUK 16:12 12 And if ye have not been faithful in that which is another’s, who will give you that which is your own?
LUK 16:13 13 No servant can serve two masters: for either he will hate the one, and love the other; or else he will hold to one, and despise the other. Ye cannot serve God and mammon.
LUK 16:14 14 And the Pharisees, who were lovers of money, heard all these things; and they scoffed at him.
LUK 16:15 15 And he said unto them, Ye are they that justify yourselves in the sight of men; but God knoweth your hearts: for that which is exalted among men is an abomination in the sight of God.
LUK 16:16 16 The law and the prophets were until John: from that time the gospel of the kingdom of God is preached, and every man entereth violently into it.
LUK 16:17 17 But it is easier for heaven and earth to pass away, than for one tittle of the law to fall.
LUK 16:18 18 Every one that putteth away his wife, and marrieth another, committeth adultery: and he that marrieth one that is put away from a husband committeth adultery.
LUK 16:19 19 Now there was a certain rich man, and he was clothed in purple and fine linen, faring sumptuously every day:
LUK 16:20 20 and a certain beggar named Lazarus was laid at his gate, full of sores,
LUK 16:21 21 and desiring to be fed with the crumbs that fell from the rich man’s table; yea, even the dogs came and licked his sores.
LUK 16:22 22 And it came to pass, that the beggar died, and that he was carried away by the angels into Abraham’s bosom: and the rich man also died, and was buried.
LUK 16:23 23 And in Hades he lifted up his eyes, being in torments, and seeth Abraham afar off, and Lazarus in his bosom.
LUK 16:24 24 And he cried and said, Father Abraham, have mercy on me, and send Lazarus, that he may dip the tip of his finger in water, and cool my tongue; for I am in anguish in this flame.
LUK 16:25 25 But Abraham said, Son, remember that thou in thy lifetime receivedst thy good things, and Lazarus in like manner evil things: but now here he is comforted, and thou art in anguish.
LUK 16:26 26 And besides all this, between us and you there is a great gulf fixed, that they that would pass from hence to you may not be able, and that none may cross over from thence to us.
LUK 16:27 27 And he said, I pray thee therefore, father, that thou wouldest send him to my father’s house;
LUK 16:28 28 for I have five brethren; that he may testify unto them, lest they also come into this place of torment.
LUK 16:29 29 But Abraham saith, They have Moses and the prophets; let them hear them.
LUK 16:30 30 And he said, Nay, father Abraham: but if one go to them from the dead, they will repent.
LUK 16:31 31 And he said unto him, If they hear not Moses and the prophets, neither will they be persuaded, if one rise from the dead.
LUK 17:1 1 And he said unto his disciples, It is impossible but that occasions of stumbling should come; but woe unto him, through whom they come!
LUK 17:2 2 It were well for him if a millstone were hanged about his neck, and he were thrown into the sea, rather than that he should cause one of these little ones to stumble.
LUK 17:3 3 Take heed to yourselves: if thy brother sin, rebuke him; and if he repent, forgive him.
LUK 17:4 4 And if he sin against thee seven times in the day, and seven times turn again to thee, saying, I repent; thou shalt forgive him.
LUK 17:5 5 And the apostles said unto the Lord, Increase our faith.
LUK 17:6 6 And the Lord said, If ye had faith as a grain of mustard seed, ye would say unto this sycamine tree, Be thou rooted up, and be thou planted in the sea; and it would obey you.
LUK 17:7 7 But who is there of you, having a servant plowing or keeping sheep, that will say unto him, when he is come in from the field, Come straightway and sit down to meat;
LUK 17:8 8 and will not rather say unto him, Make ready wherewith I may sup, and gird thyself, and serve me, till I have eaten and drunken; and afterward thou shalt eat and drink?
LUK 17:9 9 Doth he thank the servant because he did the things that were commanded?
LUK 17:10 10 Even so ye also, when ye shall have done all the things that are commanded you, say, We are unprofitable servants; we have done that which it was our duty to do.
LUK 17:11 11 And it came to pass, as they were on the way to Jerusalem, that he was passing along the borders of Samaria and Galilee.
LUK 17:12 12 And as he entered into a certain village, there met him ten men that were lepers, who stood afar off:
LUK 17:13 13 and they lifted up their voices, saying, Jesus, Master, have mercy on us.
LUK 17:14 14 And when he saw them, he said unto them, Go and show yourselves unto the priests. And it came to pass, as they went, they were cleansed.
LUK 17:15 15 And one of them, when he saw that he was healed, turned back, with a loud voice glorifying God;
LUK 17:16 16 and he fell upon his face at his feet, giving him thanks: and he was a Samaritan.
LUK 17:17 17 And Jesus answering said, Were not the ten cleansed? but where are the nine?
LUK 17:18 18 Were there none found that returned to give glory to God, save this stranger?
LUK 17:19 19 And he said unto him, Arise, and go thy way: thy faith hath made thee whole.
LUK 17:20 20 And being asked by the Pharisees, when the kingdom of God cometh, he answered them and said, The kingdom of God cometh not with observation:
LUK 17:21 21 neither shall they say, Lo, here! or, There! for lo, the kingdom of God is within you.
LUK 17:22 22 And he said unto the disciples, The days will come, when ye shall desire to see one of the days of the Son of man, and ye shall not see it.
LUK 17:23 23 And they shall say to you, Lo, there! Lo, here! go not away, nor follow after them :
LUK 17:24 24 for as the lightning, when it lighteneth out of the one part under the heaven, shineth unto the other part under heaven; so shall the Son of man be in his day.
LUK 17:25 25 But first must he suffer many things and be rejected of this generation.
LUK 17:26 26 And as it came to pass in the days of Noah, even so shall it be also in the days of the Son of man.
LUK 17:27 27 They ate, they drank, they married, they were given in marriage, until the day that Noah entered into the ark, and the flood came, and destroyed them all.
LUK 17:28 28 Likewise even as it came to pass in the days of Lot; they ate, they drank, they bought, they sold, they planted, they builded;
LUK 17:29 29 but in the day that Lot went out from Sodom it rained fire and brimstone from heaven, and destroyed them all:
LUK 17:30 30 after the same manner shall it be in the day that the Son of man is revealed.
LUK 17:31 31 In that day, he that shall be on the housetop, and his goods in the house, let him not go down to take them away: and let him that is in the field likewise not return back.
LUK 17:32 32 Remember Lot’s wife.
LUK 17:33 33 Whosoever shall seek to gain his life shall lose it: but whosoever shall lose his life shall preserve it.
LUK 17:34 34 I say unto you, In that night there shall be two men on one bed; the one shall be taken, and the other shall be left.
LUK 17:35 35 There shall be two women grinding together; the one shall be taken, and the other shall be left.
LUK 17:36 36
LUK 17:37 37 And they answering say unto him, Where, Lord? And he said unto them, Where the body is , thither will the eagles also be gathered together.
LUK 18:1 1 And he spake a parable unto them to the end that they ought always to pray, and not to faint;
LUK 18:2 2 saying, There was in a city a judge, who feared not God, and regarded not man:
LUK 18:3 3 and there was a widow in that city; and she came oft unto him, saying, Avenge me of mine adversary.
LUK 18:4 4 And he would not for a while: but afterward he said within himself, Though I fear not God, nor regard man;
LUK 18:5 5 yet because this widow troubleth me, I will avenge her, lest she wear me out by her continual coming.
LUK 18:6 6 And the Lord said, Hear what the unrighteous judge saith.
LUK 18:7 7 And shall not God avenge his elect, that cry to him day and night, and yet he is longsuffering over them?
LUK 18:8 8 I say unto you, that he will avenge them speedily. Nevertheless, when the Son of man cometh, shall he find faith on the earth?
LUK 18:9 9 And he spake also this parable unto certain who trusted in themselves that they were righteous, and set all others at nought:
LUK 18:10 10 Two men went up into the temple to pray; the one a Pharisee, and the other a publican.
LUK 18:11 11 The Pharisee stood and prayed thus with himself, God, I thank thee, that I am not as the rest of men, extortioners, unjust, adulterers, or even as this publican.
LUK 18:12 12 I fast twice in the week; I give tithes of all that I get.
LUK 18:13 13 But the publican, standing afar off, would not lift up so much as his eyes unto heaven, but smote his breast, saying, God, be thou merciful to me a sinner.
LUK 18:14 14 I say unto you, This man went down to his house justified rather than the other: for every one that exalteth himself shall be humbled; but he that humbleth himself shall be exalted.
LUK 18:15 15 And they were bringing unto him also their babes, that he should touch them: but when the disciples saw it, they rebuked them.
LUK 18:16 16 But Jesus called them unto him, saying, Suffer the little children to come unto me, and forbid them not: for to such belongeth the kingdom of God.
LUK 18:17 17 Verily I say unto you, Whosoever shall not receive the kingdom of God as a little child, he shall in no wise enter therein.
LUK 18:18 18 And a certain ruler asked him, saying, Good Teacher, what shall I do to inherit eternal life?
LUK 18:19 19 And Jesus said unto him, Why callest thou me good? none is good, save one, even God.
LUK 18:20 20 Thou knowest the commandments, Do not commit adultery, Do not kill, Do not steal, Do not bear false witness, Honor thy father and mother.
LUK 18:21 21 And he said, All these things have I observed from my youth up.
LUK 18:22 22 And when Jesus heard it, he said unto him, One thing thou lackest yet: sell all that thou hast, and distribute unto the poor, and thou shalt have treasure in heaven: and come, follow me.
LUK 18:23 23 But when he heard these things, he became exceeding sorrowful; for he was very rich.
LUK 18:24 24 And Jesus seeing him said, How hardly shall they that have riches enter into the kingdom of God!
LUK 18:25 25 For it is easier for a camel to enter in through a needle’s eye, than for a rich man to enter into the kingdom of God.
LUK 18:26 26 And they that heard it said, Then who can be saved?
LUK 18:27 27 But he said, The things which are impossible with men are possible with God.
LUK 18:28 28 And Peter said, Lo, we have left our own, and followed thee.
LUK 18:29 29 And he said unto them, Verily I say unto you, There is no man that hath left house, or wife, or brethren, or parents, or children, for the kingdom of God’s sake,
LUK 18:30 30 who shall not receive manifold more in this time, and in the world to come eternal life.
LUK 18:31 31 And he took unto him the twelve, and said unto them, Behold, we go up to Jerusalem, and all the things that are written through the prophets shall be accomplished unto the Son of man.
LUK 18:32 32 For he shall be delivered up unto the Gentiles, and shall be mocked, and shamefully treated, and spit upon:
LUK 18:33 33 and they shall scourge and kill him: and the third day he shall rise again.
LUK 18:34 34 And they understood none of these things; and this saying was hid from them, and they perceived not the things that were said.
LUK 18:35 35 And it came to pass, as he drew nigh unto Jericho, a certain blind man sat by the way side begging:
LUK 18:36 36 and hearing a multitude going by, he inquired what this meant.
LUK 18:37 37 And they told him, that Jesus of Nazareth passeth by.
LUK 18:38 38 And he cried, saying, Jesus, thou son of David, have mercy on me.
LUK 18:39 39 And they that went before rebuked him, that he should hold his peace: but he cried out the more a great deal, Thou son of David, have mercy on me.
LUK 18:40 40 And Jesus stood, and commanded him to be brought unto him: and when he was come near, he asked him,
LUK 18:41 41 What wilt thou that I should do unto thee? And he said, Lord, that I may receive my sight.
LUK 18:42 42 And Jesus said unto him, Receive thy sight: thy faith hath made thee whole.
LUK 18:43 43 And immediately he received his sight, and followed him, glorifying God: and all the people, when they saw it, gave praise unto God.
LUK 19:1 1 And he entered and was passing through Jericho.
LUK 19:2 2 And behold, a man called by name Zacchæus; and he was a chief publican, and he was rich.
LUK 19:3 3 And he sought to see Jesus who he was; and could not for the crowd, because he was little of stature.
LUK 19:4 4 And he ran on before, and climbed up into a sycomore tree to see him: for he was to pass that way.
LUK 19:5 5 And when Jesus came to the place, he looked up, and said unto him, Zacchæus, make haste, and come down; for to-day I must abide at thy house.
LUK 19:6 6 And he made haste, and came down, and received him joyfully.
LUK 19:7 7 And when they saw it, they all murmured, saying, He is gone in to lodge with a man that is a sinner.
LUK 19:8 8 And Zacchæus stood, and said unto the Lord, Behold, Lord, the half of my goods I give to the poor; and if I have wrongfully exacted aught of any man, I restore fourfold.
LUK 19:9 9 And Jesus said unto him, To-day is salvation come to this house, forasmuch as he also is a son of Abraham.
LUK 19:10 10 For the Son of man came to seek and to save that which was lost.
LUK 19:11 11 And as they heard these things, he added and spake a parable, because he was nigh to Jerusalem, and because they supposed that the kingdom of God was immediately to appear.
LUK 19:12 12 He said therefore, A certain nobleman went into a far country, to receive for himself a kingdom, and to return.
LUK 19:13 13 And he called ten servants of his, and gave them ten pounds, and said unto them, Trade ye herewith till I come.
LUK 19:14 14 But his citizens hated him, and sent an ambassage after him, saying, We will not that this man reign over us.
LUK 19:15 15 And it came to pass, when he was come back again, having received the kingdom, that he commanded these servants, unto whom he had given the money, to be called to him, that he might know what they had gained by trading.
LUK 19:16 16 And the first came before him, saying, Lord, thy pound hath made ten pounds more.
LUK 19:17 17 And he said unto him, Well done, thou good servant: because thou wast found faithful in a very little, have thou authority over ten cities.
LUK 19:18 18 And the second came, saying, Thy pound, Lord, hath made five pounds.
LUK 19:19 19 And he said unto him also, Be thou also over five cities.
LUK 19:20 20 And another came, saying, Lord, behold, here is thy pound, which I kept laid up in a napkin:
LUK 19:21 21 for I feared thee, because thou art an austere man: thou takest up that which thou layedst not down, and reapest that which thou didst not sow.
LUK 19:22 22 He saith unto him, Out of thine own mouth will I judge thee, thou wicked servant. Thou knewest that I am an austere man, taking up that which I laid not down, and reaping that which I did not sow;
LUK 19:23 23 then wherefore gavest thou not my money into the bank, and I at my coming should have required it with interest?
LUK 19:24 24 And he said unto them that stood by, Take away from him the pound, and give it unto him that hath the ten pounds.
LUK 19:25 25 And they said unto him, Lord, he hath ten pounds.
LUK 19:26 26 I say unto you, that unto every one that hath shall be given; but from him that hath not, even that which he hath shall be taken away from him.
LUK 19:27 27 But these mine enemies, that would not that I should reign over them, bring hither, and slay them before me.
LUK 19:28 28 And when he had thus spoken, he went on before, going up to Jerusalem.
LUK 19:29 29 And it came to pass, when he drew nigh unto Bethphage and Bethany, at the mount that is called Olivet, he sent two of the disciples,
LUK 19:30 30 saying, Go your way into the village over against you ; in which as ye enter ye shall find a colt tied, whereon no man ever yet sat: loose him, and bring him.
LUK 19:31 31 And if any one ask you, Why do ye loose him? thus shall ye say, The Lord hath need of him.
LUK 19:32 32 And they that were sent went away, and found even as he had said unto them.
LUK 19:33 33 And as they were loosing the colt, the owners thereof said unto them, Why loose ye the colt?
LUK 19:34 34 And they said, The Lord hath need of him.
LUK 19:35 35 And they brought him to Jesus: and they threw their garments upon the colt, and set Jesus thereon.
LUK 19:36 36 And as he went, they spread their garments in the way.
LUK 19:37 37 And as he was now drawing nigh, even at the descent of the mount of Olives, the whole multitude of the disciples began to rejoice and praise God with a loud voice for all the mighty works which they had seen;
LUK 19:38 38 saying, Blessed is the King that cometh in the name of the Lord: peace in heaven, and glory in the highest.
LUK 19:39 39 And some of the Pharisees from the multitude said unto him, Teacher, rebuke thy disciples.
LUK 19:40 40 And he answered and said, I tell you that, if these shall hold their peace, the stones will cry out.
LUK 19:41 41 And when he drew nigh, he saw the city and wept over it,
LUK 19:42 42 saying, If thou hadst known in this day, even thou, the things which belong unto peace! but now they are hid from thine eyes.
LUK 19:43 43 For the days shall come upon thee, when thine enemies shall cast up a bank about thee, and compass thee round, and keep thee in on every side,
LUK 19:44 44 and shall dash thee to the ground, and thy children within thee; and they shall not leave in thee one stone upon another; because thou knewest not the time of thy visitation.
LUK 19:45 45 And he entered into the temple, and began to cast out them that sold,
LUK 19:46 46 saying unto them, It is written, And my house shall be a house of prayer: but ye have made it a den of robbers.
LUK 19:47 47 And he was teaching daily in the temple. But the chief priests and the scribes and the principal men of the people sought to destroy him:
LUK 19:48 48 and they could not find what they might do; for the people all hung upon him, listening.
LUK 20:1 1 And it came to pass, on one of the days, as he was teaching the people in the temple, and preaching the gospel, there came upon him the chief priests and the scribes with the elders;
LUK 20:2 2 and they spake, saying unto him, Tell us: By what authority doest thou these things? or who is he that gave thee this authority?
LUK 20:3 3 And he answered and said unto them, I also will ask you a question; and tell me:
LUK 20:4 4 The baptism of John, was it from heaven, or from men?
LUK 20:5 5 And they reasoned with themselves, saying, If we shall say, From heaven; he will say, Why did ye not believe him?
LUK 20:6 6 But if we shall say, From men; all the people will stone us: for they are persuaded that John was a prophet.
LUK 20:7 7 And they answered, that they knew not whence it was .
LUK 20:8 8 And Jesus said unto them, Neither tell I you by what authority I do these things.
LUK 20:9 9 And he began to speak unto the people this parable: A man planted a vineyard, and let it out to husbandmen, and went into another country for a long time.
LUK 20:10 10 And at the season he sent unto the husbandmen a servant, that they should give him of the fruit of the vineyard: but the husbandmen beat him, and sent him away empty.
LUK 20:11 11 And he sent yet another servant: and him also they beat, and handled him shamefully, and sent him away empty.
LUK 20:12 12 And he sent yet a third: and him also they wounded, and cast him forth.
LUK 20:13 13 And the lord of the vineyard said, What shall I do? I will send my beloved son; it may be they will reverence him.
LUK 20:14 14 But when the husbandmen saw him, they reasoned one with another, saying, This is the heir; let us kill him, that the inheritance may be ours.
LUK 20:15 15 And they cast him forth out of the vineyard, and killed him. What therefore will the lord of the vineyard do unto them?
LUK 20:16 16 He will come and destroy these husbandmen, and will give the vineyard unto others. And when they heard it, they said, God forbid.
LUK 20:17 17 But he looked upon them, and said, What then is this that is written,
LUK 20:18 The stone which the builders rejected,
LUK 20:19 The same was made the head of the corner?
LUK 20:20 18 Every one that falleth on that stone shall be broken to pieces; but on whomsoever it shall fall, it will scatter him as dust.
LUK 20:21 19 And the scribes and the chief priests sought to lay hands on him in that very hour; and they feared the people: for they perceived that he spake this parable against them.
LUK 20:22 20 And they watched him, and sent forth spies, who feigned themselves to be righteous, that they might take hold of his speech, so as to deliver him up to the rule and to the authority of the governor.
LUK 20:23 21 And they asked him, saying, Teacher, we know that thou sayest and teachest rightly, and acceptest not the person of any , but of a truth teachest the way of God:
LUK 20:24 22 Is it lawful for us to give tribute unto Cæsar, or not?
LUK 20:25 23 But he perceived their craftiness, and said unto them,
LUK 20:26 24 Show me a denarius. Whose image and superscription hath it? And they said, Cæsar’s.
LUK 20:27 25 And he said unto them, Then render unto Cæsar the things that are Cæsar’s, and unto God the things that are God’s.
LUK 20:28 26 And they were not able to take hold of the saying before the people: and they marvelled at his answer, and held their peace.
LUK 20:29 27 And there came to him certain of the Sadducees, they that say that there is no resurrection;
LUK 20:30 28 and they asked him, saying, Teacher, Moses wrote unto us, that if a man’s brother die, having a wife, and he be childless, his brother should take the wife, and raise up seed unto his brother.
LUK 20:31 29 There were therefore seven brethren: and the first took a wife, and died childless;
LUK 20:32 30 and the second:
LUK 20:33 31 and the third took her; and likewise the seven also left no children, and died.
LUK 20:34 32 Afterward the woman also died.
LUK 20:35 33 In the resurrection therefore whose wife of them shall she be? for the seven had her to wife.
LUK 20:36 34 And Jesus said unto them, The sons of this world marry, and are given in marriage:
LUK 20:37 35 but they that are accounted worthy to attain to that world, and the resurrection from the dead, neither marry, nor are given in marriage:
LUK 20:38 36 for neither can they die any more: for they are equal unto the angels; and are sons of God, being sons of the resurrection.
LUK 20:39 37 But that the dead are raised, even Moses showed, in the place concerning the Bush, when he calleth the Lord the God of Abraham, and the God of Isaac, and the God of Jacob.
LUK 20:40 38 Now he is not the God of the dead, but of the living: for all live unto him.
LUK 20:41 39 And certain of the scribes answering said, Teacher, thou hast well said.
LUK 20:42 40 For they durst not any more ask him any question.
LUK 20:43 41 And he said unto them, How say they that the Christ is David’s son?
LUK 20:44 42 For David himself saith in the book of Psalms,
LUK 20:45 The Lord said unto my Lord,
LUK 20:46 Sit thou on my right hand,
LUK 20:47 43 Till I make thine enemies the footstool of thy feet.
LUK 20:48 44 David therefore calleth him Lord, and how is he his son?
LUK 20:49 45 And in the hearing of all the people he said unto his disciples,
LUK 20:50 46 Beware of the scribes, who desire to walk in long robes, and love salutations in the marketplaces, and chief seats in the synagogues, and chief places at feasts;
LUK 20:51 47 who devour widows’ houses, and for a pretence make long prayers: these shall receive greater condemnation.
LUK 21:1 1 And he looked up, and saw the rich men that were casting their gifts into the treasury.
LUK 21:2 2 And he saw a certain poor widow casting in thither two mites.
LUK 21:3 3 And he said, Of a truth I say unto you, This poor widow cast in more than they all:
LUK 21:4 4 for all these did of their superfluity cast in unto the gifts; but she of her want did cast in all the living that she had.
LUK 21:5 5 And as some spake of the temple, how it was adorned with goodly stones and offerings, he said,
LUK 21:6 6 As for these things which ye behold, the days will come, in which there shall not be left here one stone upon another, that shall not be thrown down.
LUK 21:7 7 And they asked him, saying, Teacher, when therefore shall these things be? and what shall be the sign when these things are about to come to pass?
LUK 21:8 8 And he said, Take heed that ye be not led astray: for many shall come in my name, saying, I am he ; and, The time is at hand: go ye not after them.
LUK 21:9 9 And when ye shall hear of wars and tumults, be not terrified: for these things must needs come to pass first; but the end is not immediately.
LUK 21:10 10 Then said he unto them, Nation shall rise against nation, and kingdom against kingdom;
LUK 21:11 11 and there shall be great earthquakes, and in divers places famines and pestilences; and there shall be terrors and great signs from heaven.
LUK 21:12 12 But before all these things, they shall lay their hands on you, and shall persecute you, delivering you up to the synagogues and prisons, bringing you before kings and governors for my name’s sake.
LUK 21:13 13 It shall turn out unto you for a testimony.
LUK 21:14 14 Settle it therefore in your hearts, not to meditate beforehand how to answer:
LUK 21:15 15 for I will give you a mouth and wisdom, which all your adversaries shall not be able to withstand or to gainsay.
LUK 21:16 16 But ye shall be delivered up even by parents, and brethren, and kinsfolk, and friends; and some of you shall they cause to be put to death.
LUK 21:17 17 And ye shall be hated of all men for my name’s sake.
LUK 21:18 18 And not a hair of your head shall perish.
LUK 21:19 19 In your patience ye shall win your souls.
LUK 21:20 20 But when ye see Jerusalem compassed with armies, then know that her desolation is at hand.
LUK 21:21 21 Then let them that are in Judæa flee unto the mountains; and let them that are in the midst of her depart out; and let not them that are in the country enter therein.
LUK 21:22 22 For these are days of vengeance, that all things which are written may be fulfilled.
LUK 21:23 23 Woe unto them that are with child and to them that give suck in those days! for there shall be great distress upon the land, and wrath unto this people.
LUK 21:24 24 And they shall fall by the edge of the sword, and shall be led captive into all the nations: and Jerusalem shall be trodden down of the Gentiles, until the times of the Gentiles be fulfilled.
LUK 21:25 25 And there shall be signs in sun and moon and stars; and upon the earth distress of nations, in perplexity for the roaring of the sea and the billows;
LUK 21:26 26 men fainting for fear, and for expectation of the things which are coming on the world: for the powers of the heavens shall be shaken.
LUK 21:27 27 And then shall they see the Son of man coming in a cloud with power and great glory.
LUK 21:28 28 But when these things begin to come to pass, look up, and lift up your heads; because your redemption draweth nigh.
LUK 21:29 29 And he spake to them a parable: Behold the fig tree, and all the trees:
LUK 21:30 30 when they now shoot forth, ye see it and know of your own selves that the summer is now nigh.
LUK 21:31 31 Even so ye also, when ye see these things coming to pass, know ye that the kingdom of God is nigh.
LUK 21:32 32 Verily I say unto you, This generation shall not pass away, till all things be accomplished.
LUK 21:33 33 Heaven and earth shall pass away: but my words shall not pass away.
LUK 21:34 34 But take heed to yourselves, lest haply your hearts be overcharged with surfeiting, and drunkenness, and cares of this life, and that day come on you suddenly as a snare:
LUK 21:35 35 for so shall it come upon all them that dwell on the face of all the earth.
LUK 21:36 36 But watch ye at every season, making supplication, that ye may prevail to escape all these things that shall come to pass, and to stand before the Son of man.
LUK 21:37 37 And every day he was teaching in the temple; and every night he went out, and lodged in the mount that is called Olivet.
LUK 21:38 38 And all the people came early in the morning to him in the temple, to hear him.
LUK 22:1 1 Now the feast of unleavened bread drew nigh, which is called the Passover.
LUK 22:2 2 And the chief priests and the scribes sought how they might put him to death; for they feared the people.
LUK 22:3 3 And Satan entered into Judas who was called Iscariot, being of the number of the twelve.
LUK 22:4 4 And he went away, and communed with the chief priests and captains, how he might deliver him unto them.
LUK 22:5 5 And they were glad, and covenanted to give him money.
LUK 22:6 6 And he consented, and sought opportunity to deliver him unto them in the absence of the multitude.
LUK 22:7 7 And the day of unleavened bread came, on which the passover must be sacrificed.
LUK 22:8 8 And he sent Peter and John, saying, Go and make ready for us the passover, that we may eat.
LUK 22:9 9 And they said unto him, Where wilt thou that we make ready?
LUK 22:10 10 And he said unto them, Behold, when ye are entered into the city, there shall meet you a man bearing a pitcher of water; follow him into the house whereinto he goeth.
LUK 22:11 11 And ye shall say unto the master of the house, The Teacher saith unto thee, Where is the guest-chamber, where I shall eat the passover with my disciples?
LUK 22:12 12 And he will show you a large upper room furnished: there make ready.
LUK 22:13 13 And they went, and found as he had said unto them: and they made ready the passover.
LUK 22:14 14 And when the hour was come, he sat down, and the apostles with him.
LUK 22:15 15 And he said unto them, With desire I have desired to eat this passover with you before I suffer:
LUK 22:16 16 for I say unto you, I shall not eat it, until it be fulfilled in the kingdom of God.
LUK 22:17 17 And he received a cup, and when he had given thanks, he said, Take this, and divide it among yourselves:
LUK 22:18 18 for I say unto you, I shall not drink from henceforth of the fruit of the vine, until the kingdom of God shall come.
LUK 22:19 19 And he took bread, and when he had given thanks, he brake it, and gave to them, saying, This is my body which is given for you: this do in remembrance of me.
LUK 22:20 20 And the cup in like manner after supper, saying, This cup is the new covenant in my blood, even that which is poured out for you.
LUK 22:21 21 But behold, the hand of him that betrayeth me is with me on the table.
LUK 22:22 22 For the Son of man indeed goeth, as it hath been determined: but woe unto that man through whom he is betrayed!
LUK 22:23 23 And they began to question among themselves, which of them it was that should do this thing.
LUK 22:24 24 And there arose also a contention among them, which of them was accounted to be greatest.
LUK 22:25 25 And he said unto them, The kings of the Gentiles have lordship over them; and they that have authority over them are called Benefactors.
LUK 22:26 26 But ye shall not be so: but he that is the greater among you, let him become as the younger; and he that is chief, as he that doth serve.
LUK 22:27 27 For which is greater, he that sitteth at meat, or he that serveth? is not he that sitteth at meat? but I am in the midst of you as he that serveth.
LUK 22:28 28 But ye are they that have continued with me in my temptations;
LUK 22:29 29 and I appoint unto you a kingdom, even as my Father appointed unto me,
LUK 22:30 30 that ye may eat and drink at my table in my kingdom; and ye shall sit on thrones judging the twelve tribes of Israel.
LUK 22:31 31 Simon, Simon, behold, Satan asked to have you, that he might sift you as wheat:
LUK 22:32 32 but I made supplication for thee, that thy faith fail not; and do thou, when once thou hast turned again, establish thy brethren.
LUK 22:33 33 And he said unto him, Lord, with thee I am ready to go both to prison and to death.
LUK 22:34 34 And he said, I tell thee, Peter, the cock shall not crow this day, until thou shalt thrice deny that thou knowest me.
LUK 22:35 35 And he said unto them, When I sent you forth without purse, and wallet, and shoes, lacked ye anything? And they said, Nothing.
LUK 22:36 36 And he said unto them, But now, he that hath a purse, let him take it, and likewise a wallet; and he that hath none, let him sell his cloak, and buy a sword.
LUK 22:37 37 For I say unto you, that this which is written must be fulfilled in me, And he was reckoned with transgressors: for that which concerneth me hath fulfilment.
LUK 22:38 38 And they said, Lord, behold, here are two swords. And he said unto them, It is enough.
LUK 22:39 39 And he came out, and went, as his custom was, unto the mount of Olives; and the disciples also followed him.
LUK 22:40 40 And when he was at the place, he said unto them, Pray that ye enter not into temptation.
LUK 22:41 41 And he was parted from them about a stone’s cast; and he kneeled down and prayed,
LUK 22:42 42 saying, Father, if thou be willing, remove this cup from me: nevertheless not my will, but thine, be done.
LUK 22:43 43 And there appeared unto him an angel from heaven, strengthening him.
LUK 22:44 44 And being in an agony he prayed more earnestly; and his sweat became as it were great drops of blood falling down upon the ground.
LUK 22:45 45 And when he rose up from his prayer, he came unto the disciples, and found them sleeping for sorrow,
LUK 22:46 46 and said unto them, Why sleep ye? rise and pray, that ye enter not into temptation.
LUK 22:47 47 While he yet spake, behold, a multitude, and he that was called Judas, one of the twelve, went before them; and he drew near unto Jesus to kiss him.
LUK 22:48 48 But Jesus said unto him, Judas, betrayest thou the Son of man with a kiss?
LUK 22:49 49 And when they that were about him saw what would follow, they said, Lord, shall we smite with the sword?
LUK 22:50 50 And a certain one of them smote the servant of the high priest, and struck off his right ear.
LUK 22:51 51 But Jesus answered and said, Suffer ye them thus far. And he touched his ear, and healed him.
LUK 22:52 52 And Jesus said unto the chief priests, and captains of the temple, and elders, that were come against him, Are ye come out, as against a robber, with swords and staves?
LUK 22:53 53 When I was daily with you in the temple, ye stretched not forth your hands against me: but this is your hour, and the power of darkness.
LUK 22:54 54 And they seized him, and led him away , and brought him into the high priest’s house. But Peter followed afar off.
LUK 22:55 55 And when they had kindled a fire in the midst of the court, and had sat down together, Peter sat in the midst of them.
LUK 22:56 56 And a certain maid seeing him as he sat in the light of the fire , and looking stedfastly upon him, said, This man also was with him.
LUK 22:57 57 But he denied, saying, Woman, I know him not.
LUK 22:58 58 And after a little while another saw him, and said, Thou also art one of them. But Peter said, Man, I am not.
LUK 22:59 59 And after the space of about one hour another confidently affirmed, saying, Of a truth this man also was with him; for he is a Galilæan.
LUK 22:60 60 But Peter said, Man, I know not what thou sayest. And immediately, while he yet spake, the cock crew.
LUK 22:61 61 And the Lord turned, and looked upon Peter. And Peter remembered the word of the Lord, how that he said unto him, Before the cock crow this day thou shalt deny me thrice.
LUK 22:62 62 And he went out, and wept bitterly.
LUK 22:63 63 And the men that held Jesus mocked him, and beat him.
LUK 22:64 64 And they blindfolded him, and asked him, saying, Prophesy: who is he that struck thee?
LUK 22:65 65 And many other things spake they against him, reviling him.
LUK 22:66 66 And as soon as it was day, the assembly of the elders of the people was gathered together, both chief priests and scribes; and they led him away into their council, saying,
LUK 22:67 67 If thou art the Christ, tell us. But he said unto them, If I tell you, ye will not believe:
LUK 22:68 68 and if I ask you , ye will not answer.
LUK 22:69 69 But from henceforth shall the Son of man be seated at the right hand of the power of God.
LUK 22:70 70 And they all said, Art thou then the Son of God? And he said unto them, Ye say that I am.
LUK 22:71 71 And they said, What further need have we of witness? for we ourselves have heard from his own mouth.
LUK 23:1 1 And the whole company of them rose up, and brought him before Pilate.
LUK 23:2 2 And they began to accuse him, saying, We found this man perverting our nation, and forbidding to give tribute to Cæsar, and saying that he himself is Christ a king.
LUK 23:3 3 And Pilate asked him, saying, Art thou the King of the Jews? And he answered him and said, Thou sayest.
LUK 23:4 4 And Pilate said unto the chief priests and the multitudes, I find no fault in this man.
LUK 23:5 5 But they were the more urgent, saying, He stirreth up the people, teaching throughout all Judæa, and beginning from Galilee even unto this place.
LUK 23:6 6 But when Pilate heard it, he asked whether the man were a Galilæan.
LUK 23:7 7 And when he knew that he was of Herod’s jurisdiction, he sent him unto Herod, who himself also was at Jerusalem in these days.
LUK 23:8 8 Now when Herod saw Jesus, he was exceeding glad: for he was of a long time desirous to see him, because he had heard concerning him; and he hoped to see some miracle done by him.
LUK 23:9 9 And he questioned him in many words; but he answered him nothing.
LUK 23:10 10 And the chief priests and the scribes stood, vehemently accusing him.
LUK 23:11 11 And Herod with his soldiers set him at nought, and mocked him, and arraying him in gorgeous apparel sent him back to Pilate.
LUK 23:12 12 And Herod and Pilate became friends with each other that very day: for before they were at enmity between themselves.
LUK 23:13 13 And Pilate called together the chief priests and the rulers and the people,
LUK 23:14 14 and said unto them, Ye brought unto me this man, as one that perverteth the people: and behold, I, having examined him before you, found no fault in this man touching those things whereof ye accuse him:
LUK 23:15 15 no, nor yet Herod: for he sent him back unto us; and behold, nothing worthy of death hath been done by him.
LUK 23:16 16 I will therefore chastise him, and release him.
LUK 23:17 17
LUK 23:18 18 But they cried out all together, saying, Away with this man, and release unto us Barabbas:—
LUK 23:19 19 one who for a certain insurrection made in the city, and for murder, was cast into prison.
LUK 23:20 20 And Pilate spake unto them again, desiring to release Jesus;
LUK 23:21 21 but they shouted, saying, Crucify, crucify him.
LUK 23:22 22 And he said unto them the third time, Why, what evil hath this man done? I have found no cause of death in him: I will therefore chastise him and release him.
LUK 23:23 23 But they were urgent with loud voices, asking that he might be crucified. And their voices prevailed.
LUK 23:24 24 And Pilate gave sentence that what they asked for should be done.
LUK 23:25 25 And he released him that for insurrection and murder had been cast into prison, whom they asked for; but Jesus he delivered up to their will.
LUK 23:26 26 And when they led him away, they laid hold upon one Simon of Cyrene, coming from the country, and laid on him the cross, to bear it after Jesus.
LUK 23:27 27 And there followed him a great multitude of the people, and of women who bewailed and lamented him.
LUK 23:28 28 But Jesus turning unto them said, Daughters of Jerusalem, weep not for me, but weep for yourselves, and for your children.
LUK 23:29 29 For behold, the days are coming, in which they shall say, Blessed are the barren, and the wombs that never bare, and the breasts that never gave suck.
LUK 23:30 30 Then shall they begin to say to the mountains, Fall on us; and to the hills, Cover us.
LUK 23:31 31 For if they do these things in the green tree, what shall be done in the dry?
LUK 23:32 32 And there were also two others, malefactors, led with him to be put to death.
LUK 23:33 33 And when they came unto the place which is called The skull, there they crucified him, and the malefactors, one on the right hand and the other on the left.
LUK 23:34 34 And Jesus said, Father, forgive them; for they know not what they do. And parting his garments among them, they cast lots.
LUK 23:35 35 And the people stood beholding. And the rulers also scoffed at him, saying, He saved others; let him save himself, if this is the Christ of God, his chosen.
LUK 23:36 36 And the soldiers also mocked him, coming to him, offering him vinegar,
LUK 23:37 37 and saying, If thou art the King of the Jews, save thyself.
LUK 23:38 38 And there was also a superscription over him, THIS IS THE KING OF THE JEWS.
LUK 23:39 39 And one of the malefactors that were hanged railed on him, saying, Art not thou the Christ? save thyself and us.
LUK 23:40 40 But the other answered, and rebuking him said, Dost thou not even fear God, seeing thou art in the same condemnation?
LUK 23:41 41 And we indeed justly; for we receive the due reward of our deeds: but this man hath done nothing amiss.
LUK 23:42 42 And he said, Jesus, remember me when thou comest in thy kingdom.
LUK 23:43 43 And he said unto him, Verily I say unto thee, To-day shalt thou be with me in Paradise.
LUK 23:44 44 And it was now about the sixth hour, and a darkness came over the whole land until the ninth hour,
LUK 23:45 45 the sun’s light failing: and the veil of the temple was rent in the midst.
LUK 23:46 46 And Jesus, crying with a loud voice, said, Father, into thy hands I commend my spirit: and having said this, he gave up the ghost.
LUK 23:47 47 And when the centurion saw what was done, he glorified God, saying, Certainly this was a righteous man.
LUK 23:48 48 And all the multitudes that came together to this sight, when they beheld the things that were done, returned smiting their breasts.
LUK 23:49 49 And all his acquaintance, and the women that followed with him from Galilee, stood afar off, seeing these things.
LUK 23:50 50 And behold, a man named Joseph, who was a councillor, a good and righteous man
LUK 23:51 51 (he had not consented to their counsel and deed), a man of Arimathæa, a city of the Jews, who was looking for the kingdom of God:
LUK 23:52 52 this man went to Pilate, and asked for the body of Jesus.
LUK 23:53 53 And he took it down, and wrapped it in a linen cloth, and laid him in a tomb that was hewn in stone, where never man had yet lain.
LUK 23:54 54 And it was the day of the Preparation, and the sabbath drew on.
LUK 23:55 55 And the women, who had come with him out of Galilee, followed after, and beheld the tomb, and how his body was laid.
LUK 23:56 56 And they returned, and prepared spices and ointments.
LUK 23:57 And on the sabbath they rested according to the commandment.
LUK 24:1 1 But on the first day of the week, at early dawn, they came unto the tomb, bringing the spices which they had prepared.
LUK 24:2 2 And they found the stone rolled away from the tomb.
LUK 24:3 3 And they entered in, and found not the body of the Lord Jesus.
LUK 24:4 4 And it came to pass, while they were perplexed thereabout, behold, two men stood by them in dazzling apparel:
LUK 24:5 5 and as they were affrighted and bowed down their faces to the earth, they said unto them, Why seek ye the living among the dead?
LUK 24:6 6 He is not here, but is risen: remember how he spake unto you when he was yet in Galilee,
LUK 24:7 7 saying that the Son of man must be delivered up into the hands of sinful men, and be crucified, and the third day rise again.
LUK 24:8 8 And they remembered his words,
LUK 24:9 9 and returned from the tomb, and told all these things to the eleven, and to all the rest.
LUK 24:10 10 Now they were Mary Magdalene, and Joanna, and Mary the mother of James: and the other women with them told these things unto the apostles.
LUK 24:11 11 And these words appeared in their sight as idle talk; and they disbelieved them.
LUK 24:12 12 But Peter arose, and ran unto the tomb; and stooping and looking in, he seeth the linen cloths by themselves; and he departed to his home, wondering at that which was come to pass.
LUK 24:13 13 And behold, two of them were going that very day to a village named Emmaus, which was threescore furlongs from Jerusalem.
LUK 24:14 14 And they communed with each other of all these things which had happened.
LUK 24:15 15 And it came to pass, while they communed and questioned together, that Jesus himself drew near, and went with them.
LUK 24:16 16 But their eyes were holden that they should not know him.
LUK 24:17 17 And he said unto them, What communications are these that ye have one with another, as ye walk? And they stood still, looking sad.
LUK 24:18 18 And one of them, named Cleopas, answering said unto him, Dost thou alone sojourn in Jerusalem and not know the things which are come to pass there in these days?
LUK 24:19 19 And he said unto them, What things? And they said unto him, The things concerning Jesus the Nazarene, who was a prophet mighty in deed and word before God and all the people:
LUK 24:20 20 and how the chief priests and our rulers delivered him up to be condemned to death, and crucified him.
LUK 24:21 21 But we hoped that it was he who should redeem Israel. Yea and besides all this, it is now the third day since these things came to pass.
LUK 24:22 22 Moreover certain women of our company amazed us, having been early at the tomb;
LUK 24:23 23 and when they found not his body, they came, saying, that they had also seen a vision of angels, who said that he was alive.
LUK 24:24 24 And certain of them that were with us went to the tomb, and found it even so as the women had said: but him they saw not.
LUK 24:25 25 And he said unto them, O foolish men, and slow of heart to believe in all that the prophets have spoken!
LUK 24:26 26 Behooved it not the Christ to suffer these things, and to enter into his glory?
LUK 24:27 27 And beginning from Moses and from all the prophets, he interpreted to them in all the scriptures the things concerning himself.
LUK 24:28 28 And they drew nigh unto the village, whither they were going: and he made as though he would go further.
LUK 24:29 29 And they constrained him, saying, Abide with us; for it is toward evening, and the day is now far spent. And he went in to abide with them.
LUK 24:30 30 And it came to pass, when he had sat down with them to meat, he took the bread and blessed; and breaking it he gave to them.
LUK 24:31 31 And their eyes were opened, and they knew him; and he vanished out of their sight.
LUK 24:32 32 And they said one to another, Was not our heart burning within us, while he spake to us in the way, while he opened to us the scriptures?
LUK 24:33 33 And they rose up that very hour, and returned to Jerusalem, and found the eleven gathered together, and them that were with them,
LUK 24:34 34 saying, The Lord is risen indeed, and hath appeared to Simon.
LUK 24:35 35 And they rehearsed the things that happened in the way, and how he was known of them in the breaking of the bread.
LUK 24:36 36 And as they spake these things, he himself stood in the midst of them, and saith unto them, Peace be unto you.
LUK 24:37 37 But they were terrified and affrighted, and supposed that they beheld a spirit.
LUK 24:38 38 And he said unto them, Why are ye troubled? and wherefore do questionings arise in your heart?
LUK 24:39 39 See my hands and my feet, that it is I myself: handle me, and see; for a spirit hath not flesh and bones, as ye behold me having.
LUK 24:40 40 And when he had said this, he showed them his hands and his feet.
LUK 24:41 41 And while they still disbelieved for joy, and wondered, he said unto them, Have ye here anything to eat?
LUK 24:42 42 And they gave him a piece of a broiled fish.
LUK 24:43 43 And he took it, and ate before them.
LUK 24:44 44 And he said unto them, These are my words which I spake unto you, while I was yet with you, that all things must needs be fulfilled, which are written in the law of Moses, and the prophets, and the psalms, concerning me.
LUK 24:45 45 Then opened he their mind, that they might understand the scriptures;
LUK 24:46 46 and he said unto them, Thus it is written, that the Christ should suffer, and rise again from the dead the third day;
LUK 24:47 47 and that repentance and remission of sins should be preached in his name unto all the nations, beginning from Jerusalem.
LUK 24:48 48 Ye are witnesses of these things.
LUK 24:49 49 And behold, I send forth the promise of my Father upon you: but tarry ye in the city, until ye be clothed with power from on high.
LUK 24:50 50 And he led them out until they were over against Bethany: and he lifted up his hands, and blessed them.
LUK 24:51 51 And it came to pass, while he blessed them, he parted from them, and was carried up into heaven.
LUK 24:52 52 And they worshipped him, and returned to Jerusalem with great joy:
LUK 24:53 53 and were continually in the temple, blessing God.
JHN 1:1 1 In the beginning was the Word, and the Word was with God, and the Word was God.
JHN 1:2 2 The same was in the beginning with God.
JHN 1:3 3 All things were made through him; and without him was not anything made that hath been made.
JHN 1:4 4 In him was life; and the life was the light of men.
JHN 1:5 5 And the light shineth in the darkness; and the darkness apprehended it not.
JHN 1:6 6 There came a man, sent from God, whose name was John.
JHN 1:7 7 The same came for witness, that he might bear witness of the light, that all might believe through him.
JHN 1:8 8 He was not the light, but came that he might bear witness of the light.
JHN 1:9 9 There was the true light, even the light which lighteth every man, coming into the world.
JHN 1:10 10 He was in the world, and the world was made through him, and the world knew him not.
JHN 1:11 11 He came unto his own, and they that were his own received him not.
JHN 1:12 12 But as many as received him, to them gave he the right to become children of God, even to them that believe on his name:
JHN 1:13 13 who were born, not of blood, nor of the will of the flesh, nor of the will of man, but of God.
JHN 1:14 14 And the Word became flesh, and dwelt among us (and we beheld his glory, glory as of the only begotten from the Father), full of grace and truth.
JHN 1:15 15 John beareth witness of him, and crieth, saying, This was he of whom I said, He that cometh after me is become before me: for he was before me.
JHN 1:16 16 For of his fulness we all received, and grace for grace.
JHN 1:17 17 For the law was given through Moses; grace and truth came through Jesus Christ.
JHN 1:18 18 No man hath seen God at any time; the only begotten Son, who is in the bosom of the Father, he hath declared him .
JHN 1:19 19 And this is the witness of John, when the Jews sent unto him from Jerusalem priests and Levites to ask him, Who art thou?
JHN 1:20 20 And he confessed, and denied not; and he confessed, I am not the Christ.
JHN 1:21 21 And they asked him, What then? Art thou Elijah? And he saith, I am not. Art thou the prophet? And he answered, No.
JHN 1:22 22 They said therefore unto him, Who art thou? that we may give an answer to them that sent us. What sayest thou of thyself?
JHN 1:23 23 He said, I am the voice of one crying in the wilderness, Make straight the way of the Lord, as said Isaiah the prophet.
JHN 1:24 24 And they had been sent from the Pharisees.
JHN 1:25 25 And they asked him, and said unto him, Why then baptizest thou, if thou art not the Christ, neither Elijah, neither the prophet?
JHN 1:26 26 John answered them, saying, I baptize in water: in the midst of you standeth one whom ye know not,
JHN 1:27 27 even he that cometh after me, the latchet of whose shoe I am not worthy to unloose.
JHN 1:28 28 These things were done in Bethany beyond the Jordan, where John was baptizing.
JHN 1:29 29 On the morrow he seeth Jesus coming unto him, and saith, Behold, the Lamb of God, that taketh away the sin of the world!
JHN 1:30 30 This is he of whom I said, After me cometh a man who is become before me: for he was before me.
JHN 1:31 31 And I knew him not; but that he should be made manifest to Israel, for this cause came I baptizing in water.
JHN 1:32 32 And John bare witness, saying, I have beheld the Spirit descending as a dove out of heaven; and it abode upon him.
JHN 1:33 33 And I knew him not: but he that sent me to baptize in water, he said unto me, Upon whomsoever thou shalt see the Spirit descending, and abiding upon him, the same is he that baptizeth in the Holy Spirit.
JHN 1:34 34 And I have seen, and have borne witness that this is the Son of God.
JHN 1:35 35 Again on the morrow John was standing, and two of his disciples;
JHN 1:36 36 and he looked upon Jesus as he walked, and saith, Behold, the Lamb of God!
JHN 1:37 37 And the two disciples heard him speak, and they followed Jesus.
JHN 1:38 38 And Jesus turned, and beheld them following, and saith unto them, What seek ye? And they said unto him, Rabbi (which is to say, being interpreted, Teacher), where abidest thou?
JHN 1:39 39 He saith unto them, Come, and ye shall see. They came therefore and saw where he abode; and they abode with him that day: it was about the tenth hour.
JHN 1:40 40 One of the two that heard John speak , and followed him, was Andrew, Simon Peter’s brother.
JHN 1:41 41 He findeth first his own brother Simon, and saith unto him, We have found the Messiah (which is, being interpreted, Christ).
JHN 1:42 42 He brought him unto Jesus. Jesus looked upon him, and said, Thou art Simon the son of John: thou shalt be called Cephas (which is by interpretation, Peter).
JHN 1:43 43 On the morrow he was minded to go forth into Galilee, and he findeth Philip: and Jesus saith unto him, Follow me.
JHN 1:44 44 Now Philip was from Bethsaida, of the city of Andrew and Peter.
JHN 1:45 45 Philip findeth Nathanael, and saith unto him, We have found him, of whom Moses in the law, and the prophets, wrote, Jesus of Nazareth, the son of Joseph.
JHN 1:46 46 And Nathanael said unto him, Can any good thing come out of Nazareth? Philip saith unto him, Come and see.
JHN 1:47 47 Jesus saw Nathanael coming to him, and saith of him, Behold, an Israelite indeed, in whom is no guile!
JHN 1:48 48 Nathanael saith unto him, Whence knowest thou me? Jesus answered and said unto him, Before Philip called thee, when thou wast under the fig tree, I saw thee.
JHN 1:49 49 Nathanael answered him, Rabbi, thou art the Son of God; thou art King of Israel.
JHN 1:50 50 Jesus answered and said unto him, Because I said unto thee, I saw thee underneath the fig tree, believest thou? thou shalt see greater things than these.
JHN 1:51 51 And he saith unto him, Verily, verily, I say unto you, Ye shall see the heaven opened, and the angels of God ascending and descending upon the Son of man.
JHN 2:1 1 And the third day there was a marriage in Cana of Galilee; and the mother of Jesus was there:
JHN 2:2 2 and Jesus also was bidden, and his disciples, to the marriage.
JHN 2:3 3 And when the wine failed, the mother of Jesus saith unto him, They have no wine.
JHN 2:4 4 And Jesus saith unto her, Woman, what have I to do with thee? mine hour is not yet come.
JHN 2:5 5 His mother saith unto the servants, Whatsoever he saith unto you, do it.
JHN 2:6 6 Now there were six waterpots of stone set there after the Jews’ manner of purifying, containing two or three firkins apiece.
JHN 2:7 7 Jesus saith unto them, Fill the waterpots with water. And they filled them up to the brim.
JHN 2:8 8 And he saith unto them, Draw out now, and bear unto the ruler of the feast. And they bare it.
JHN 2:9 9 And when the ruler of the feast tasted the water now become wine, and knew not whence it was (but the servants that had drawn the water knew), the ruler of the feast calleth the bridegroom,
JHN 2:10 10 and saith unto him, Every man setteth on first the good wine; and when men have drunk freely, then that which is worse: thou hast kept the good wine until now.
JHN 2:11 11 This beginning of his signs did Jesus in Cana of Galilee, and manifested his glory; and his disciples believed on him.
JHN 2:12 12 After this he went down to Capernaum, he, and his mother, and his brethren, and his disciples; and there they abode not many days.
JHN 2:13 13 And the passover of the Jews was at hand, and Jesus went up to Jerusalem.
JHN 2:14 14 And he found in the temple those that sold oxen and sheep and doves, and the changers of money sitting:
JHN 2:15 15 and he made a scourge of cords, and cast all out of the temple, both the sheep and the oxen; and he poured out the changers’ money, and overthrew their tables;
JHN 2:16 16 and to them that sold the doves he said, Take these things hence; make not my Father’s house a house of merchandise.
JHN 2:17 17 His disciples remembered that it was written, Zeal for thy house shall eat me up.
JHN 2:18 18 The Jews therefore answered and said unto him, What sign showest thou unto us, seeing that thou doest these things?
JHN 2:19 19 Jesus answered and said unto them, Destroy this temple, and in three days I will raise it up.
JHN 2:20 20 The Jews therefore said, Forty and six years was this temple in building, and wilt thou raise it up in three days?
JHN 2:21 21 But he spake of the temple of his body.
JHN 2:22 22 When therefore he was raised from the dead, his disciples remembered that he spake this; and they believed the scripture, and the word which Jesus had said.
JHN 2:23 23 Now when he was in Jerusalem at the passover, during the feast, many believed on his name, beholding his signs which he did.
JHN 2:24 24 But Jesus did not trust himself unto them, for that he knew all men,
JHN 2:25 25 and because he needed not that any one should bear witness concerning man; for he himself knew what was in man.
JHN 3:1 1 Now there was a man of the Pharisees, named Nicodemus, a ruler of the Jews:
JHN 3:2 2 the same came unto him by night, and said to him, Rabbi, we know that thou art a teacher come from God; for no one can do these signs that thou doest, except God be with him.
JHN 3:3 3 Jesus answered and said unto him, Verily, verily, I say unto thee, Except one be born anew, he cannot see the kingdom of God.
JHN 3:4 4 Nicodemus saith unto him, How can a man be born when he is old? can he enter a second time into his mother’s womb, and be born?
JHN 3:5 5 Jesus answered, Verily, verily, I say unto thee, Except one be born of water and the Spirit, he cannot enter into the kingdom of God.
JHN 3:6 6 That which is born of the flesh is flesh; and that which is born of the Spirit is spirit.
JHN 3:7 7 Marvel not that I said unto thee, Ye must be born anew.
JHN 3:8 8 The wind bloweth where it will, and thou hearest the voice thereof, but knowest not whence it cometh, and whither it goeth: so is every one that is born of the Spirit.
JHN 3:9 9 Nicodemus answered and said unto him, How can these things be?
JHN 3:10 10 Jesus answered and said unto him, Art thou the teacher of Israel, and understandest not these things?
JHN 3:11 11 Verily, verily, I say unto thee, We speak that which we know, and bear witness of that which we have seen; and ye receive not our witness.
JHN 3:12 12 If I told you earthly things and ye believe not, how shall ye believe if I tell you heavenly things?
JHN 3:13 13 And no one hath ascended into heaven, but he that descended out of heaven, even the Son of man, who is in heaven.
JHN 3:14 14 And as Moses lifted up the serpent in the wilderness, even so must the Son of man be lifted up;
JHN 3:15 15 that whosoever believeth may in him have eternal life.
JHN 3:16 16 For God so loved the world, that he gave his only begotten Son, that whosoever believeth on him should not perish, but have eternal life.
JHN 3:17 17 For God sent not the Son into the world to judge the world; but that the world should be saved through him.
JHN 3:18 18 He that believeth on him is not judged: he that believeth not hath been judged already, because he hath not believed on the name of the only begotten Son of God.
JHN 3:19 19 And this is the judgment, that the light is come into the world, and men loved the darkness rather than the light; for their works were evil.
JHN 3:20 20 For every one that doeth evil hateth the light, and cometh not to the light, lest his works should be reproved.
JHN 3:21 21 But he that doeth the truth cometh to the light, that his works may be made manifest, that they have been wrought in God.
JHN 3:22 22 After these things came Jesus and his disciples into the land of Judæa; and there he tarried with them, and baptized.
JHN 3:23 23 And John also was baptizing in Ænon near to Salim, because there was much water there: and they came, and were baptized.
JHN 3:24 24 For John was not yet cast into prison.
JHN 3:25 25 There arose therefore a questioning on the part of John’s disciples with a Jew about purifying.
JHN 3:26 26 And they came unto John, and said to him, Rabbi, he that was with thee beyond the Jordan, to whom thou hast borne witness, behold, the same baptizeth, and all men come to him.
JHN 3:27 27 John answered and said, A man can receive nothing, except it have been given him from heaven.
JHN 3:28 28 Ye yourselves bear me witness, that I said, I am not the Christ, but, that I am sent before him.
JHN 3:29 29 He that hath the bride is the bridegroom: but the friend of the bridegroom, that standeth and heareth him, rejoiceth greatly because of the bridegroom’s voice: this my joy therefore is made full.
JHN 3:30 30 He must increase, but I must decrease.
JHN 3:31 31 He that cometh from above is above all: he that is of the earth is of the earth, and of the earth he speaketh: he that cometh from heaven is above all.
JHN 3:32 32 What he hath seen and heard, of that he beareth witness; and no man receiveth his witness.
JHN 3:33 33 He that hath received his witness hath set his seal to this , that God is true.
JHN 3:34 34 For he whom God hath sent speaketh the words of God: for he giveth not the Spirit by measure.
JHN 3:35 35 The Father loveth the Son, and hath given all things into his hand.
JHN 3:36 36 He that believeth on the Son hath eternal life; but he that obeyeth not the Son shall not see life, but the wrath of God abideth on him.
JHN 4:1 1 When therefore the Lord knew that the Pharisees had heard that Jesus was making and baptizing more disciples than John
JHN 4:2 2 (although Jesus himself baptized not, but his disciples),
JHN 4:3 3 he left Judæa, and departed again into Galilee.
JHN 4:4 4 And he must needs pass through Samaria.
JHN 4:5 5 So he cometh to a city of Samaria, called Sychar, near to the parcel of ground that Jacob gave to his son Joseph:
JHN 4:6 6 and Jacob’s well was there. Jesus therefore, being wearied with his journey, sat thus by the well. It was about the sixth hour.
JHN 4:7 7 There cometh a woman of Samaria to draw water: Jesus saith unto her, Give me to drink.
JHN 4:8 8 For his disciples were gone away into the city to buy food.
JHN 4:9 9 The Samaritan woman therefore saith unto him, How is it that thou, being a Jew, askest drink of me, who am a Samaritan woman? (For Jews have no dealings with Samaritans.)
JHN 4:10 10 Jesus answered and said unto her, If thou knewest the gift of God, and who it is that saith to thee, Give me to drink; thou wouldest have asked of him, and he would have given thee living water.
JHN 4:11 11 The woman saith unto him, Sir, thou hast nothing to draw with, and the well is deep: whence then hast thou that living water?
JHN 4:12 12 Art thou greater than our father Jacob, who gave us the well, and drank thereof himself, and his sons, and his cattle?
JHN 4:13 13 Jesus answered and said unto her, Every one that drinketh of this water shall thirst again:
JHN 4:14 14 but whosoever drinketh of the water that I shall give him shall never thirst; but the water that I shall give him shall become in him a well of water springing up unto eternal life.
JHN 4:15 15 The woman saith unto him, Sir, give me this water, that I thirst not, neither come all the way hither to draw.
JHN 4:16 16 Jesus saith unto her, Go, call thy husband, and come hither.
JHN 4:17 17 The woman answered and said unto him, I have no husband. Jesus saith unto her, Thou saidst well, I have no husband:
JHN 4:18 18 for thou hast had five husbands; and he whom thou now hast is not thy husband: this hast thou said truly.
JHN 4:19 19 The woman saith unto him, Sir, I perceive that thou art a prophet.
JHN 4:20 20 Our fathers worshipped in this mountain; and ye say, that in Jerusalem is the place where men ought to worship.
JHN 4:21 21 Jesus saith unto her, Woman, believe me, the hour cometh, when neither in this mountain, nor in Jerusalem, shall ye worship the Father.
JHN 4:22 22 Ye worship that which ye know not: we worship that which we know; for salvation is from the Jews.
JHN 4:23 23 But the hour cometh, and now is, when the true worshippers shall worship the Father in spirit and truth: for such doth the Father seek to be his worshippers.
JHN 4:24 24 God is a Spirit: and they that worship him must worship in spirit and truth.
JHN 4:25 25 The woman saith unto him, I know that Messiah cometh (he that is called Christ): when he is come, he will declare unto us all things.
JHN 4:26 26 Jesus saith unto her, I that speak unto thee am he .
JHN 4:27 27 And upon this came his disciples; and they marvelled that he was speaking with a woman; yet no man said, What seekest thou? or, Why speakest thou with her?
JHN 4:28 28 So the woman left her waterpot, and went away into the city, and saith to the people,
JHN 4:29 29 Come, see a man, who told me all things that ever I did: can this be the Christ?
JHN 4:30 30 They went out of the city, and were coming to him.
JHN 4:31 31 In the mean while the disciples prayed him, saying, Rabbi, eat.
JHN 4:32 32 But he said unto them, I have meat to eat that ye know not.
JHN 4:33 33 The disciples therefore said one to another, Hath any man brought him aught to eat?
JHN 4:34 34 Jesus saith unto them, My meat is to do the will of him that sent me, and to accomplish his work.
JHN 4:35 35 Say not ye, There are yet four months, and then cometh the harvest? behold, I say unto you, Lift up your eyes, and look on the fields, that they are white already unto harvest.
JHN 4:36 36 He that reapeth receiveth wages, and gathereth fruit unto life eternal; that he that soweth and he that reapeth may rejoice together.
JHN 4:37 37 For herein is the saying true, One soweth, and another reapeth.
JHN 4:38 38 I sent you to reap that whereon ye have not labored: others have labored, and ye are entered into their labor.
JHN 4:39 39 And from that city many of the Samaritans believed on him because of the word of the woman, who testified, He told me all things that ever I did.
JHN 4:40 40 So when the Samaritans came unto him, they besought him to abide with them: and he abode there two days.
JHN 4:41 41 And many more believed because of his word;
JHN 4:42 42 and they said to the woman, Now we believe, not because of thy speaking: for we have heard for ourselves, and know that this is indeed the Saviour of the world.
JHN 4:43 43 And after the two days he went forth from thence into Galilee.
JHN 4:44 44 For Jesus himself testified, that a prophet hath no honor in his own country.
JHN 4:45 45 So when he came into Galilee, the Galilæans received him, having seen all the things that he did in Jerusalem at the feast: for they also went unto the feast.
JHN 4:46 46 He came therefore again unto Cana of Galilee, where he made the water wine. And there was a certain nobleman, whose son was sick at Capernaum.
JHN 4:47 47 When he heard that Jesus was come out of Judæa into Galilee, he went unto him, and besought him that he would come down, and heal his son; for he was at the point of death.
JHN 4:48 48 Jesus therefore said unto him, Except ye see signs and wonders, ye will in no wise believe.
JHN 4:49 49 The nobleman saith unto him, Sir, come down ere my child die.
JHN 4:50 50 Jesus saith unto him, Go thy way; thy son liveth. The man believed the word that Jesus spake unto him, and he went his way.
JHN 4:51 51 And as he was now going down, his servants met him, saying, that his son lived.
JHN 4:52 52 So he inquired of them the hour when he began to amend. They said therefore unto him, Yesterday at the seventh hour the fever left him.
JHN 4:53 53 So the father knew that it was at that hour in which Jesus said unto him, Thy son liveth: and himself believed, and his whole house.
JHN 4:54 54 This is again the second sign that Jesus did, having come out of Judæa into Galilee.
JHN 5:1 1 After these things there was a feast of the Jews; and Jesus went up to Jerusalem.
JHN 5:2 2 Now there is in Jerusalem by the sheep gate a pool, which is called in Hebrew Bethesda, having five porches.
JHN 5:3 3 In these lay a multitude of them that were sick, blind, halt, withered.
JHN 5:4 4
JHN 5:5 5 And a certain man was there, who had been thirty and eight years in his infirmity.
JHN 5:6 6 When Jesus saw him lying, and knew that he had been now a long time in that case , he saith unto him, Wouldest thou be made whole?
JHN 5:7 7 The sick man answered him, Sir, I have no man, when the water is troubled, to put me into the pool: but while I am coming, another steppeth down before me.
JHN 5:8 8 Jesus saith unto him, Arise, take up thy bed, and walk.
JHN 5:9 9 And straightway the man was made whole, and took up his bed and walked.
JHN 5:10 Now it was the sabbath on that day.
JHN 5:11 10 So the Jews said unto him that was cured, It is the sabbath, and it is not lawful for thee to take up thy bed.
JHN 5:12 11 But he answered them, He that made me whole, the same said unto me, Take up thy bed, and walk.
JHN 5:13 12 They asked him, Who is the man that said unto thee, Take up thy bed , and walk?
JHN 5:14 13 But he that was healed knew not who it was; for Jesus had conveyed himself away, a multitude being in the place.
JHN 5:15 14 Afterward Jesus findeth him in the temple, and said unto him, Behold, thou art made whole: sin no more, lest a worse thing befall thee.
JHN 5:16 15 The man went away, and told the Jews that it was Jesus who had made him whole.
JHN 5:17 16 And for this cause the Jews persecuted Jesus, because he did these things on the sabbath.
JHN 5:18 17 But Jesus answered them, My Father worketh even until now, and I work.
JHN 5:19 18 For this cause therefore the Jews sought the more to kill him, because he not only brake the sabbath, but also called God his own Father, making himself equal with God.
JHN 5:20 19 Jesus therefore answered and said unto them, Verily, verily, I say unto you, The Son can do nothing of himself, but what he seeth the Father doing: for what things soever he doeth, these the Son also doeth in like manner.
JHN 5:21 20 For the Father loveth the Son, and showeth him all things that himself doeth: and greater works than these will he show him, that ye may marvel.
JHN 5:22 21 For as the Father raiseth the dead and giveth them life, even so the Son also giveth life to whom he will.
JHN 5:23 22 For neither doth the Father judge any man, but he hath given all judgment unto the Son;
JHN 5:24 23 that all may honor the Son, even as they honor the Father. He that honoreth not the Son honoreth not the Father that sent him.
JHN 5:25 24 Verily, verily, I say unto you, He that heareth my word, and believeth him that sent me, hath eternal life, and cometh not into judgment, but hath passed out of death into life.
JHN 5:26 25 Verily, verily, I say unto you, The hour cometh, and now is, when the dead shall hear the voice of the Son of God; and they that hear shall live.
JHN 5:27 26 For as the Father hath life in himself, even so gave he to the Son also to have life in himself:
JHN 5:28 27 and he gave him authority to execute judgment, because he is a son of man.
JHN 5:29 28 Marvel not at this: for the hour cometh, in which all that are in the tombs shall hear his voice,
JHN 5:30 29 and shall come forth; they that have done good, unto the resurrection of life; and they that have done evil, unto the resurrection of judgment.
JHN 5:31 30 I can of myself do nothing: as I hear, I judge: and my judgment is righteous; because I seek not mine own will, but the will of him that sent me.
JHN 5:32 31 If I bear witness of myself, my witness is not true.
JHN 5:33 32 It is another that beareth witness of me; and I know that the witness which he witnesseth of me is true.
JHN 5:34 33 Ye have sent unto John, and he hath borne witness unto the truth.
JHN 5:35 34 But the witness which I receive is not from man: howbeit I say these things, that ye may be saved.
JHN 5:36 35 He was the lamp that burneth and shineth; and ye were willing to rejoice for a season in his light.
JHN 5:37 36 But the witness which I have is greater than that of John; for the works which the Father hath given me to accomplish, the very works that I do, bear witness of me, that the Father hath sent me.
JHN 5:38 37 And the Father that sent me, he hath borne witness of me. Ye have neither heard his voice at any time, nor seen his form.
JHN 5:39 38 And ye have not his word abiding in you: for whom he sent, him ye believe not.
JHN 5:40 39 Ye search the scriptures, because ye think that in them ye have eternal life; and these are they which bear witness of me;
JHN 5:41 40 and ye will not come to me, that ye may have life.
JHN 5:42 41 I receive not glory from men.
JHN 5:43 42 But I know you, that ye have not the love of God in yourselves.
JHN 5:44 43 I am come in my Father’s name, and ye receive me not: if another shall come in his own name, him ye will receive.
JHN 5:45 44 How can ye believe, who receive glory one of another, and the glory that cometh from the only God ye seek not?
JHN 5:46 45 Think not that I will accuse you to the Father: there is one that accuseth you, even Moses, on whom ye have set your hope.
JHN 5:47 46 For if ye believed Moses, ye would believe me; for he wrote of me.
JHN 5:48 47 But if ye believe not his writings, how shall ye believe my words?
JHN 6:1 1 After these things Jesus went away to the other side of the sea of Galilee, which is the sea of Tiberias.
JHN 6:2 2 And a great multitude followed him, because they beheld the signs which he did on them that were sick.
JHN 6:3 3 And Jesus went up into the mountain, and there he sat with his disciples.
JHN 6:4 4 Now the passover, the feast of the Jews, was at hand.
JHN 6:5 5 Jesus therefore lifting up his eyes, and seeing that a great multitude cometh unto him, saith unto Philip, Whence are we to buy bread, that these may eat?
JHN 6:6 6 And this he said to prove him: for he himself knew what he would do.
JHN 6:7 7 Philip answered him, Two hundred shillings’ worth of bread is not sufficient for them, that every one may take a little.
JHN 6:8 8 One of his disciples, Andrew, Simon Peter’s brother, saith unto him,
JHN 6:9 9 There is a lad here, who hath five barley loaves, and two fishes: but what are these among so many?
JHN 6:10 10 Jesus said, Make the people sit down. Now there was much grass in the place. So the men sat down, in number about five thousand.
JHN 6:11 11 Jesus therefore took the loaves; and having given thanks, he distributed to them that were set down; likewise also of the fishes as much as they would.
JHN 6:12 12 And when they were filled, he saith unto his disciples, Gather up the broken pieces which remain over, that nothing be lost.
JHN 6:13 13 So they gathered them up, and filled twelve baskets with broken pieces from the five barley loaves, which remained over unto them that had eaten.
JHN 6:14 14 When therefore the people saw the sign which he did, they said, This is of a truth the prophet that cometh into the world.
JHN 6:15 15 Jesus therefore perceiving that they were about to come and take him by force, to make him king, withdrew again into the mountain himself alone.
JHN 6:16 16 And when evening came, his disciples went down unto the sea;
JHN 6:17 17 and they entered into a boat, and were going over the sea unto Capernaum. And it was now dark, and Jesus had not yet come to them.
JHN 6:18 18 And the sea was rising by reason of a great wind that blew.
JHN 6:19 19 When therefore they had rowed about five and twenty or thirty furlongs, they behold Jesus walking on the sea, and drawing nigh unto the boat: and they were afraid.
JHN 6:20 20 But he saith unto them, It is I; be not afraid.
JHN 6:21 21 They were willing therefore to receive him into the boat: and straightway the boat was at the land whither they were going.
JHN 6:22 22 On the morrow the multitude that stood on the other side of the sea saw that there was no other boat there, save one, and that Jesus entered not with his disciples into the boat, but that his disciples went away alone
JHN 6:23 23 (howbeit there came boats from Tiberias nigh unto the place where they ate the bread after the Lord had given thanks):
JHN 6:24 24 when the multitude therefore saw that Jesus was not there, neither his disciples, they themselves got into the boats, and came to Capernaum, seeking Jesus.
JHN 6:25 25 And when they found him on the other side of the sea, they said unto him, Rabbi, when camest thou hither?
JHN 6:26 26 Jesus answered them and said, Verily, verily, I say unto you, Ye seek me, not because ye saw signs, but because ye ate of the loaves, and were filled.
JHN 6:27 27 Work not for the food which perisheth, but for the food which abideth unto eternal life, which the Son of man shall give unto you: for him the Father, even God, hath sealed.
JHN 6:28 28 They said therefore unto him, What must we do, that we may work the works of God?
JHN 6:29 29 Jesus answered and said unto them, This is the work of God, that ye believe on him whom he hath sent.
JHN 6:30 30 They said therefore unto him, What then doest thou for a sign, that we may see, and believe thee? what workest thou?
JHN 6:31 31 Our fathers ate the manna in the wilderness; as it is written, He gave them bread out of heaven to eat.
JHN 6:32 32 Jesus therefore said unto them, Verily, verily, I say unto you, It was not Moses that gave you the bread out of heaven; but my Father giveth you the true bread out of heaven.
JHN 6:33 33 For the bread of God is that which cometh down out of heaven, and giveth life unto the world.
JHN 6:34 34 They said therefore unto him, Lord, evermore give us this bread.
JHN 6:35 35 Jesus said unto them, I am the bread of life: he that cometh to me shall not hunger, and he that believeth on me shall never thirst.
JHN 6:36 36 But I said unto you, that ye have seen me, and yet believe not.
JHN 6:37 37 All that which the Father giveth me shall come unto me; and him that cometh to me I will in no wise cast out.
