ACT 1:7 7 And he said unto them, It is not for you to know times or seasons, which the Father hath set within his own authority.
ACT 1:8 8 But ye shall receive power, when the Holy Spirit is come upon you: and ye shall be my witnesses both in Jerusalem, and in all Judæa and Samaria, and unto the uttermost part of the earth.
ACT 1:9 9 And when he had said these things, as they were looking, he was taken up; and a cloud received him out of their sight.
ACT 1:10 10 And while they were looking stedfastly into heaven as he went, behold two men stood by them in white apparel;
ACT 1:11 11 who also said, Ye men of Galilee, why stand ye looking into heaven? this Jesus, who was received up from you into heaven, shall so come in like manner as ye beheld him going into heaven.
ACT 1:12 12 Then returned they unto Jerusalem from the mount called Olivet, which is nigh unto Jerusalem, a sabbath day’s journey off.
ACT 1:13 13 And when they were come in, they went up into the upper chamber, where they were abiding; both Peter and John and James and Andrew, Philip and Thomas, Bartholomew and Matthew, James the son of Alphæus, and Simon the Zealot, and Judas the son of James.
ACT 1:14 14 These all with one accord continued stedfastly in prayer, with the women, and Mary the mother of Jesus, and with his brethren.
ACT 1:15 15 And in these days Peter stood up in the midst of the brethren, and said (and there was a multitude of persons gathered together, about a hundred and twenty),
ACT 1:16 16 Brethren, it was needful that the scripture should be fulfilled, which the Holy Spirit spake before by the mouth of David concerning Judas, who was guide to them that took Jesus.
ACT 1:17 17 For he was numbered among us, and received his portion in this ministry.
ACT 1:18 18 (Now this man obtained a field with the reward of his iniquity; and falling headlong, he burst asunder in the midst, and all his bowels gushed out.
ACT 1:19 19 And it became known to all the dwellers at Jerusalem; insomuch that in their language that field was called Akeldama, that is, The field of blood.)
ACT 1:20 20 For it is written in the book of Psalms,
ACT 1:21 Let his habitation be made desolate,
ACT 1:22 And let no man dwell therein:
ACT 1:23 and,
ACT 1:24 His office let another take.
ACT 1:25 21 Of the men therefore that have companied with us all the time that the Lord Jesus went in and went out among us,
ACT 1:26 22 beginning from the baptism of John, unto the day that he was received up from us, of these must one become a witness with us of his resurrection.
ACT 1:27 23 And they put forward two, Joseph called Barsabbas, who was surnamed Justus, and Matthias.
ACT 1:28 24 And they prayed, and said, Thou, Lord, who knowest the hearts of all men, show of these two the one whom thou hast chosen,
ACT 1:29 25 to take the place in this ministry and apostleship from which Judas fell away, that he might go to his own place.
ACT 1:30 26 And they gave lots for them; and the lot fell upon Matthias; and he was numbered with the eleven apostles.
ACT 2:1 1 And when the day of Pentecost was now come, they were all together in one place.
ACT 2:2 2 And suddenly there came from heaven a sound as of the rushing of a mighty wind, and it filled all the house where they were sitting.
ACT 2:3 3 And there appeared unto them tongues parting asunder, like as of fire; and it sat upon each one of them.
ACT 2:4 4 And they were all filled with the Holy Spirit, and began to speak with other tongues, as the Spirit gave them utterance.
ACT 2:5 5 Now there were dwelling at Jerusalem Jews, devout men, from every nation under heaven.
ACT 2:6 6 And when this sound was heard, the multitude came together, and were confounded, because that every man heard them speaking in his own language.
ACT 2:7 7 And they were all amazed and marvelled, saying, Behold, are not all these that speak Galilæans?
ACT 2:8 8 And how hear we, every man in our own language wherein we were born?
ACT 2:9 9 Parthians and Medes and Elamites, and the dwellers in Mesopotamia, in Judæa and Cappadocia, in Pontus and Asia,
ACT 2:10 10 in Phrygia and Pamphylia, in Egypt and the parts of Libya about Cyrene, and sojourners from Rome, both Jews and proselytes,
ACT 2:11 11 Cretans and Arabians, we hear them speaking in our tongues the mighty works of God.
ACT 2:12 12 And they were all amazed, and were perplexed, saying one to another, What meaneth this?
ACT 2:13 13 But others mocking said, They are filled with new wine.
ACT 2:14 14 But Peter, standing up with the eleven, lifted up his voice, and spake forth unto them, saying , Ye men of Judæa, and all ye that dwell at Jerusalem, be this known unto you, and give ear unto my words.
ACT 2:15 15 For these are not drunken, as ye suppose; seeing it is but the third hour of the day;
ACT 2:16 16 but this is that which hath been spoken through the prophet Joel:
ACT 2:17 17 And it shall be in the last days, saith God,
ACT 2:18 I will pour forth of my Spirit upon all flesh:
ACT 2:19 And your sons and your daughters shall prophesy,
ACT 2:20 And your young men shall see visions,
ACT 2:21 And your old men shall dream dreams:
ACT 2:22 18 Yea and on my servants and on my handmaidens in those days
ACT 2:23 Will I pour forth of my Spirit; and they shall prophesy.
ACT 2:24 19 And I will show wonders in the heaven above,
ACT 2:25 And signs on the earth beneath;
ACT 2:26 Blood, and fire, and vapor of smoke:
ACT 2:27 20 The sun shall be turned into darkness,
ACT 2:28 And the moon into blood,
ACT 2:29 Before the day of the Lord come,
ACT 2:30 That great and notable day :
ACT 2:31 21 And it shall be, that whosoever shall call on the name of the Lord shall be saved.
ACT 2:32 22 Ye men of Israel, hear these words: Jesus of Nazareth, a man approved of God unto you by mighty works and wonders and signs which God did by him in the midst of you, even as ye yourselves know;
ACT 2:33 23 him, being delivered up by the determinate counsel and foreknowledge of God, ye by the hand of lawless men did crucify and slay:
ACT 2:34 24 whom God raised up, having loosed the pangs of death: because it was not possible that he should be holden of it.
ACT 2:35 25 For David saith concerning him,
ACT 2:36 I beheld the Lord always before my face;
ACT 2:37 For he is on my right hand, that I should not be moved:
ACT 2:38 26 Therefore my heart was glad, and my tongue rejoiced;
ACT 2:39 Moreover my flesh also shall dwell in hope:
ACT 2:40 27 Because thou wilt not leave my soul unto Hades,
ACT 2:41 Neither wilt thou give thy Holy One to see corruption.
ACT 2:42 28 Thou madest known unto me the ways of life;
ACT 2:43 Thou shalt make me full of gladness with thy countenance.
ACT 2:44 29 Brethren, I may say unto you freely of the patriarch David, that he both died and was buried, and his tomb is with us unto this day.
ACT 2:45 30 Being therefore a prophet, and knowing that God had sworn with an oath to him, that of the fruit of his loins he would set one upon his throne;
ACT 2:46 31 he foreseeing this spake of the resurrection of the Christ, that neither was he left unto Hades, nor did his flesh see corruption.
ACT 2:47 32 This Jesus did God raise up, whereof we all are witnesses.
ACT 2:48 33 Being therefore by the right hand of God exalted, and having received of the Father the promise of the Holy Spirit, he hath poured forth this, which ye see and hear.
ACT 2:49 34 For David ascended not into the heavens: but he saith himself,
ACT 2:50 The Lord said unto my Lord, Sit thou on my right hand,
ACT 2:51 35 Till I make thine enemies the footstool of thy feet.
ACT 2:52 36 Let all the house of Israel therefore know assuredly, that God hath made him both Lord and Christ, this Jesus whom ye crucified.
ACT 2:53 37 Now when they heard this , they were pricked in their heart, and said unto Peter and the rest of the apostles, Brethren, what shall we do?
ACT 2:54 38 And Peter said unto them, Repent ye, and be baptized every one of you in the name of Jesus Christ unto the remission of your sins; and ye shall receive the gift of the Holy Spirit.
ACT 2:55 39 For to you is the promise, and to your children, and to all that are afar off, even as many as the Lord our God shall call unto him.
ACT 2:56 40 And with many other words he testified, and exhorted them, saying, Save yourselves from this crooked generation.
ACT 2:57 41 They then that received his word were baptized: and there were added unto them in that day about three thousand souls.
ACT 2:58 42 And they continued stedfastly in the apostles’ teaching and fellowship, in the breaking of bread and the prayers.
ACT 2:59 43 And fear came upon every soul: and many wonders and signs were done through the apostles.
ACT 2:60 44 And all that believed were together, and had all things common;
ACT 2:61 45 and they sold their possessions and goods, and parted them to all, according as any man had need.
ACT 2:62 46 And day by day, continuing stedfastly with one accord in the temple, and breaking bread at home, they took their food with gladness and singleness of heart,
ACT 2:63 47 praising God, and having favor with all the people. And the Lord added to them day by day those that were saved.
ACT 3:1 1 Now Peter and John were going up into the temple at the hour of prayer, being the ninth hour .
ACT 3:2 2 And a certain man that was lame from his mother’s womb was carried, whom they laid daily at the door of the temple which is called Beautiful, to ask alms of them that entered into the temple;
ACT 3:3 3 who seeing Peter and John about to go into the temple, asked to receive an alms.
ACT 3:4 4 And Peter, fastening his eyes upon him, with John, said, Look on us.
ACT 3:5 5 And he gave heed unto them, expecting to receive something from them.
ACT 3:6 6 But Peter said, Silver and gold have I none; but what I have, that give I thee. In the name of Jesus Christ of Nazareth, walk.
ACT 3:7 7 And he took him by the right hand, and raised him up: and immediately his feet and his ankle-bones received strength.
ACT 3:8 8 And leaping up, he stood, and began to walk; and he entered with them into the temple, walking, and leaping, and praising God.
ACT 3:9 9 And all the people saw him walking and praising God:
ACT 3:10 10 and they took knowledge of him, that it was he that sat for alms at the Beautiful Gate of the temple; and they were filled with wonder and amazement at that which had happened unto him.
ACT 3:11 11 And as he held Peter and John, all the people ran together unto them in the porch that is called Solomon’s, greatly wondering.
ACT 3:12 12 And when Peter saw it, he answered unto the people, Ye men of Israel, why marvel ye at this man? or why fasten ye your eyes on us, as though by our own power or godliness we had made him to walk?
ACT 3:13 13 The God of Abraham, and of Isaac, and of Jacob, the God of our fathers, hath glorified his Servant Jesus; whom ye delivered up, and denied before the face of Pilate, when he had determined to release him.
ACT 3:14 14 But ye denied the Holy and Righteous One, and asked for a murderer to be granted unto you,
ACT 3:15 15 and killed the Prince of life; whom God raised from the dead; whereof we are witnesses.
ACT 3:16 16 And by faith in his name hath his name made this man strong, whom ye behold and know: yea, the faith which is through him hath given him this perfect soundness in the presence of you all.
ACT 3:17 17 And now, brethren, I know that in ignorance ye did it, as did also your rulers.
ACT 3:18 18 But the things which God foreshowed by the mouth of all the prophets, that his Christ should suffer, he thus fulfilled.
ACT 3:19 19 Repent ye therefore, and turn again, that your sins may be blotted out, that so there may come seasons of refreshing from the presence of the Lord;
ACT 3:20 20 and that he may send the Christ who hath been appointed for you, even Jesus:
ACT 3:21 21 whom the heaven must receive until the times of restoration of all things, whereof God spake by the mouth of his holy prophets that have been from of old.
ACT 3:22 22 Moses indeed said, A prophet shall the Lord God raise up unto you from among your brethren, like unto me; to him shall ye hearken in all things whatsoever he shall speak unto you.
ACT 3:23 23 And it shall be, that every soul that shall not hearken to that prophet, shall be utterly destroyed from among the people.
ACT 3:24 24 Yea and all the prophets from Samuel and them that followed after, as many as have spoken, they also told of these days.
ACT 3:25 25 Ye are the sons of the prophets, and of the covenant which God made with your fathers, saying unto Abraham, And in thy seed shall all the families of the earth be blessed.
ACT 3:26 26 Unto you first God, having raised up his Servant, sent him to bless you, in turning away every one of you from your iniquities.
ACT 4:1 1 And as they spake unto the people, the priests and the captain of the temple and the Sadducees came upon them,
ACT 4:2 2 being sore troubled because they taught the people, and proclaimed in Jesus the resurrection from the dead.
ACT 4:3 3 And they laid hands on them, and put them in ward unto the morrow: for it was now eventide.
ACT 4:4 4 But many of them that heard the word believed; and the number of the men came to be about five thousand.
ACT 4:5 5 And it came to pass on the morrow, that their rulers and elders and scribes were gathered together in Jerusalem;
ACT 4:6 6 and Annas the high priest was there , and Caiaphas, and John, and Alexander, and as many as were of the kindred of the high priest.
ACT 4:7 7 And when they had set them in the midst, they inquired, By what power, or in what name, have ye done this?
ACT 4:8 8 Then Peter, filled with the Holy Spirit, said unto them, Ye rulers of the people, and elders,
ACT 4:9 9 if we this day are examined concerning a good deed done to an impotent man, by what means this man is made whole;
ACT 4:10 10 be it known unto you all, and to all the people of Israel, that in the name of Jesus Christ of Nazareth, whom ye crucified, whom God raised from the dead, even in him doth this man stand here before you whole.
ACT 4:11 11 He is the stone which was set at nought of you the builders, which was made the head of the corner.
ACT 4:12 12 And in none other is there salvation: for neither is there any other name under heaven, that is given among men, wherein we must be saved.
ACT 4:13 13 Now when they beheld the boldness of Peter and John, and had perceived that they were unlearned and ignorant men, they marvelled; and they took knowledge of them, that they had been with Jesus.
ACT 4:14 14 And seeing the man that was healed standing with them, they could say nothing against it.
ACT 4:15 15 But when they had commanded them to go aside out of the council, they conferred among themselves,
ACT 4:16 16 saying, What shall we do to these men? for that indeed a notable miracle hath been wrought through them, is manifest to all that dwell in Jerusalem; and we cannot deny it.
ACT 4:17 17 But that it spread no further among the people, let us threaten them, that they speak henceforth to no man in this name.
ACT 4:18 18 And they called them, and charged them not to speak at all nor teach in the name of Jesus.
ACT 4:19 19 But Peter and John answered and said unto them, Whether it is right in the sight of God to hearken unto you rather than unto God, judge ye:
ACT 4:20 20 for we cannot but speak the things which we saw and heard.
ACT 4:21 21 And they, when they had further threatened them, let them go, finding nothing how they might punish them, because of the people; for all men glorified God for that which was done.
ACT 4:22 22 For the man was more than forty years old, on whom this miracle of healing was wrought.
ACT 4:23 23 And being let go, they came to their own company, and reported all that the chief priests and the elders had said unto them.
ACT 4:24 24 And they, when they heard it, lifted up their voice to God with one accord, and said, O Lord, thou that didst make the heaven and the earth and the sea, and all that in them is:
ACT 4:25 25 who by the Holy Spirit, by the mouth of our father David thy servant, didst say,
ACT 4:26 Why did the Gentiles rage,
ACT 4:27 And the peoples imagine vain things?
ACT 4:28 26 The kings of the earth set themselves in array,
ACT 4:29 And the rulers were gathered together,
ACT 4:30 Against the Lord, and against his Anointed:
ACT 4:31 27 for of a truth in this city against thy holy Servant Jesus, whom thou didst anoint, both Herod and Pontius Pilate, with the Gentiles and the peoples of Israel, were gathered together,
ACT 4:32 28 to do whatsoever thy hand and thy counsel foreordained to come to pass.
ACT 4:33 29 And now, Lord, look upon their threatenings: and grant unto thy servants to speak thy word with all boldness,
ACT 4:34 30 while thou stretchest forth thy hand to heal; and that signs and wonders may be done through the name of thy holy Servant Jesus.
ACT 4:35 31 And when they had prayed, the place was shaken wherein they were gathered together; and they were all filled with the Holy Spirit, and they spake the word of God with boldness.
ACT 4:36 32 And the multitude of them that believed were of one heart and soul: and not one of them said that aught of the things which he possessed was his own; but they had all things common.
ACT 4:37 33 And with great power gave the apostles their witness of the resurrection of the Lord Jesus: and great grace was upon them all.
ACT 4:38 34 For neither was there among them any that lacked: for as many as were possessors of lands or houses sold them, and brought the prices of the things that were sold,
ACT 4:39 35 and laid them at the apostles’ feet: and distribution was made unto each, according as any one had need.
ACT 4:40 36 And Joseph, who by the apostles was surnamed Barnabas (which is, being interpreted, Son of exhortation), a Levite, a man of Cyprus by race,
ACT 4:41 37 having a field, sold it, and brought the money and laid it at the apostles’ feet.
ACT 5:1 1 But a certain man named Ananias, with Sapphira his wife, sold a possession,
ACT 5:2 2 and kept back part of the price, his wife also being privy to it, and brought a certain part, and laid it at the apostles’ feet.
ACT 5:3 3 But Peter said, Ananias, why hath Satan filled thy heart to lie to the Holy Spirit, and to keep back part of the price of the land?
ACT 5:4 4 While it remained, did it not remain thine own? and after it was sold, was it not in thy power? How is it that thou hast conceived this thing in thy heart? thou hast not lied unto men, but unto God.
ACT 5:5 5 And Ananias hearing these words fell down and gave up the ghost: and great fear came upon all that heard it.
ACT 5:6 6 And the young men arose and wrapped him round, and they carried him out and buried him.
ACT 5:7 7 And it was about the space of three hours after, when his wife, not knowing what was done, came in.
ACT 5:8 8 And Peter answered unto her, Tell me whether ye sold the land for so much. And she said, Yea, for so much.
ACT 5:9 9 But Peter said unto her, How is it that ye have agreed together to try the Spirit of the Lord? behold, the feet of them that have buried thy husband are at the door, and they shall carry thee out.
ACT 5:10 10 And she fell down immediately at his feet, and gave up the ghost: and the young men came in and found her dead, and they carried her out and buried her by her husband.
ACT 5:11 11 And great fear came upon the whole church, and upon all that heard these things.
ACT 5:12 12 And by the hands of the apostles were many signs and wonders wrought among the people: and they were all with one accord in Solomon’s porch.
ACT 5:13 13 But of the rest durst no man join himself to them: howbeit the people magnified them;
ACT 5:14 14 and believers were the more added to the Lord, multitudes both of men and women:
ACT 5:15 15 insomuch that they even carried out the sick into the streets, and laid them on beds and couches, that, as Peter came by, at the least his shadow might overshadow some one of them.
ACT 5:16 16 And there also came together the multitude from the cities round about Jerusalem, bringing sick folk, and them that were vexed with unclean spirits: and they were healed every one.
ACT 5:17 17 But the high priest rose up, and all they that were with him (which is the sect of the Sadducees), and they were filled with jealousy,
ACT 5:18 18 and laid hands on the apostles, and put them in public ward.
ACT 5:19 19 But an angel of the Lord by night opened the prison doors, and brought them out, and said,
ACT 5:20 20 Go ye, and stand and speak in the temple to the people all the words of this Life.
ACT 5:21 21 And when they heard this , they entered into the temple about daybreak, and taught. But the high priest came, and they that were with him, and called the council together, and all the senate of the children of Israel, and sent to the prison-house to have them brought.
ACT 5:22 22 But the officers that came found them not in the prison; and they returned, and told,
ACT 5:23 23 saying, The prison-house we found shut in all safety, and the keepers standing at the doors: but when we had opened, we found no man within.
ACT 5:24 24 Now when the captain of the temple and the chief priests heard these words, they were much perplexed concerning them whereunto this would grow.
ACT 5:25 25 And there came one and told them, Behold, the men whom ye put in the prison are in the temple standing and teaching the people.
ACT 5:26 26 Then went the captain with the officers, and brought them, but without violence; for they feared the people, lest they should be stoned.
ACT 5:27 27 And when they had brought them, they set them before the council. And the high priest asked them,
ACT 5:28 28 saying, We strictly charged you not to teach in this name: and behold, ye have filled Jerusalem with your teaching, and intend to bring this man’s blood upon us.
ACT 5:29 29 But Peter and the apostles answered and said, We must obey God rather than men.
ACT 5:30 30 The God of our fathers raised up Jesus, whom ye slew, hanging him on a tree.
ACT 5:31 31 Him did God exalt with his right hand to be a Prince and a Saviour, to give repentance to Israel, and remission of sins.
ACT 5:32 32 And we are witnesses of these things; and so is the Holy Spirit, whom God hath given to them that obey him.
ACT 5:33 33 But they, when they heard this, were cut to the heart, and were minded to slay them.
ACT 5:34 34 But there stood up one in the council, a Pharisee, named Gamaliel, a doctor of the law, had in honor of all the people, and commanded to put the men forth a little while.
ACT 5:35 35 And he said unto them, Ye men of Israel, take heed to yourselves as touching these men, what ye are about to do.
ACT 5:36 36 For before these days rose up Theudas, giving himself out to be somebody; to whom a number of men, about four hundred, joined themselves: who was slain; and all, as many as obeyed him, were dispersed, and came to nought.
ACT 5:37 37 After this man rose up Judas of Galilee in the days of the enrolment, and drew away some of the people after him: he also perished; and all, as many as obeyed him, were scattered abroad.
ACT 5:38 38 And now I say unto you, Refrain from these men, and let them alone: for if this counsel or this work be of men, it will be overthrown:
ACT 5:39 39 but if it is of God, ye will not be able to overthrow them; lest haply ye be found even to be fighting against God.
ACT 5:40 40 And to him they agreed: and when they had called the apostles unto them, they beat them and charged them not to speak in the name of Jesus, and let them go.
ACT 5:41 41 They therefore departed from the presence of the council, rejoicing that they were counted worthy to suffer dishonor for the Name.
ACT 5:42 42 And every day, in the temple and at home, they ceased not to teach and to preach Jesus as the Christ.
ACT 6:1 1 Now in these days, when the number of the disciples was multiplying, there arose a murmuring of the Grecian Jews against the Hebrews, because their widows were neglected in the daily ministration.
ACT 6:2 2 And the twelve called the multitude of the disciples unto them, and said, It is not fit that we should forsake the word of God, and serve tables.
ACT 6:3 3 Look ye out therefore, brethren, from among you seven men of good report, full of the Spirit and of wisdom, whom we may appoint over this business.
ACT 6:4 4 But we will continue stedfastly in prayer, and in the ministry of the word.
ACT 6:5 5 And the saying pleased the whole multitude: and they chose Stephen, a man full of faith and of the Holy Spirit, and Philip, and Prochorus, and Nicanor, and Timon, and Parmenas, and Nicolaüs a proselyte of Antioch;
ACT 6:6 6 whom they set before the apostles: and when they had prayed, they laid their hands upon them.
ACT 6:7 7 And the word of God increased; and the number of the disciples multiplied in Jerusalem exceedingly; and a great company of the priests were obedient to the faith.
ACT 6:8 8 And Stephen, full of grace and power, wrought great wonders and signs among the people.
ACT 6:9 9 But there arose certain of them that were of the synagogue called the synagogue of the Libertines, and of the Cyrenians, and of the Alexandrians, and of them of Cilicia and Asia, disputing with Stephen.
ACT 6:10 10 And they were not able to withstand the wisdom and the Spirit by which he spake.
ACT 6:11 11 Then they suborned men, who said, We have heard him speak blasphemous words against Moses, and against God.
ACT 6:12 12 And they stirred up the people, and the elders, and the scribes, and came upon him, and seized him, and brought him into the council,
ACT 6:13 13 and set up false witnesses, who said, This man ceaseth not to speak words against this holy place, and the law:
ACT 6:14 14 for we have heard him say, that this Jesus of Nazareth shall destroy this place, and shall change the customs which Moses delivered unto us.
ACT 6:15 15 And all that sat in the council, fastening their eyes on him, saw his face as it had been the face of an angel.
ACT 7:1 1 And the high priest said, Are these things so?
ACT 7:2 2 And he said,
ACT 7:3 Brethren and fathers, hearken: The God of glory appeared unto our father Abraham, when he was in Mesopotamia, before he dwelt in Haran,
ACT 7:4 3 and said unto him, Get thee out of thy land, and from thy kindred, and come into the land which I shall show thee.
ACT 7:5 4 Then came he out of the land of the Chaldæans, and dwelt in Haran: and from thence, when his father was dead, God removed him into this land, wherein ye now dwell:
ACT 7:6 5 and he gave him none inheritance in it, no, not so much as to set his foot on: and he promised that he would give it to him in possession, and to his seed after him, when as yet he had no child.
ACT 7:7 6 And God spake on this wise, that his seed should sojourn in a strange land, and that they should bring them into bondage, and treat them ill, four hundred years.
ACT 7:8 7 And the nation to which they shall be in bondage will I judge, said God: and after that shall they come forth, and serve me in this place.
ACT 7:9 8 And he gave him the covenant of circumcision: and so Abraham begat Isaac, and circumcised him the eighth day; and Isaac begat Jacob, and Jacob the twelve patriarchs.
ACT 7:10 9 And the patriarchs, moved with jealousy against Joseph, sold him into Egypt: and God was with him,
ACT 7:11 10 and delivered him out of all his afflictions, and gave him favor and wisdom before Pharaoh king of Egypt; and he made him governor over Egypt and all his house.
ACT 7:12 11 Now there came a famine over all Egypt and Canaan, and great affliction: and our fathers found no sustenance.
ACT 7:13 12 But when Jacob heard that there was grain in Egypt, he sent forth our fathers the first time.
ACT 7:14 13 And at the second time Joseph was made known to his brethren; and Joseph’s race became manifest unto Pharaoh.
ACT 7:15 14 And Joseph sent, and called to him Jacob his father, and all his kindred, threescore and fifteen souls.
ACT 7:16 15 And Jacob went down into Egypt; and he died, himself and our fathers;
ACT 7:17 16 and they were carried over unto Shechem, and laid in the tomb that Abraham bought for a price in silver of the sons of Hamor in Shechem.
ACT 7:18 17 But as the time of the promise drew nigh which God vouchsafed unto Abraham, the people grew and multiplied in Egypt,
ACT 7:19 18 till there arose another king over Egypt, who knew not Joseph.
ACT 7:20 19 The same dealt craftily with our race, and ill-treated our fathers, that they should cast out their babes to the end they might not live.
ACT 7:21 20 At which season Moses was born, and was exceeding fair; and he was nourished three months in his father’s house:
ACT 7:22 21 and when he was cast out, Pharaoh’s daughter took him up, and nourished him for her own son.
ACT 7:23 22 And Moses was instructed in all the wisdom of the Egyptians; and he was mighty in his words and works.
ACT 7:24 23 But when he was well-nigh forty years old, it came into his heart to visit his brethren the children of Israel.
ACT 7:25 24 And seeing one of them suffer wrong, he defended him, and avenged him that was oppressed, smiting the Egyptian:
ACT 7:26 25 and he supposed that his brethren understood that God by his hand was giving them deliverance; but they understood not.
ACT 7:27 26 And the day following he appeared unto them as they strove, and would have set them at one again, saying, Sirs, ye are brethren; why do ye wrong one to another?
ACT 7:28 27 But he that did his neighbor wrong thrust him away, saying, Who made thee a ruler and a judge over us?
ACT 7:29 28 Wouldest thou kill me, as thou killedst the Egyptian yesterday?
ACT 7:30 29 And Moses fled at this saying, and became a sojourner in the land of Midian, where he begat two sons.
ACT 7:31 30 And when forty years were fulfilled, an angel appeared to him in the wilderness of mount Sinai, in a flame of fire in a bush.
ACT 7:32 31 And when Moses saw it, he wondered at the sight: and as he drew near to behold, there came a voice of the Lord,
ACT 7:33 32 I am the God of thy fathers, the God of Abraham, and of Isaac, and of Jacob. And Moses trembled, and durst not behold.
ACT 7:34 33 And the Lord said unto him, Loose the shoes from thy feet: for the place whereon thou standest is holy ground.
ACT 7:35 34 I have surely seen the affliction of my people that is in Egypt, and have heard their groaning, and I am come down to deliver them: and now come, I will send thee into Egypt.
ACT 7:36 35 This Moses whom they refused, saying, Who made thee a ruler and a judge? him hath God sent to be both a ruler and a deliverer with the hand of the angel that appeared to him in the bush.
ACT 7:37 36 This man led them forth, having wrought wonders and signs in Egypt, and in the Red sea, and in the wilderness forty years.
ACT 7:38 37 This is that Moses, who said unto the children of Israel, A prophet shall God raise up unto you from among your brethren, like unto me.
ACT 7:39 38 This is he that was in the church in the wilderness with the angel that spake to him in the mount Sinai, and with our fathers: who received living oracles to give unto us:
ACT 7:40 39 to whom our fathers would not be obedient, but thrust him from them, and turned back in their hearts unto Egypt,
ACT 7:41 40 saying unto Aaron, Make us gods that shall go before us: for as for this Moses, who led us forth out of the land of Egypt, we know not what is become of him.
ACT 7:42 41 And they made a calf in those days, and brought a sacrifice unto the idol, and rejoiced in the works of their hands.
ACT 7:43 42 But God turned, and gave them up to serve the host of heaven; as it is written in the book of the prophets,
ACT 7:44 Did ye offer unto me slain beasts and sacrifices
ACT 7:45 Forty years in the wilderness, O house of Israel?
ACT 7:46 43 And ye took up the tabernacle of Moloch,
ACT 7:47 And the star of the god Rephan,
ACT 7:48 The figures which ye made to worship them:
ACT 7:49 And I will carry you away beyond Babylon.
ACT 7:50 44 Our fathers had the tabernacle of the testimony in the wilderness, even as he appointed who spake unto Moses, that he should make it according to the figure that he had seen.
ACT 7:51 45 Which also our fathers, in their turn, brought in with Joshua when they entered on the possession of the nations, that God thrust out before the face of our fathers, unto the days of David;
ACT 7:52 46 who found favor in the sight of God, and asked to find a habitation for the God of Jacob.
ACT 7:53 47 But Solomon built him a house.
ACT 7:54 48 Howbeit the Most High dwelleth not in houses made with hands; as saith the prophet,
ACT 7:55 49 The heaven is my throne,
ACT 7:56 And the earth the footstool of my feet:
ACT 7:57 What manner of house will ye build me? saith the Lord:
ACT 7:58 Or what is the place of my rest?
ACT 7:59 50 Did not my hand make all these things?
ACT 7:60 51 Ye stiffnecked and uncircumcised in heart and ears, ye do always resist the Holy Spirit: as your fathers did, so do ye.
ACT 7:61 52 Which of the prophets did not your fathers persecute? and they killed them that showed before of the coming of the Righteous One; of whom ye have now become betrayers and murderers;
ACT 7:62 53 ye who received the law as it was ordained by angels, and kept it not.
ACT 7:63 54 Now when they heard these things, they were cut to the heart, and they gnashed on him with their teeth.
ACT 7:64 55 But he, being full of the Holy Spirit, looked up stedfastly into heaven, and saw the glory of God, and Jesus standing on the right hand of God,
ACT 7:65 56 and said, Behold, I see the heavens opened, and the Son of man standing on the right hand of God.
ACT 7:66 57 But they cried out with a loud voice, and stopped their ears, and rushed upon him with one accord;
ACT 7:67 58 and they cast him out of the city, and stoned him: and the witnesses laid down their garments at the feet of a young man named Saul.
ACT 7:68 59 And they stoned Stephen, calling upon the Lord , and saying, Lord Jesus, receive my spirit.
ACT 7:69 60 And he kneeled down, and cried with a loud voice, Lord, lay not this sin to their charge. And when he had said this, he fell asleep.
ACT 8:1 1 And Saul was consenting unto his death.
ACT 8:2 And there arose on that day a great persecution against the church which was in Jerusalem; and they were all scattered abroad throughout the regions of Judæa and Samaria, except the apostles.
ACT 8:3 2 And devout men buried Stephen, and made great lamentation over him.
ACT 8:4 3 But Saul laid waste the church, entering into every house, and dragging men and women committed them to prison.
ACT 8:5 4 They therefore that were scattered abroad went about preaching the word.
ACT 8:6 5 And Philip went down to the city of Samaria, and proclaimed unto them the Christ.
ACT 8:7 6 And the multitudes gave heed with one accord unto the things that were spoken by Philip, when they heard, and saw the signs which he did.
ACT 8:8 7 For from many of those that had unclean spirits, they came out, crying with a loud voice: and many that were palsied, and that were lame, were healed.
ACT 8:9 8 And there was much joy in that city.
ACT 8:10 9 But there was a certain man, Simon by name, who beforetime in the city used sorcery, and amazed the people of Samaria, giving out that himself was some great one:
ACT 8:11 10 to whom they all gave heed, from the least to the greatest, saying, This man is that power of God which is called Great.
ACT 8:12 11 And they gave heed to him, because that of long time he had amazed them with his sorceries.
ACT 8:13 12 But when they believed Philip preaching good tidings concerning the kingdom of God and the name of Jesus Christ, they were baptized, both men and women.
ACT 8:14 13 And Simon also himself believed: and being baptized, he continued with Philip; and beholding signs and great miracles wrought, he was amazed.
ACT 8:15 14 Now when the apostles that were at Jerusalem heard that Samaria had received the word of God, they sent unto them Peter and John:
ACT 8:16 15 who, when they were come down, prayed for them, that they might receive the Holy Spirit:
ACT 8:17 16 for as yet it was fallen upon none of them: only they had been baptized into the name of the Lord Jesus.
ACT 8:18 17 Then laid they their hands on them, and they received the Holy Spirit.
ACT 8:19 18 Now when Simon saw that through the laying on of the apostles’ hands the Holy Spirit was given, he offered them money,
ACT 8:20 19 saying, Give me also this power, that on whomsoever I lay my hands, he may receive the Holy Spirit.
ACT 8:21 20 But Peter said unto him, Thy silver perish with thee, because thou hast thought to obtain the gift of God with money.
ACT 8:22 21 Thou hast neither part nor lot in this matter: for thy heart is not right before God.
ACT 8:23 22 Repent therefore of this thy wickedness, and pray the Lord, if perhaps the thought of thy heart shall be forgiven thee.
ACT 8:24 23 For I see that thou art in the gall of bitterness and in the bond of iniquity.
ACT 8:25 24 And Simon answered and said, Pray ye for me to the Lord, that none of the things which ye have spoken come upon me.
ACT 8:26 25 They therefore, when they had testified and spoken the word of the Lord, returned to Jerusalem, and preached the gospel to many villages of the Samaritans.
ACT 8:27 26 But an angel of the Lord spake unto Philip, saying, Arise, and go toward the south unto the way that goeth down from Jerusalem unto Gaza: the same is desert.
ACT 8:28 27 And he arose and went: and behold, a man of Ethiopia, a eunuch of great authority under Candace, queen of the Ethiopians, who was over all her treasure, who had come to Jerusalem to worship;
ACT 8:29 28 and he was returning and sitting in his chariot, and was reading the prophet Isaiah.
ACT 8:30 29 And the Spirit said unto Philip, Go near, and join thyself to this chariot.
ACT 8:31 30 And Philip ran to him, and heard him reading Isaiah the prophet, and said, Understandest thou what thou readest?
ACT 8:32 31 And he said, How can I, except some one shall guide me? And he besought Philip to come up and sit with him.
ACT 8:33 32 Now the passage of the scripture which he was reading was this,
ACT 8:34 He was led as a sheep to the slaughter;
ACT 8:35 And as a lamb before his shearer is dumb,
ACT 8:36 So he openeth not his mouth:
ACT 8:37 33 In his humiliation his judgment was taken away:
ACT 8:38 His generation who shall declare?
ACT 8:39 For his life is taken from the earth.
ACT 8:40 34 And the eunuch answered Philip, and said, I pray thee, of whom speaketh the prophet this? of himself, or of some other?
ACT 8:41 35 And Philip opened his mouth, and beginning from this scripture, preached unto him Jesus.
ACT 8:42 36 And as they went on the way, they came unto a certain water; and the eunuch saith, Behold, here is water; what doth hinder me to be baptized?
ACT 8:43 37
ACT 8:44 38 And he commanded the chariot to stand still: and they both went down into the water, both Philip and the eunuch; and he baptized him.
ACT 8:45 39 And when they came up out of the water, the Spirit of the Lord caught away Philip; and the eunuch saw him no more, for he went on his way rejoicing.
ACT 8:46 40 But Philip was found at Azotus: and passing through he preached the gospel to all the cities, till he came to Cæsarea.
ACT 9:1 1 But Saul, yet breathing threatening and slaughter against the disciples of the Lord, went unto the high priest,
ACT 9:2 2 and asked of him letters to Damascus unto the synagogues, that if he found any that were of the Way, whether men or women, he might bring them bound to Jerusalem.
ACT 9:3 3 And as he journeyed, it came to pass that he drew nigh unto Damascus: and suddenly there shone round about him a light out of heaven:
ACT 9:4 4 and he fell upon the earth, and heard a voice saying unto him, Saul, Saul, why persecutest thou me?
ACT 9:5 5 And he said, Who art thou, Lord? And he said , I am Jesus whom thou persecutest:
ACT 9:6 6 but rise, and enter into the city, and it shall be told thee what thou must do.
ACT 9:7 7 And the men that journeyed with him stood speechless, hearing the voice, but beholding no man.
ACT 9:8 8 And Saul arose from the earth; and when his eyes were opened, he saw nothing; and they led him by the hand, and brought him into Damascus.
ACT 9:9 9 And he was three days without sight, and did neither eat nor drink.
ACT 9:10 10 Now there was a certain disciple at Damascus, named Ananias; and the Lord said unto him in a vision, Ananias. And he said, Behold, I am here , Lord.
ACT 9:11 11 And the Lord said unto him, Arise, and go to the street which is called Straight, and inquire in the house of Judas for one named Saul, a man of Tarsus: for behold, he prayeth;
ACT 9:12 12 and he hath seen a man named Ananias coming in, and laying his hands on him, that he might receive his sight.
ACT 9:13 13 But Ananias answered, Lord, I have heard from many of this man, how much evil he did to thy saints at Jerusalem:
ACT 9:14 14 and here he hath authority from the chief priests to bind all that call upon thy name.
ACT 9:15 15 But the Lord said unto him, Go thy way: for he is a chosen vessel unto me, to bear my name before the Gentiles and kings, and the children of Israel:
ACT 9:16 16 for I will show him how many things he must suffer for my name’s sake.
ACT 9:17 17 And Ananias departed, and entered into the house; and laying his hands on him said, Brother Saul, the Lord, even Jesus, who appeared unto thee in the way which thou camest, hath sent me, that thou mayest receive thy sight, and be filled with the Holy Spirit.
ACT 9:18 18 And straightway there fell from his eyes as it were scales, and he received his sight; and he arose and was baptized;
ACT 9:19 19 and he took food and was strengthened.
ACT 9:20 And he was certain days with the disciples that were at Damascus.
ACT 9:21 20 And straightway in the synagogues he proclaimed Jesus, that he is the Son of God.
ACT 9:22 21 And all that heard him were amazed, and said, Is not this he that in Jerusalem made havoc of them that called on this name? and he had come hither for this intent, that he might bring them bound before the chief priests.
ACT 9:23 22 But Saul increased the more in strength, and confounded the Jews that dwelt at Damascus, proving that this is the Christ.
ACT 9:24 23 And when many days were fulfilled, the Jews took counsel together to kill him:
ACT 9:25 24 but their plot became known to Saul. And they watched the gates also day and night that they might kill him:
ACT 9:26 25 but his disciples took him by night, and let him down through the wall, lowering him in a basket.
ACT 9:27 26 And when he was come to Jerusalem, he assayed to join himself to the disciples: and they were all afraid of him, not believing that he was a disciple.
ACT 9:28 27 But Barnabas took him, and brought him to the apostles, and declared unto them how he had seen the Lord in the way, and that he had spoken to him, and how at Damascus he had preached boldly in the name of Jesus.
ACT 9:29 28 And he was with them going in and going out at Jerusalem,
ACT 9:30 29 preaching boldly in the name of the Lord: and he spake and disputed against the Grecian Jews; but they were seeking to kill him.
ACT 9:31 30 And when the brethren knew it, they brought him down to Cæsarea, and sent him forth to Tarsus.
ACT 9:32 31 So the church throughout all Judæa and Galilee and Samaria had peace, being edified; and, walking in the fear of the Lord and in the comfort of the Holy Spirit, was multiplied.
ACT 9:33 32 And it came to pass, as Peter went throughout all parts, he came down also to the saints that dwelt at Lydda.
ACT 9:34 33 And there he found a certain man named Aeneas, who had kept his bed eight years; for he was palsied.
ACT 9:35 34 And Peter said unto him, Aeneas, Jesus Christ healeth thee: arise, and make thy bed. And straightway he arose.
ACT 9:36 35 And all that dwelt at Lydda and in Sharon saw him, and they turned to the Lord.
ACT 9:37 36 Now there was at Joppa a certain disciple named Tabitha, which by interpretation is called Dorcas: this woman was full of good works and almsdeeds which she did.
ACT 9:38 37 And it came to pass in those days, that she fell sick, and died: and when they had washed her, they laid her in an upper chamber.
ACT 9:39 38 And as Lydda was nigh unto Joppa, the disciples, hearing that Peter was there, sent two men unto him, entreating him, Delay not to come on unto us.
ACT 9:40 39 And Peter arose and went with them. And when he was come, they brought him into the upper chamber: and all the widows stood by him weeping, and showing the coats and garments which Dorcas made, while she was with them.
ACT 9:41 40 But Peter put them all forth, and kneeled down, and prayed; and turning to the body, he said, Tabitha, arise. And she opened her eyes; and when she saw Peter, she sat up.
ACT 9:42 41 And he gave her his hand, and raised her up; and calling the saints and widows, he presented her alive.
ACT 9:43 42 And it became known throughout all Joppa: and many believed on the Lord.
ACT 9:44 43 And it came to pass, that he abode many days in Joppa with one Simon a tanner.
ACT 10:1 1 Now there was a certain man in Cæsarea, Cornelius by name, a centurion of the band called the Italian band ,
ACT 10:2 2 a devout man, and one that feared God with all his house, who gave much alms to the people, and prayed to God always.
ACT 10:3 3 He saw in a vision openly, as it were about the ninth hour of the day, an angel of God coming in unto him, and saying to him, Cornelius.
ACT 10:4 4 And he, fastening his eyes upon him, and being affrighted, said, What is it, Lord? And he said unto him, Thy prayers and thine alms are gone up for a memorial before God.
ACT 10:5 5 And now send men to Joppa, and fetch one Simon, who is surnamed Peter:
ACT 10:6 6 he lodgeth with one Simon a tanner, whose house is by the sea side.
ACT 10:7 7 And when the angel that spake unto him was departed, he called two of his household-servants, and a devout soldier of them that waited on him continually;
ACT 10:8 8 and having rehearsed all things unto them, he sent them to Joppa.
ACT 10:9 9 Now on the morrow, as they were on their journey, and drew nigh unto the city, Peter went up upon the housetop to pray, about the sixth hour:
ACT 10:10 10 and he became hungry, and desired to eat: but while they made ready, he fell into a trance;
ACT 10:11 11 and he beholdeth the heaven opened, and a certain vessel descending, as it were a great sheet, let down by four corners upon the earth:
ACT 10:12 12 wherein were all manner of fourfooted beasts and creeping things of the earth and birds of the heaven.
ACT 10:13 13 And there came a voice to him, Rise, Peter; kill and eat.
ACT 10:14 14 But Peter said, Not so, Lord; for I have never eaten anything that is common and unclean.
ACT 10:15 15 And a voice came unto him again the second time, What God hath cleansed, make not thou common.
ACT 10:16 16 And this was done thrice: and straightway the vessel was received up into heaven.
ACT 10:17 17 Now while Peter was much perplexed in himself what the vision which he had seen might mean, behold, the men that were sent by Cornelius, having made inquiry for Simon’s house, stood before the gate,
ACT 10:18 18 and called and asked whether Simon, who was surnamed Peter, were lodging there.
ACT 10:19 19 And while Peter thought on the vision, the Spirit said unto him, Behold, three men seek thee.
ACT 10:20 20 But arise, and get thee down, and go with them, nothing doubting: for I have sent them.
ACT 10:21 21 And Peter went down to the men, and said, Behold, I am he whom ye seek: what is the cause wherefore ye are come?
ACT 10:22 22 And they said, Cornelius a centurion, a righteous man and one that feareth God, and well reported of by all the nation of the Jews, was warned of God by a holy angel to send for thee into his house, and to hear words from thee.
ACT 10:23 23 So he called them in and lodged them.
ACT 10:24 And on the morrow he arose and went forth with them, and certain of the brethren from Joppa accompanied him.
ACT 10:25 24 And on the morrow they entered into Cæsarea. And Cornelius was waiting for them, having called together his kinsmen and his near friends.
ACT 10:26 25 And when it came to pass that Peter entered, Cornelius met him, and fell down at his feet, and worshipped him.
ACT 10:27 26 But Peter raised him up, saying, Stand up; I myself also am a man.
ACT 10:28 27 And as he talked with him, he went in, and findeth many come together:
ACT 10:29 28 and he said unto them, Ye yourselves know how it is an unlawful thing for a man that is a Jew to join himself or come unto one of another nation; and yet unto me hath God showed that I should not call any man common or unclean:
ACT 10:30 29 wherefore also I came without gainsaying, when I was sent for. I ask therefore with what intent ye sent for me.
ACT 10:31 30 And Cornelius said, Four days ago, until this hour, I was keeping the ninth hour of prayer in my house; and behold, a man stood before me in bright apparel,
ACT 10:32 31 and saith, Cornelius, thy prayer is heard, and thine alms are had in remembrance in the sight of God.
ACT 10:33 32 Send therefore to Joppa, and call unto thee Simon, who is surnamed Peter; he lodgeth in the house of Simon a tanner, by the sea side.
ACT 10:34 33 Forthwith therefore I sent to thee; and thou hast well done that thou art come. Now therefore we are all here present in the sight of God, to hear all things that have been commanded thee of the Lord.
ACT 10:35 34 And Peter opened his mouth, and said,
ACT 10:36 Of a truth I perceive that God is no respecter of persons:
ACT 10:37 35 but in every nation he that feareth him, and worketh righteousness, is acceptable to him.
ACT 10:38 36 The word which he sent unto the children of Israel, preaching good tidings of peace by Jesus Christ (he is Lord of all)—
ACT 10:39 37 that saying ye yourselves know, which was published throughout all Judæa, beginning from Galilee, after the baptism which John preached;
ACT 10:40 38 even Jesus of Nazareth, how God anointed him with the Holy Spirit and with power: who went about doing good, and healing all that were oppressed of the devil; for God was with him.
ACT 10:41 39 And we are witnesses of all things which he did both in the country of the Jews, and in Jerusalem; whom also they slew, hanging him on a tree.
ACT 10:42 40 Him God raised up the third day, and gave him to be made manifest,
ACT 10:43 41 not to all the people, but unto witnesses that were chosen before of God, even to us, who ate and drank with him after he rose from the dead.
ACT 10:44 42 And he charged us to preach unto the people, and to testify that this is he who is ordained of God to be the Judge of the living and the dead.
ACT 10:45 43 To him bear all the prophets witness, that through his name every one that believeth on him shall receive remission of sins.
ACT 10:46 44 While Peter yet spake these words, the Holy Spirit fell on all them that heard the word.
ACT 10:47 45 And they of the circumcision that believed were amazed, as many as came with Peter, because that on the Gentiles also was poured out the gift of the Holy Spirit.
ACT 10:48 46 For they heard them speak with tongues, and magnify God. Then answered Peter,
ACT 10:49 47 Can any man forbid the water, that these should not be baptized, who have received the Holy Spirit as well as we?
ACT 10:50 48 And he commanded them to be baptized in the name of Jesus Christ. Then prayed they him to tarry certain days.
ACT 11:1 1 Now the apostles and the brethren that were in Judæa heard that the Gentiles also had received the word of God.
ACT 11:2 2 And when Peter was come up to Jerusalem, they that were of the circumcision contended with him,
ACT 11:3 3 saying, Thou wentest in to men uncircumcised, and didst eat with them.
ACT 11:4 4 But Peter began, and expounded the matter unto them in order, saying,
ACT 11:5 5 I was in the city of Joppa praying: and in a trance I saw a vision, a certain vessel descending, as it were a great sheet let down from heaven by four corners; and it came even unto me:
ACT 11:6 6 upon which when I had fastened mine eyes, I considered, and saw the fourfooted beasts of the earth and wild beasts and creeping things and birds of the heaven.
ACT 11:7 7 And I heard also a voice saying unto me, Rise, Peter; kill and eat.
ACT 11:8 8 But I said, Not so, Lord: for nothing common or unclean hath ever entered into my mouth.
ACT 11:9 9 But a voice answered the second time out of heaven, What God hath cleansed, make not thou common.
ACT 11:10 10 And this was done thrice: and all were drawn up again into heaven.
ACT 11:11 11 And behold, forthwith three men stood before the house in which we were, having been sent from Cæsarea unto me.
ACT 11:12 12 And the Spirit bade me go with them, making no distinction. And these six brethren also accompanied me; and we entered into the man’s house:
ACT 11:13 13 and he told us how he had seen the angel standing in his house, and saying, Send to Joppa, and fetch Simon, whose surname is Peter;
ACT 11:14 14 who shall speak unto thee words, whereby thou shalt be saved, thou and all thy house.
ACT 11:15 15 And as I began to speak, the Holy Spirit fell on them, even as on us at the beginning.
ACT 11:16 16 And I remembered the word of the Lord, how he said, John indeed baptized with water; but ye shall be baptized in the Holy Spirit.
ACT 11:17 17 If then God gave unto them the like gift as he did also unto us, when we believed on the Lord Jesus Christ, who was I, that I could withstand God?
ACT 11:18 18 And when they heard these things, they held their peace, and glorified God, saying, Then to the Gentiles also hath God granted repentance unto life.
ACT 11:19 19 They therefore that were scattered abroad upon the tribulation that arose about Stephen travelled as far as Phoenicia, and Cyprus, and Antioch, speaking the word to none save only to Jews.
ACT 11:20 20 But there were some of them, men of Cyprus and Cyrene, who, when they were come to Antioch, spake unto the Greeks also, preaching the Lord Jesus.
ACT 11:21 21 And the hand of the Lord was with them: and a great number that believed turned unto the Lord.
ACT 11:22 22 And the report concerning them came to the ears of the church which was in Jerusalem: and they sent forth Barnabas as far as Antioch:
ACT 11:23 23 who, when he was come, and had seen the grace of God, was glad; and he exhorted them all, that with purpose of heart they would cleave unto the Lord:
ACT 11:24 24 for he was a good man, and full of the Holy Spirit and of faith: and much people was added unto the Lord.
ACT 11:25 25 And he went forth to Tarsus to seek for Saul;
ACT 11:26 26 and when he had found him, he brought him unto Antioch. And it came to pass, that even for a whole year they were gathered together with the church, and taught much people; and that the disciples were called Christians first in Antioch.
ACT 11:27 27 Now in these days there came down prophets from Jerusalem unto Antioch.
ACT 11:28 28 And there stood up one of them named Agabus, and signified by the Spirit that there should be a great famine over all the world: which came to pass in the days of Claudius.
ACT 11:29 29 And the disciples, every man according to his ability, determined to send relief unto the brethren that dwelt in Judæa:
ACT 11:30 30 which also they did, sending it to the elders by the hand of Barnabas and Saul.
ACT 12:1 1 Now about that time Herod the king put forth his hands to afflict certain of the church.
ACT 12:2 2 And he killed James the brother of John with the sword.
ACT 12:3 3 And when he saw that it pleased the Jews, he proceeded to seize Peter also. And those were the days of unleavened bread.
ACT 12:4 4 And when he had taken him, he put him in prison, and delivered him to four quaternions of soldiers to guard him; intending after the Passover to bring him forth to the people.
ACT 12:5 5 Peter therefore was kept in the prison: but prayer was made earnestly of the church unto God for him.
ACT 12:6 6 And when Herod was about to bring him forth, the same night Peter was sleeping between two soldiers, bound with two chains: and guards before the door kept the prison.
ACT 12:7 7 And behold, an angel of the Lord stood by him, and a light shined in the cell: and he smote Peter on the side, and awoke him, saying, Rise up quickly. And his chains fell off from his hands.
ACT 12:8 8 And the angel said unto him, Gird thyself, and bind on thy sandals. And he did so. And he saith unto him, Cast thy garment about thee, and follow me.
ACT 12:9 9 And he went out, and followed; and he knew not that it was true which was done by the angel, but thought he saw a vision.
ACT 12:10 10 And when they were past the first and the second guard, they came unto the iron gate that leadeth into the city; which opened to them of its own accord: and they went out, and passed on through one street; and straightway the angel departed from him.
ACT 12:11 11 And when Peter was come to himself, he said, Now I know of a truth, that the Lord hath sent forth his angel and delivered me out of the hand of Herod, and from all the expectation of the people of the Jews.
ACT 12:12 12 And when he had considered the thing , he came to the house of Mary the mother of John whose surname was Mark; where many were gathered together and were praying.
ACT 12:13 13 And when he knocked at the door of the gate, a maid came to answer, named Rhoda.
ACT 12:14 14 And when she knew Peter’s voice, she opened not the gate for joy, but ran in, and told that Peter stood before the gate.
ACT 12:15 15 And they said unto her, Thou art mad. But she confidently affirmed that it was even so. And they said, It is his angel.
ACT 12:16 16 But Peter continued knocking: and when they had opened, they saw him, and were amazed.
ACT 12:17 17 But he, beckoning unto them with the hand to hold their peace, declared unto them how the Lord had brought him forth out of the prison. And he said, Tell these things unto James, and to the brethren. And he departed, and went to another place.
ACT 12:18 18 Now as soon as it was day, there was no small stir among the soldiers, what was become of Peter.
ACT 12:19 19 And when Herod had sought for him, and found him not, he examined the guards, and commanded that they should be put to death. And he went down from Judæa to Cæsarea, and tarried there.
ACT 12:20 20 Now he was highly displeased with them of Tyre and Sidon: and they came with one accord to him, and, having made Blastus the king’s chamberlain their friend, they asked for peace, because their country was fed from the king’s country.
ACT 12:21 21 And upon a set day Herod arrayed himself in royal apparel, and sat on the throne, and made an oration unto them.
ACT 12:22 22 And the people shouted, saying , The voice of a god, and not of a man.
ACT 12:23 23 And immediately an angel of the Lord smote him, because he gave not God the glory: and he was eaten of worms, and gave up the ghost.
ACT 12:24 24 But the word of God grew and multiplied.
ACT 12:25 25 And Barnabas and Saul returned from Jerusalem, when they had fulfilled their ministration, taking with them John whose surname was Mark.
ACT 13:1 1 Now there were at Antioch, in the church that was there , prophets and teachers, Barnabas, and Symeon that was called Niger, and Lucius of Cyrene, and Manaen the foster-brother of Herod the tetrarch, and Saul.
ACT 13:2 2 And as they ministered to the Lord, and fasted, the Holy Spirit said, Separate me Barnabas and Saul for the work whereunto I have called them.
ACT 13:3 3 Then, when they had fasted and prayed and laid their hands on them, they sent them away.
ACT 13:4 4 So they, being sent forth by the Holy Spirit, went down to Seleucia; and from thence they sailed to Cyprus.
ACT 13:5 5 And when they were at Salamis, they proclaimed the word of God in the synagogues of the Jews: and they had also John as their attendant.
ACT 13:6 6 And when they had gone through the whole island unto Paphos, they found a certain sorcerer, a false prophet, a Jew, whose name was Bar-Jesus;
ACT 13:7 7 who was with the proconsul, Sergius Paulus, a man of understanding. The same called unto him Barnabas and Saul, and sought to hear the word of God.
ACT 13:8 8 But Elymas the sorcerer (for so is his name by interpretation) withstood them, seeking to turn aside the proconsul from the faith.
ACT 13:9 9 But Saul, who is also called Paul, filled with the Holy Spirit, fastened his eyes on him,
ACT 13:10 10 and said, O full of all guile and all villany, thou son of the devil, thou enemy of all righteousness, wilt thou not cease to pervert the right ways of the Lord?
ACT 13:11 11 And now, behold, the hand of the Lord is upon thee, and thou shalt be blind, not seeing the sun for a season. And immediately there fell on him a mist and a darkness; and he went about seeking some to lead him by the hand.
ACT 13:12 12 Then the proconsul, when he saw what was done, believed, being astonished at the teaching of the Lord.
ACT 13:13 13 Now Paul and his company set sail from Paphos, and came to Perga in Pamphylia: and John departed from them and returned to Jerusalem.
ACT 13:14 14 But they, passing through from Perga, came to Antioch of Pisidia; and they went into the synagogue on the sabbath day, and sat down.
ACT 13:15 15 And after the reading of the law and the prophets the rulers of the synagogue sent unto them, saying, Brethren, if ye have any word of exhortation for the people, say on.
ACT 13:16 16 And Paul stood up, and beckoning with the hand said,
ACT 13:17 Men of Israel, and ye that fear God, hearken:
ACT 13:18 17 The God of this people Israel chose our fathers, and exalted the people when they sojourned in the land of Egypt, and with a high arm led he them forth out of it.
ACT 13:19 18 And for about the time of forty years as a nursing-father bare he them in the wilderness.
ACT 13:20 19 And when he had destroyed seven nations in the land of Canaan, he gave them their land for an inheritance, for about four hundred and fifty years:
ACT 13:21 20 and after these things he gave them judges until Samuel the prophet.
ACT 13:22 21 And afterward they asked for a king: and God gave unto them Saul the son of Kish, a man of the tribe of Benjamin, for the space of forty years.
ACT 13:23 22 And when he had removed him, he raised up David to be their king; to whom also he bare witness and said, I have found David the son of Jesse, a man after my heart, who shall do all my will.
ACT 13:24 23 Of this man’s seed hath God according to promise brought unto Israel a Saviour, Jesus;
ACT 13:25 24 when John had first preached before his coming the baptism of repentance to all the people of Israel.
ACT 13:26 25 And as John was fulfilling his course, he said, What suppose ye that I am? I am not he . But behold, there cometh one after me the shoes of whose feet I am not worthy to unloose.
ACT 13:27 26 Brethren, children of the stock of Abraham, and those among you that fear God, to us is the word of this salvation sent forth.
ACT 13:28 27 For they that dwell in Jerusalem, and their rulers, because they knew him not, nor the voices of the prophets which are read every sabbath, fulfilled them by condemning him .
ACT 13:29 28 And though they found no cause of death in him , yet asked they of Pilate that he should be slain.
ACT 13:30 29 And when they had fulfilled all things that were written of him, they took him down from the tree, and laid him in a tomb.
ACT 13:31 30 But God raised him from the dead:
ACT 13:32 31 and he was seen for many days of them that came up with him from Galilee to Jerusalem, who are now his witnesses unto the people.
ACT 13:33 32 And we bring you good tidings of the promise made unto the fathers,
ACT 13:34 33 that God hath fulfilled the same unto our children, in that he raised up Jesus; as also it is written in the second psalm, Thou art my Son, this day have I begotten thee.
ACT 13:35 34 And as concerning that he raised him up from the dead, now no more to return to corruption, he hath spoken on this wise, I will give you the holy and sure blessings of David.
ACT 13:36 35 Because he saith also in another psalm , Thou wilt not give thy Holy One to see corruption.
ACT 13:37 36 For David, after he had in his own generation served the counsel of God, fell asleep, and was laid unto his fathers, and saw corruption:
ACT 13:38 37 but he whom God raised up saw no corruption.
ACT 13:39 38 Be it known unto you therefore, brethren, that through this man is proclaimed unto you remission of sins:
ACT 13:40 39 and by him every one that believeth is justified from all things, from which ye could not be justified by the law of Moses.
ACT 13:41 40 Beware therefore, lest that come upon you which is spoken in the prophets:
ACT 13:42 41 Behold, ye despisers, and wonder, and perish;
ACT 13:43 For I work a work in your days,
ACT 13:44 A work which ye shall in no wise believe, if one declare it unto you.
ACT 13:45 42 And as they went out, they besought that these words might be spoken to them the next sabbath.
ACT 13:46 43 Now when the synagogue broke up, many of the Jews and of the devout proselytes followed Paul and Barnabas; who, speaking to them, urged them to continue in the grace of God.
ACT 13:47 44 And the next sabbath almost the whole city was gathered together to hear the word of God.
ACT 13:48 45 But when the Jews saw the multitudes, they were filled with jealousy, and contradicted the things which were spoken by Paul, and blasphemed.
ACT 13:49 46 And Paul and Barnabas spake out boldly, and said, It was necessary that the word of God should first be spoken to you. Seeing ye thrust it from you, and judge yourselves unworthy of eternal life, lo, we turn to the Gentiles.
ACT 13:50 47 For so hath the Lord commanded us, saying ,
ACT 13:51 I have set thee for a light of the Gentiles,
ACT 13:52 That thou shouldest be for salvation unto the uttermost part of the earth.
ACT 13:53 48 And as the Gentiles heard this, they were glad, and glorified the word of God: and as many as were ordained to eternal life believed.
ACT 13:54 49 And the word of the Lord was spread abroad throughout all the region.
ACT 13:55 50 But the Jews urged on the devout women of honorable estate, and the chief men of the city, and stirred up a persecution against Paul and Barnabas, and cast them out of their borders.
ACT 13:56 51 But they shook off the dust of their feet against them, and came unto Iconium.
ACT 13:57 52 And the disciples were filled with joy and with the Holy Spirit.
ACT 14:1 1 And it came to pass in Iconium that they entered together into the synagogue of the Jews, and so spake that a great multitude both of Jews and of Greeks believed.
ACT 14:2 2 But the Jews that were disobedient stirred up the souls of the Gentiles, and made them evil affected against the brethren.
ACT 14:3 3 Long time therefore they tarried there speaking boldly in the Lord, who bare witness unto the word of his grace, granting signs and wonders to be done by their hands.
ACT 14:4 4 But the multitude of the city was divided; and part held with the Jews, and part with the apostles.
ACT 14:5 5 And when there was made an onset both of the Gentiles and of the Jews with their rulers, to treat them shamefully and to stone them,
ACT 14:6 6 they became aware of it, and fled unto the cities of Lycaonia, Lystra and Derbe, and the region round about:
ACT 14:7 7 and there they preached the gospel.
ACT 14:8 8 And at Lystra there sat a certain man, impotent in his feet, a cripple from his mother’s womb, who never had walked.
ACT 14:9 9 The same heard Paul speaking: who, fastening his eyes upon him, and seeing that he had faith to be made whole,
ACT 14:10 10 said with a loud voice, Stand upright on thy feet. And he leaped up and walked.
ACT 14:11 11 And when the multitude saw what Paul had done, they lifted up their voice, saying in the speech of Lycaonia, The gods are come down to us in the likeness of men.
ACT 14:12 12 And they called Barnabas, Jupiter; and Paul, Mercury, because he was the chief speaker.
ACT 14:13 13 And the priest of Jupiter whose temple was before the city, brought oxen and garlands unto the gates, and would have done sacrifice with the multitudes.
ACT 14:14 14 But when the apostles, Barnabas and Paul, heard of it, they rent their garments, and sprang forth among the multitude, crying out
ACT 14:15 15 and saying, Sirs, why do ye these things? We also are men of like passions with you, and bring you good tidings, that ye should turn from these vain things unto a living God, who made the heaven and the earth and the sea, and all that in them is:
ACT 14:16 16 who in the generations gone by suffered all the nations to walk in their own ways.
ACT 14:17 17 And yet he left not himself without witness, in that he did good and gave you from heaven rains and fruitful seasons, filling your hearts with food and gladness.
ACT 14:18 18 And with these sayings scarce restrained they the multitudes from doing sacrifice unto them.
ACT 14:19 19 But there came Jews thither from Antioch and Iconium: and having persuaded the multitudes, they stoned Paul, and dragged him out of the city, supposing that he was dead.
ACT 14:20 20 But as the disciples stood round about him, he rose up, and entered into the city: and on the morrow he went forth with Barnabas to Derbe.
ACT 14:21 21 And when they had preached the gospel to that city, and had made many disciples, they returned to Lystra, and to Iconium, and to Antioch,
ACT 14:22 22 confirming the souls of the disciples, exhorting them to continue in the faith, and that through many tribulations we must enter into the kingdom of God.
ACT 14:23 23 And when they had appointed for them elders in every church, and had prayed with fasting, they commended them to the Lord, on whom they had believed.
ACT 14:24 24 And they passed through Pisidia, and came to Pamphylia.
ACT 14:25 25 And when they had spoken the word in Perga, they went down to Attalia;
ACT 14:26 26 and thence they sailed to Antioch, from whence they had been committed to the grace of God for the work which they had fulfilled.
ACT 14:27 27 And when they were come, and had gathered the church together, they rehearsed all things that God had done with them, and that he had opened a door of faith unto the Gentiles.
ACT 14:28 28 And they tarried no little time with the disciples.
ACT 15:1 1 And certain men came down from Judæa and taught the brethren, saying , Except ye be circumcised after the custom of Moses, ye cannot be saved.
ACT 15:2 2 And when Paul and Barnabas had no small dissension and questioning with them, the brethren appointed that Paul and Barnabas, and certain other of them, should go up to Jerusalem unto the apostles and elders about this question.
ACT 15:3 3 They therefore, being brought on their way by the church, passed through both Phoenicia and Samaria, declaring the conversion of the Gentiles: and they caused great joy unto all the brethren.
ACT 15:4 4 And when they were come to Jerusalem, they were received of the church and the apostles and the elders, and they rehearsed all things that God had done with them.
ACT 15:5 5 But there rose up certain of the sect of the Pharisees who believed, saying, It is needful to circumcise them, and to charge them to keep the law of Moses.
ACT 15:6 6 And the apostles and the elders were gathered together to consider of this matter.
ACT 15:7 7 And when there had been much questioning, Peter rose up, and said unto them,
ACT 15:8 Brethren, ye know that a good while ago God made choice among you, that by my mouth the Gentiles should hear the word of the gospel, and believe.
ACT 15:9 8 And God, who knoweth the heart, bare them witness, giving them the Holy Spirit, even as he did unto us;
ACT 15:10 9 and he made no distinction between us and them, cleansing their hearts by faith.
ACT 15:11 10 Now therefore why make ye trial of God, that ye should put a yoke upon the neck of the disciples which neither our fathers nor we were able to bear?
ACT 15:12 11 But we believe that we shall be saved through the grace of the Lord Jesus, in like manner as they.
ACT 15:13 12 And all the multitude kept silence; and they hearkened unto Barnabas and Paul rehearsing what signs and wonders God had wrought among the Gentiles through them.
ACT 15:14 13 And after they had held their peace, James answered, saying,
ACT 15:15 Brethren, hearken unto me:
ACT 15:16 14 Symeon hath rehearsed how first God visited the Gentiles, to take out of them a people for his name.
ACT 15:17 15 And to this agree the words of the prophets; as it is written,
ACT 15:18 16 After these things I will return,
ACT 15:19 And I will build again the tabernacle of David, which is fallen;
ACT 15:20 And I will build again the ruins thereof,
ACT 15:21 And I will set it up:
ACT 15:22 17 That the residue of men may seek after the Lord,
ACT 15:23 And all the Gentiles, upon whom my name is called,
ACT 15:24 18 Saith the Lord, who maketh these things known from of old.
ACT 15:25 19 Wherefore my judgment is, that we trouble not them that from among the Gentiles turn to God;
ACT 15:26 20 but that we write unto them, that they abstain from the pollutions of idols, and from fornication, and from what is strangled, and from blood.
ACT 15:27 21 For Moses from generations of old hath in every city them that preach him, being read in the synagogues every sabbath.
ACT 15:28 22 Then it seemed good to the apostles and the elders, with the whole church, to choose men out of their company, and send them to Antioch with Paul and Barnabas; namely , Judas called Barsabbas, and Silas, chief men among the brethren:
ACT 15:29 23 and they wrote thus by them, The apostles and the elders, brethren, unto the brethren who are of the Gentiles in Antioch and Syria and Cilicia, greeting:
ACT 15:30 24 Forasmuch as we have heard that certain who went out from us have troubled you with words, subverting your souls; to whom we gave no commandment;
ACT 15:31 25 it seemed good unto us, having come to one accord, to choose out men and send them unto you with our beloved Barnabas and Paul,
ACT 15:32 26 men that have hazarded their lives for the name of our Lord Jesus Christ.
ACT 15:33 27 We have sent therefore Judas and Silas, who themselves also shall tell you the same things by word of mouth.
ACT 15:34 28 For it seemed good to the Holy Spirit, and to us, to lay upon you no greater burden than these necessary things:
ACT 15:35 29 that ye abstain from things sacrificed to idols, and from blood, and from things strangled, and from fornication; from which if ye keep yourselves, it shall be well with you. Fare ye well.
ACT 15:36 30 So they, when they were dismissed, came down to Antioch; and having gathered the multitude together, they delivered the epistle.
ACT 15:37 31 And when they had read it, they rejoiced for the consolation.
ACT 15:38 32 And Judas and Silas, being themselves also prophets, exhorted the brethren with many words, and confirmed them.
ACT 15:39 33 And after they had spent some time there , they were dismissed in peace from the brethren unto those that had sent them forth.
ACT 15:40 34
ACT 15:41 35 But Paul and Barnabas tarried in Antioch, teaching and preaching the word of the Lord, with many others also.
ACT 15:42 36 And after some days Paul said unto Barnabas, Let us return now and visit the brethren in every city wherein we proclaimed the word of the Lord, and see how they fare.
ACT 15:43 37 And Barnabas was minded to take with them John also, who was called Mark.
ACT 15:44 38 But Paul thought not good to take with them him who withdrew from them from Pamphylia, and went not with them to the work.
ACT 15:45 39 And there arose a sharp contention, so that they parted asunder one from the other, and Barnabas took Mark with him, and sailed away unto Cyprus:
ACT 15:46 40 but Paul chose Silas, and went forth, being commended by the brethren to the grace of the Lord.
ACT 15:47 41 And he went through Syria and Cilicia, confirming the churches.
ACT 16:1 1 And he came also to Derbe and to Lystra: and behold, a certain disciple was there, named Timothy, the son of a Jewess that believed; but his father was a Greek.
ACT 16:2 2 The same was well reported of by the brethren that were at Lystra and Iconium.
ACT 16:3 3 Him would Paul have to go forth with him; and he took and circumcised him because of the Jews that were in those parts: for they all knew that his father was a Greek.
ACT 16:4 4 And as they went on their way through the cities, they delivered them the decrees to keep which had been ordained of the apostles and elders that were at Jerusalem.
ACT 16:5 5 So the churches were strengthened in the faith, and increased in number daily.
ACT 16:6 6 And they went through the region of Phrygia and Galatia, having been forbidden of the Holy Spirit to speak the word in Asia;
ACT 16:7 7 and when they were come over against Mysia, they assayed to go into Bithynia; and the Spirit of Jesus suffered them not;
ACT 16:8 8 and passing by Mysia, they came down to Troas.
ACT 16:9 9 And a vision appeared to Paul in the night: There was a man of Macedonia standing, beseeching him, and saying, Come over into Macedonia, and help us.
ACT 16:10 10 And when he had seen the vision, straightway we sought to go forth into Macedonia, concluding that God had called us to preach the gospel unto them.
ACT 16:11 11 Setting sail therefore from Troas, we made a straight course to Samothrace, and the day following to Neapolis;
ACT 16:12 12 and from thence to Philippi, which is a city of Macedonia, the first of the district, a Roman colony: and we were in this city tarrying certain days.
ACT 16:13 13 And on the sabbath day we went forth without the gate by a river side, where we supposed there was a place of prayer; and we sat down, and spake unto the women that were come together.
ACT 16:14 14 And a certain woman named Lydia, a seller of purple, of the city of Thyatira, one that worshipped God, heard us: whose heart the Lord opened to give heed unto the things which were spoken by Paul.
ACT 16:15 15 And when she was baptized, and her household, she besought us, saying, If ye have judged me to be faithful to the Lord, come into my house, and abide there . And she constrained us.
ACT 16:16 16 And it came to pass, as we were going to the place of prayer, that a certain maid having a spirit of divination met us, who brought her masters much gain by soothsaying.
ACT 16:17 17 The same following after Paul and us cried out, saying, These men are servants of the Most High God, who proclaim unto you the way of salvation.
ACT 16:18 18 And this she did for many days. But Paul, being sore troubled, turned and said to the spirit, I charge thee in the name of Jesus Christ to come out of her. And it came out that very hour.
ACT 16:19 19 But when her masters saw that the hope of their gain was gone, they laid hold on Paul and Silas, and dragged them into the marketplace before the rulers,
ACT 16:20 20 and when they had brought them unto the magistrates, they said, These men, being Jews, do exceedingly trouble our city,
ACT 16:21 21 and set forth customs which it is not lawful for us to receive, or to observe, being Romans.
ACT 16:22 22 And the multitude rose up together against them: and the magistrates rent their garments off them, and commanded to beat them with rods.
ACT 16:23 23 And when they had laid many stripes upon them, they cast them into prison, charging the jailor to keep them safely:
ACT 16:24 24 who, having received such a charge, cast them into the inner prison, and made their feet fast in the stocks.
ACT 16:25 25 But about midnight Paul and Silas were praying and singing hymns unto God, and the prisoners were listening to them;
ACT 16:26 26 and suddenly there was a great earthquake, so that the foundations of the prison-house were shaken: and immediately all the doors were opened; and every one’s bands were loosed.
